### 364 subject events

- **1912.10** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-door` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  门外有人上楼，Klara忽然指着门说：“爸爸。”Josef Bauer看了看空门口，没有纠正。那天晚上，孩子把家里每个人的名字都叫了一遍，叫错两个，却叫得很认真。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +first_word_door_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josef Bauer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; health 0.009999999999999898 (a good day)_

- **1914.01** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Klara踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Katharina Bauer把Klara抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Klara举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Klara问是什么，Josef Bauer只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Josef在楼道里滚一只铁环，Klara站在门口看。Josef把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood 0.05_

- **1917.05** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环在石板缝上颠了一下，Klara用钩子一拨，抢先绕过煤车。Mitzi Lechner跟在后面，铁环倒在地上转了两圈。三颗玻璃弹珠从Mitzi Lechner的口袋里摸出来，有一颗还带着体温。Mitzi Lechner把弹珠按进Klara手心，转身踢了一脚墙根。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.05; skill_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Otto Moser朝Klara招手。Klara挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Klara把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Klara拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Anna Bauer弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Klara接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Klara前面的大婶一直往后靠，Klara就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Klara的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Klara空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Josef Bauer来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Klara用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Klara把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Josef Bauer。
  _effects: mood -0.04; food; skill_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Klara后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Klara没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Klara用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1922.02** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  土豆从围裙里滚出来，Klara蹲下去追，手心一松，三枚十克鲁泽的铜板叮叮当当掉在石板路上。两枚捡回来了，最后一枚滚进排水格栅，在铁条下转了个圈。Klara趴下去够，指甲刮在铁条上。回家Josef Bauer数完钱，脸色沉下来，Klara站在桌边不敢动。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.05999999999999999_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-neighbor-speaks` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “记我名下。”Alois Lechner说完，掌柜才把秤砣挪开。账本上多出两赫勒的利息，煤筐却只装了半下。Klara跟着Alois Lechner走出门，Alois Lechner低声嘱咐明天先把钱送来，别让掌柜再找到话头。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1923.04** `vienna-adolescence.stolen-roll/caught-and-returned` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  面包房后门的筐里堆着隔夜面包。Klara拿起半个黑面包，刚要往怀里塞，Berta Weiss从晾衣绳后面绕出来，手一伸，掌心朝上。Klara把面包放回去，Berta Weiss用指节在Klara手背上敲了两下，声音不大，巷口的人都听见了。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; mood -0.1_

- **1923.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Anna Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Klara把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Anna Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.03_

  Klara把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Klara在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Klara把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。

  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Klara穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Emil从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Klara走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:164; health 0.019999999999999997 (cold river water and a long swim); relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  亲戚把他带到工头面前，工头让他下星期来试半天，先看裁纸和搬版。母亲熨平的衬衫领子还是硬得硌脖子，他站在厂门口把姓名说了两遍。
  _effects: flag +half_day_trial_at_big_printshop; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; mood 0.05_

- **1926.05** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/straight-to-factory` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Stein把Klara的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Klara。Klara进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

- **1926.05** `r2-leisure.scullery-punishment::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::scullery-punishment-reform/reported-to-inspector` tags: domestic_service, abuse, punishment, burn, servant, reform, medical, schooling
  检查员来查佣工合同，Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien让Klara把手藏在围裙下面。Klara没有藏，摊开掌心，那块新疤还没有褪成白色。检查员看了一眼，又看了一眼Ferdinand Winter，把名字写进本子。Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien说这是烫衣服不小心，谁家没有这种事。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; flag +reported_iron_burn_to_inspector; role; knowledge “the inspector recorded the burn and the household address”; relation colleague with person-ferdinand-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1926.08** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-humiliation` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.1; flag +wish:power:fixated_on_revenge_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003691_

- **1926.09** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Klara推刨子。Klara推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Klara，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josef Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1927.01** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuse-the-machine` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  教堂年历的订单催得紧，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Klara看着那块铁板，说手会进去；Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Klara是不是不想出师了。Klara没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Grete Winter 2接手。
  _effects: relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 5; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1927.01** `r13-civ.vaccination::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.vaccination-reform/reform-class-vaccinated` tags: health, vaccination, school, parent, public_health, reform, medical, schooling
  Helene Bauer让全班按座位排队，Grete Lechner在讲台边摆开酒精瓶和针盒。轮到Klara时，Helene Bauer替她把袖子卷好，说别怕，去年他自己也种过。下课后，黑板上还留着一行没擦掉的日期。
  _effects: health 0.05 (种痘室门口的袖子); condition; flag +vaccinated_in_school_campaign; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1927.06** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pass-it-on` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Stein把一本拉丁文课本推到Klara面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Klara翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Klara把纸抄了一遍，塞进Emil的历史作业本；那天晚上，Klara第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

  Klara把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Klara每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:238; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  _effects: mood -0.1; flag +lost_apprenticeship_to_sibling; role; skill; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000167:child_

  Klara在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Anton。这一次对方先点了头，Klara准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Klara才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Klara以为走就走了，工钱总会结。等到月底，家里人把空钱袋翻过来，Klara才回到作坊。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne正在给新学徒示范上墨，头也没抬，说人没打招呼就走，只能给几个铜币。Klara站在门口，听见里面的机器又转起来，铜币在掌心硌得生疼。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (没拿到最后一个月的整月工钱); mood -0.07999999999999999; unemployment (不告而别离开作坊); relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  Helene Bauer把Klara和Elisabeth Bauer一起叫到讲台前，问墨水瓶底下的公式是谁写的。Klara说不知道；Elisabeth Bauer低着头，袖口还沾着同样的蓝墨水。Helene Bauer让两人各写一份检讨，Klara写到一半，听见Elisabeth Bauer在旁边小声说“对不起”，笔尖把纸戳破了。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +yf18_exam_suspicion; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  Helene Bauer在班上念了Klara的作文，念到“她的手套放在木座上”时停了一下，说这就是考试里最危险的东西：把私事当成论据。全班笑了，Klara盯着桌面上的墨水圈，听见Rosa在最后一排合上了书。

  考试周里，Klara把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Klara用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。

  Klara只把Anton的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Klara是不是要请人喝咖啡；Klara说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 22 -> 34; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 3 -> 0; reputation -0.03; mood -0.02999999999999997_

  你数出四十赫勒搁在秤盘上，说剩下的一克朗用那张旧折叠桌抵。老板撇了撇嘴，让伙计跟你上楼搬桌子。桌子腿有点晃，但木料还结实。邻居在楼道里搭了把手，账本上这一页只写了一行，没写利息。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买煤付的部分现钱（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  风从教堂的拱廊里灌过来，队伍里没人说话，只听见罐子碰罐子的声音。Otto Weiss排在前面，领完没走，站在墙根等Klara，把自己的面包掰了一半递过去。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1929.06** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Gustav Kral 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Klara把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

  印刷机的滚筒咬住Klara的袖口时，谁都以为只擦破了一层皮。伤口结痂后，却留下一只张开翅膀的鸟形烫痕。Helene Berg给了Klara两枚银币，说别去报工伤，厂里正查安全。Klara把钱收下，回到排字房，觉得那只鸟在袖子里发烫。

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation 0.08000000000000002; position 名誉主席 @org-sdap; flag +puppet_office_

  又过了一个冬天，Klara能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Klara画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 25 -> 35; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 25 -> 35; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/kept-small` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  又过了一年，Klara学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Klara的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 35 -> 29; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 20 -> 16; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1932.01** `p9-work-career.night-course/p9-work-career.night-course.dropout` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, night_school, bookkeeping
  Klara头两次课都到了，第三次车间赶一批订单，工头留他加班到九点。他赶到夜校时黑板已经擦干净。此后两个月，加班总落在星期三。六克朗学费不退，课本只翻到第十四页，书脊还是硬的。他把书塞进床底下的木箱。
  _effects: cash -6 (学费不退，等于白付); mood -0.040000000000000036; skill_

- **1932.03** `ad20-rent-renewal::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/a-year-of-quiet` tags: rent, housing, renewal, everyday, obligation, money, reform, medical
  Josefine Hofer看完租约，没有加价，也没有提补充条款。Klara把房租数好，放在桌上；Josefine Hofer数了一遍，又从里面拿出一枚硬币，说“上次多收了”。Klara接过硬币，觉得这一年总算有一件事没有变。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid on time); flag +rent_renewed; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.06** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Klara走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.11** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1932.12** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1932.12** `hs18-queue.figure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pretend-not-to-see` tags: historical_figure, queue, recognition, bread, witness, history, reform, medical
  Klara把头转向橱窗，假装在看一双标价离谱的鞋。Elisabeth Berg从队伍旁走过，靴跟敲得很慢。Sigmund Freud往前挪了一步，帽檐仍压着；两人之间隔着三个人和一整条街的沉默。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:429; knowledge “保持沉默也需要记住每一个细节。”; flag +ignored_historical_figure_in_queue; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; reputation -0.06; state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:428; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  Klara又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 75 -> 77_

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.apologize::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/forgiven` tags: task, social, apology, reputation, conflict, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara在院子的水龙头边等到Rosa，把那天酒桌上的话重复了一遍，没有找补。Rosa拧上水龙头，说那句话已经传到第三个院子了。Klara问要怎样才能收回，Rosa说收不回，但可以当着传话的人再说一遍是胡说的。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; flag +apology_accepted; role; knowledge “the insult has already reached three courtyards”_

- **1933.06** `r3-mig.political-exile::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/checked-in-under-false-name` tags: migration, exile, politics, safe_house, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara在旅馆登记簿上写了一个假名，Elisabeth Hofer 2在柜台后面看了一眼，说“住三楼，别开窗”。半夜有人敲门，Klara从猫眼看见Alois Lechner站在走廊里。Alois Lechner说门口停着一辆没牌照的车，已经停了两个钟头。
  _effects: cash -3 (边境旅馆的假名（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +checked_in_under_false_name; relation friend with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “a car without plates is parked outside the hotel”_

- **1933.08** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-negotiate-installments` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你没去借钱，直接下楼找房东，说月底前先付两克朗，剩下两克朗分两个星期补齐。房东叼着雪茄，拿铅笔在你的纸条背面写了个日期，说再拖一天就换锁。邻居在楼道口听见了，冲你摇了摇头，但没说什么。
  _effects: cash -2 (按分期付掉的第一笔欠租); relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +rent_installment_plan_

- **1933.10** `r13-rel.lodger-after-separation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-lodger-brought-trouble` tags: relationship, separation, housing, lodger, money, vienna, urban, crisis
  Maria Bauer夜里带人回来，走廊里全是烟味。Klara说过两次，Maria Bauer把房租往桌上一拍，说付了钱就有权利。月底，Klara在门口又贴了一张纸条，这次写的是“只租给上夜班的”。
  _effects: cash 2 (空出来的床位); condition; role; household; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000482:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +faction_failed_to_form_

  放贷人把借据推过来，第二行用更小的字写着月息。Klara念到“逾期以家具作抵”时，Otto Hofer把钢笔帽拧上，说这条街上没有第二家肯借钱给一个刚被停用的人。Elisabeth Bauer在门口咳了一声，算是见证。Klara签了名，十八克朗当场点清。

  _effects: reputation 0.06; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; knowledge “我知道{rival}在关键委员会里真正想要什么，也知道这笔交易的价格。”_

- **1934.03** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sibling-medicine-bill/split-per-head` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money

  _effects: cash -5 (分摊母亲药费中自己的一份（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000214:child_

  Anton Berg 2把三本名册都翻完，说没有这个人，也许登记时用了别的写法。Maria Stein 2不肯走，又把名字念了一遍，念到第三遍时声音低下去。Klara扶她到走廊的长椅上坐下，替她把围巾重新系好；窗口后面已经叫了下一个号。
  _effects: cash -1 (念错的名字（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.07000000000000006; condition; flag +missing_relative_no_record; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; knowledge “there is no record under the name the aunt remembers”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1935.03** `kernel-crisis.arrest-questioning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/questioning-released` tags: crisis, police, questioning, repression, vienna, urban, war, industrial
  Klara在宵禁后回家，被街角的巡警叫住。区公所的讯问室里没有窗，桌上只有一盏台灯；问了两个小时，签了名字，按了手印，天亮前被放出来，工票被翻得起了毛边。台灯罩上有一只死苍蝇，没人去碰。
  _effects: move place-wien-magistrat; move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; condition; flag +questioned_by_police; role; cash -2 (tram fare and a replacement work-book cover); knowledge “区公所的讯问室没有窗，签名字按手印后可以天亮前放人；工票会被翻旧。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1936.01** `r14-work.loan-default::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/half-a-year-more` tags: debt, loan, loan_default, law, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  Maria Bauer 2把双方叫到楼下的门房，桌上摊着算到小数点后的账。Grete Lechner同意再宽限半年，条件是Klara每周多还一克朗，并在借据背面写清违约的后果。Alois Hofer端来两杯水，杯底沉着茶叶。Klara签字时，笔尖把“半年”两个字洇开了。
  _effects: cash -6 (到期日那天（其中6克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +loan_rescheduled; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; knowledge “the rescheduled loan costs one extra krone a week”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  市场尽头的Katharina Hofer 2会看手相。Klara把铜币放在桌上，Katharina Hofer 2捏着手腕看了半天，说这不是病，是“有人替你许过愿”，还送了一条红绳。Klara把红绳系在袖口里，走出棚子时，觉得路上的人都在看自己的手。
  _effects: cash -1 (看手相的铜币); state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 27 -> 37; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> blessing; flag +df18_mark_read_as_blessing; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara把Luise Stein的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Luise Stein今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 32 -> 40; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 38 -> 35; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.4 -> 0.35000000000000003_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Katharina 3这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 34 -> 37; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Franz Kral在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Anton Berg 2没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室); reputation 0.06; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:009066; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1936.10** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.notary-inheritance/demand-accounts-first` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  旧账本摊开，纸页上的铅笔字迹深浅不一。Emil念一笔，Klara就在边上记一笔，Helene Huber的钢笔悬在遗嘱上方没有落。Josef Bauer咳了一声，说屋顶那八克朗是自己看着修的。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +demands_estate_accounting_

- **1936.11** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/municipal-mortuary` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  市立太平间缺一个夜班杂役，Helene Huber只问Klara怕不怕冷。地下室的抽屉一排十二个，有的拉开了就不再推回去；Klara的工作是登记遗物、给无名尸挂上编号，再在天亮前把地拖干净。
  _effects: organization_found 市立太平间 (state); position 太平间杂役 @morgue:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:009067; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:589; cash 2 (municipal night wages); condition; knowledge “太平间地下室的抽屉一排十二个，无名尸也要有编号。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  _effects: reputation 0.06_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009067_

  Klara被叫到学校，老师说Rosa Moser这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 34 -> 29; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 47; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/learned-the-building` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  又过了一个冬天，Klara能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Klara画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 30 -> 40; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1937.01** `r13-work.deliver-goods::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/bribed-the-guard` tags: commerce, delivery, transport, weather, bribe, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton Berg 2在路口设了检查，说板车不许走这条街。Klara把三枚硬币压在送货单下面递过去，Anton Berg 2用指头弹了弹，说下次早点来。车推过去以后，Klara回头看了一眼，Anton Berg 2正在把硬币收进手套里，动作很熟。
  _effects: cash -3 (板车上的十二箱（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +work13_bribed_a_route_guard; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; knowledge “the route guard takes a small payment to let carts through”_

- **1937.09** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Klara在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Klara去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为圣马克斯殡葬合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1938.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/grew` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Berta Weiss对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.08000000000000002; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”; flag +faction_grew_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009066_

  有人翻出Klara两年前的一次妥协，说那是立场问题。Klara被移出名单，办公室的钥匙当天收回。Mitzi Moser 2接替了位置，没有看Klara一眼。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.06; state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:608; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

  Helene Huber在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Klara排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1938.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/blocked-the-permit` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Klara把一份扩建许可证压在抽屉最下面，理由是排水和消防通道不合规。申请人Otto Stein在走廊里等到下班，最后只拿到一张写着“补件”的纸条。Klara知道这份许可明年还会再来，但今年不会从自己的桌上出去。
  _effects: reputation 0.019999999999999962; skill; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.borrow-handcart/hire-the-movers` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  站在门口把借钱的话过了两遍，最后没有开口。转身去找了街角的搬运行，一辆板车加两个帮工，一趟一克朗五十赫勒。家里能当的都当了，现钱只够这一趟，搬完当天连买煤的钱都要现算。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (搬运行一趟的工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1938.04** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/take-in-a-lodger` tags: housing, home, health, family
  Klara把厨房角落隔出一块，租给一个在附近上工的学徒。每月多四克朗，但煤、水和厕所都要排队；夜里有人咳嗽，Klara睡不踏实，第二天眼下发青，孩子也在床上翻来覆去，四克朗换来一屋子醒着的人。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); condition_

- **1938.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Helene Huber把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Klara去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1938.11** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Klara转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Helene Huber还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1938.12** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Luise Hofer追出来问怎么不领了，Klara说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Klara把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.7 -> 0.85; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1939.02** `r3-crime.false-accusation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/maid-confesses` tags: crime, court, servant, class, injustice, vienna, urban, crisis
  就在法官要敲槌时，Luise Kral忽然站起来，说糖罐是她打碎的，碎片埋在院子里的李树下。圣马克斯殡葬合作社的脸涨得通红，Katharina Stein 2被叫去后院挖了半个钟头。Klara被当庭释放，门外下着雨，Luise Kral追出来，把一个纸包塞进Klara手里，里面是三个月的工钱。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; flag +cleared_by_maid_confession; relation friend with person-luise-kral; knowledge “the sugar bowl was broken and buried in the yard”_

- **1939.08** `r2-law.building-permit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/permit-issued` tags: law, building_permit, construction, housing, inspection, vienna, urban, crisis
  Alois Hofer 2把图纸摊在窗口，用尺量了后院到邻居墙的距离，说退让不够，要改。Klara说改了就少一间房，Alois Hofer 2说少一间房总比拆了好。Josef Berg在旁边重新画了一条线，把厨房往后挪了半米；Alois Hofer 2收下修改图，盖了三个章，说脚手架搭起来之前还要来验一次。
  _effects: cash -18 (脚手架上的批文（其中18克朗记入债务）); flag +building_permit_granted_after_setback; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; knowledge “the kitchen must be moved half a meter from the boundary”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

- **1939.10** `r3-mig.steamship-ticket::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/resold-ticket` tags: migration, ticket, travel, papers, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Helene Bauer说三等的铺位要等三个月，先买这张“家眷票”可以立刻走，只是到了港口要自己找住处。Klara把票翻过来，看见背面用铅笔写着一个已经划掉的名字。Helene Bauer说那是上一个买主，临时不走了。Klara没有问那个人为什么没走。
  _effects: cash -7 (统舱票上的铅笔字（其中7克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +bought_resold_ticket; knowledge “the ticket was resold from a passenger who did not sail”_

- **1940.01** `initiative:found_process:mortuary:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006212`
  圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。

- **1940.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Otto Bauer在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Otto Bauer敲开Klara的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1940.07** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-fee-lost` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  介绍所的墙上钉着上百块木牌，大半翻过去露出灰白的背面。Helene Huber收了一克朗，从抽屉里摸出纸条，墨水还没干透。第二天Klara站在工厂门口，门房说清扫工昨天就齐了。
  _effects: cash -1 (职业介绍所登记费（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1940.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Otto Bauer在门口替Klara捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: reputation -0.040000000000000036; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:736; flag -detained; flag +released_from_detention_

  Maria Bauer在门口拦住Klara，说这阵子别来了，名单上多一个名字就多一份麻烦。Klara把歌本还回去，走到街角又回头，看见Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的窗户用报纸糊了一半，里面还有人低声唱。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +kept_off_choir_roll_for_safety; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000482:child; knowledge “the choir is being watched”_

  Klara在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Helene 2拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002685:child_

  Klara用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Otto Kral发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.040000000000000036; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +balanced_the_books; knowledge “停掉两份简报、合并零碎支出后，年底还剩一笔够换档案柜的钱。”_

- **1944.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Anton Weiss先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Klara点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003560:child; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子); mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1944.03** `ad20-favor-called-in::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/do-the-favor` tags: favor, obligation, everyday, trust, debt, risk, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Stein说只需要Klara在明天下午去一趟车站，把一个箱子交给穿灰外套的人。Klara问箱子里是什么；Mitzi Stein说“你不需要知道”。Klara还是去了，因为当年Mitzi Stein替Klara垫过一笔医药费。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:914; cash -1 (the trip to the station（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000868:child; flag +favor_done; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “箱子里是什么，邻居没有说。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1944.05** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1944.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1944.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺结核中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1945.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Anton Weiss在楼梯间坐了很久；Klara在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Klara的名字；此后邻居提起Klara，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中3克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1947.10** `r3-war.war-memorial::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/added-unofficial-names` tags: war, memorial, grief, stone, community, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara提议把自杀者和逃兵的名字也刻上去，Alois Hofer 2说“名单是上面定的”。Rosa没有争，只把一束野花放在碑脚；Grete Winter 3低声说，他可以在碑背刻一行小字，不收费。那天夜里，Klara看见碑背多了三个名字，刻得很浅。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +added_unofficial_names_to_memorial; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; knowledge “three names are carved on the back of the memorial”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1947.12** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/give-place-to-mother` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Gustav Bauer抱着孩子连声道谢时，Klara已经弯着腰在咳。药房玻璃瓶上贴着褪色标签，喝下去满嘴甘草味；后半夜Klara听见自己牙齿打颤，炉子早灭了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000524:child_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/waiting-list` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  Klara去住房局问结果，办事员翻过卡片说分数不够：家里人口少，现住房虽挤但还没到拆除标准。回程的电车上，Helene Huber劝他明年再递一次申请。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (application and tram fare（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1948.01** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refused-by-committee` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  编制委员会只用了十分钟就否掉了Berta Bauer的名字：预算冻结，岗位归并，连桌子都要搬去仓库。Klara把那张表折起来，答应明年再试。Berta Bauer说没关系，第二天去了另一家事务所面试。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002371:child; flag +protege_hiring_refused; knowledge “编制委员会今年冻住了所有新增岗位。”_

- **1948.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999994_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016012_

- **1948.01** `r1-civ.suffrage-tea-room::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::r1-civ.suffrage-tea-room-reform/reform-committee-hearing` tags: politics, suffrage, petition, women, meeting, vienna, urban, postwar
  请愿书终于排进市议会的旁听议程，Mitzi Stein 3把座位让给一个洗衣妇，自己站在走廊里。Hedwig念到“寡妇与女工”时，旁听席上有人笑；Grete Stein站起来把签名簿举高，守门人过来让她坐下。Klara坐在最后一排，把每个笑的人的位置记了下来。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; flag +suffrage_petition_heard_in_council; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; relation friend with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the franchise committee will hear the petition but not vote ”_

- **1948.02** `r2-law.residency-permit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/permit-refused` tags: law, residency, papers, police, housing, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Huber说Klara的合同没有在区里备案，居留许可不能续。Klara问备案要多久，Helene Huber说先让房东来，再等通知。Alois Hofer 2来了，听说要补三个月的税，转身就走；Elisabeth Berg在楼梯口说，这条街上被赶走的人，都是先丢了那张备案纸。
  _effects: cash -6 (居留章盖在旧地址上（其中5克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +residency_permit_denied_for_unregistered_lease; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; knowledge “the lease must be registered before the permit can be renewe”_

- **1948.09** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/coffin-coop` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  师傅Grete Winter 3把一块冷毛巾塞进Klara手里，让Klara先擦死者的脸，再擦自己的。殡葬合作社的规矩写在门背后：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块写着日期的木牌。Klara做完第一具，手抖得点不着烟。
  _effects: organization_found 圣马克斯殡葬合作社 (other); position 入殓工 @mortuary:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:016678; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:1087; cash 1 (first mortuary wages); skill; knowledge “殡葬合作社的规矩：先入殓，后结账；无名者也要有一块日期木牌。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1948.09** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Klara听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 25 -> 33; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 25 -> 22; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

  矿山还没出金，Klara就把股份转给了一个想给女儿做嫁妆的男爵，赚了二十五克朗。半年后，假金矿的消息见报，男爵在俱乐部里拦住Klara，问知不知道岩芯的事。Klara说不知道，手却一直放在酒杯后面。
  _effects: cash 25 (profit from selling the mining shares early); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1142; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +sold_mine_shares_early; knowledge “男爵买下股份时，矿山还没有出过一克金。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +sudden_fortune; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Klara把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。
  _effects: reputation -0.040000000000000036; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; skill_

  Elisabeth Stein用拇指甲刮着一件领子的边线，说这儿跳了针。Klara把煤油灯往前推了推，让他凑近看，说线是细了点，活没少做。Friedrich Winter倚在门框上没走，屋里一时只有尺子碰桌面的响。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003178:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.09** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/burn-bird` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, vienna
  印刷机的滚筒咬住Klara的袖口时，谁都以为只擦破了一层皮。伤口结痂后，却留下一只张开翅膀的鸟形烫痕。Katharina Winter 2给了Klara两枚银币，说别去报工伤，厂里正查安全。Klara把钱收下，回到排字房，觉得那只鸟在袖子里发烫。
  _effects: cash 2 (工头给的封口费); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:1206; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-151; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-151; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1950.09** `state_reaction:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-public` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Klara在人群里挽了一下袖子，第二天就有人来问那块印子是不是“会动的”。Klara说不会，问的人却已经把自己的解释说完了。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 35 -> 43; reputation -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018051_

- **1950.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Klara下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1951.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/grew` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Luise Kral对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.015000000000000013; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017332_

- **1951.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/small-surplus` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  账目连续第二年没有超支，Franz Moser在报告里加了一句“未发现异常”。Klara用结余给夜班守卫换了炉子，又把旧账本按年份装进箱子；没有人写报道，但来办事的人发现窗口开得比从前早。
  _effects: organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; flag +quiet_surplus; knowledge “审计报告里只有一句“未发现异常”，但窗口提前开门被办事的人记住了。”_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara被叫到学校，老师说Maria Weiss这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 18 -> 13; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 55; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara被叫到学校，老师说Helene Weiss这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 10; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 65 -> 70; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Klara的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Klara想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 6 -> 0; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 45 -> 35_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:lc17-empty-nest/quiet-year/room-stays` tags: lc17-empty-nest, family, home, empty_nest, aging, household, transition, state
  Klara把朝北那间房的窗帘拉开又合上，床单还是孩子离家时铺的那一条。午饭只煮了一小锅，锅底结了一圈干米；下午有人敲门，Klara先去看了钟，才想起这个点不会有人来。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-empty-nest.quiet 25 -> 30; state_parameter lc17-empty-nest.reorientation 50 -> 48_

- **1951.01** `state_expire:ml18-sudden-fortune` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  Klara的「一笔突然到手的钱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1142; flag -sudden_fortune_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Klara在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Klara低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 43 -> 53; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 20 -> 26; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed unknown -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_expire:raising-child` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara的「家里有个正在长大的孩子」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:326; flag -raising_child_active_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara被叫到学校，老师说Rosa Moser这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 53 -> 48; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 53 -> 58; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

- **1951.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.03** `r2-law.bank-run::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/moved-to-credit-union` tags: finance, bank, panic, crisis, savings, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara没有再排队，拿着存折去了街对面的信用合作社。柜台后的老会计把利率算在纸角上，说转过来只能按八成兑。Alois Berg跟着出来，站在两家门之间，最后把存折撕成两半，一半塞给Klara。
  _effects: cash -6 (铁栅门后的第三排（其中6克朗记入债务）); flag +moved_savings_to_credit_union; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; knowledge “the credit union discounts transferred savings by twenty per”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-158; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1951.04** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/show-the-reel` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, vienna
  Klara没有剪，让那卷新闻片继续走。观众先以为这是加映，直到有人站起来喊“这不是我们买的票”。警察在散场前堵住侧门；夜校同学会说是放映员接错了本，Klara被记了名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (lost wages and paid a fine); condition; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:1251; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +showed_banned_reel; knowledge “那卷新闻片里有一张被按下去的脸，散场前警察就堵住了侧门。”_

- **1951.05** `state_reaction:em18-family-archive/inheritance-claim` tags: em18-family-archive, elderhood, family, archive, memory, inheritance, documents, secret
  律师念到“旧信件与照片归……”时停了一下，因为清单上没有写清楚。Klara回家后把箱子重新翻了一遍，发现少了一张已经泛黄的收据；没有人承认拿过。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.quarrel 25 -> 33; state_parameter em18-family-archive.access 35 -> 30; knowledge “遗产清单上有一张已经泛黄的收据，后来不见了。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018688_

- **1951.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Klara摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Alois Huber第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Klara只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

- **1951.09** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa Moser这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 18; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018872_

- **1952.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/clean-audit` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Helene Huber带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Klara把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Helene Huber抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.040000000000000036; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +passed_audit; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 16 -> 19; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018791_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara把Rosa Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Rosa Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 19 -> 27; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 70 -> 67; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  入冬后，Klara在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Klara低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 53 -> 63; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 26 -> 32; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:em18-family-archive/archive-year/a-box-gets-opened` tags: em18-family-archive, elderhood, family, archive, memory, inheritance, documents, secret
  又一年过去，Klara从柜顶取下一只纸箱，里面的信封按年份捆着，绳子一碰就碎。Klara把出生证、地契和一张没寄出的明信片分开，桌上多出三小堆纸；最小的那一堆，Klara没有让别人看。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.order 15 -> 19; state_parameter em18-family-archive.secrets 55 -> 58; mood 0.010000000000000009; knowledge “家里的纸箱里有一张没有寄出的明信片。”_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/the-logbook-mislaid` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Klara半个月的夜班钱；Klara没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 4; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 40 -> 30; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `p10-migration-tasks.move-district/move-stay-and-bargain` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, moving, district
  Klara最终没有搬，把箱子又拖回楼上，跟旧房东坐在厨房桌边谈了半个钟头。按月付、煤账分两次清，房东要她每周扫一次楼道。孩子不用换学校，母亲的药也还在原来的药房抓，只是房租每月多了八十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -3 (按月先付一部分房租与旧欠煤账（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1952.06** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Gustav Kral 3凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Klara把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1952.08** `p9-history-medical-daily.sanitation-inspection/sign-and-demand-repair` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  签字时Klara把笔握得很紧，在“住户意见”一栏写了“下水道堵塞，垃圾箱无盖”。Katharina Stein 2念了一遍，说会转给房东；Hermann Huber凑过来看，问要不要按手印。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; knowledge “卫生巡查员把下水道堵塞记进了记录，说会通知房东限期修”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1952.08** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Klara端着一只旧锅去敲Elisabeth Berg的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Elisabeth Berg让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Klara连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `initiative:found_process:mortuary:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:016678`
  圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 圣马克斯殡葬合作社的持续运动; historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:016678-1953 as organizer; historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:016678-1953 as organizer; knowledge “圣马克斯殡葬合作社的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”_

- **1953.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。
  _effects: knowledge “我亲历或知晓「圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019964_

- **1953.01** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Friedrich Bauer排在前面，回头替Klara看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Klara用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005664:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Klara嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Klara用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1953.09** `state_reaction:em18-family-archive/death-in-the-family` tags: em18-family-archive, elderhood, family, archive, memory, inheritance, documents, secret
  葬礼结束，亲戚们回到屋里，有人问起地契，有人问起那张全家福。Klara把箱子搬到桌上，却没有立刻开锁；屋里安静得能听见纸在箱子里滑动。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.secrets 66 -> 71; state_parameter em18-family-archive.quarrel 33 -> 41; state_parameter em18-family-archive.order 25 -> 22; flag +archive_opened_after_death_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020234_

- **1953.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara没有立刻答话，先去敲Berta Bauer的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Berta Bauer提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Klara把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002371:child; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1954.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Klara和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Klara把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.020000000000000018; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:1346; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1954.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Klara的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Klara家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020175_

- **1954.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.030000000000000027_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020175_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara被叫到学校，老师说Rosa Weiss这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 8 -> 3; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 55 -> 60; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:lc17-empty-nest/quiet-year/new-use` tags: lc17-empty-nest, family, home, empty_nest, aging, household, transition, state
  Klara把朝北的房腾出来，靠窗摆了一张旧桌，开始替街坊抄信、写申请、算煤账。第一天来了两个人，第二天来了五个；桌上多了一只装零钱的果酱瓶，瓶身上还留着去年的标签。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-empty-nest.quiet 40 -> 34; state_parameter lc17-empty-nest.reorientation 44 -> 52; cash 1 (small fees for letters and accounts); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Klara在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Klara低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 57 -> 67; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 28 -> 34; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:em18-family-archive/archive-year/damp-and-mice` tags: em18-family-archive, elderhood, family, archive, memory, inheritance, documents, secret
  雨季过后，箱底的文件粘在一起，边角被老鼠咬掉。Klara用熨斗隔着布把纸烘干，字迹还是淡了一半；一张能证明工龄的收据，从此只剩下半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.order 22 -> 18; state_parameter em18-family-archive.quarrel 41 -> 43; cash -1 (new paper and a visit to the notary); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.01** `state_expire:oj18-odd-apprenticeship` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  Klara的「奇职学徒」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:1251_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Klara把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Klara来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1954.01** `state_expire:raising-child` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara的「家里有个正在长大的孩子」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:510_

- **1954.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara为圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsame出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:016678-1953 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000013_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Helene Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 5 -> 8; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020688_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

- **1954.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Friedrich Winter在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Klara把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水)_

- **1954.08** `r1-fam.gossip-campaign::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/gossip.unresolved` tags: social, gossip, reputation, market, rumour, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara去找Grete Weiss对质，Grete Weiss说不是她说的，是洗衣房的人说的。两人一路问到洗衣房，洗衣房的人说是市场卖鱼的说的；最后谁也没找到源头。Klara回到家，发现门口被人用粉笔画了一个圈，孩子在圈里跳房子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; reputation -0.12; condition; flag +rumour_without_a_source; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1954.08** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-candle-bites` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, vienna
  仪式做完，Rosa Stein把铜钥匙放进Klara手里，钥匙却烫得像刚从炉子里夹出来。Klara松手时掌心已经起泡；第二天，聘书上的名字褪成一片空白，只有Klara的手还疼。Rosa Stein说这算成了，也算没成。
  _effects: state_add curse-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:1396; flag +curse_recoiled_at_once; cash -1 (烫伤的药膏（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-167_

- **1954.11** `state_reaction:em18-family-archive/inheritance-claim` tags: em18-family-archive, elderhood, family, archive, memory, inheritance, documents, secret
  律师念到“旧信件与照片归……”时停了一下，因为清单上没有写清楚。Klara回家后把箱子重新翻了一遍，发现少了一张已经泛黄的收据；没有人承认拿过。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.quarrel 43 -> 51; state_parameter em18-family-archive.access 30 -> 25; knowledge “遗产清单上有一张已经泛黄的收据，后来不见了。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021063_

  Grete Stein问Klara，Rosa Weiss最近为什么总在课上打瞌睡。Klara说家里晚上在赶一批活；Grete Stein点点头，说“别让孩子熬太晚”。Klara应了一声，心里却在算那批活还能不能按时交。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 6 -> 8; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +teacher_asked_homework_

- **1955.01** `state_reaction:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-public` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Klara在人群里挽了一下袖子，第二天就有人来问那块印子是不是“会动的”。Klara说不会，问的人却已经把自己的解释说完了。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 67 -> 75; mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020930_

  Klara把Maria Weiss的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Maria Weiss今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 11 -> 19; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 60 -> 57; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Klara把朝北那间房的窗帘拉开又合上，床单还是孩子离家时铺的那一条。午饭只煮了一小锅，锅底结了一圈干米；下午有人敲门，Klara先去看了钟，才想起这个点不会有人来。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-empty-nest.quiet 34 -> 39; state_parameter lc17-empty-nest.reorientation 52 -> 50; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  又一年过去，Klara从柜顶取下一只纸箱，里面的信封按年份捆着，绳子一碰就碎。Klara把出生证、地契和一张没寄出的明信片分开，桌上多出三小堆纸；最小的那一堆，Klara没有让别人看。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-family-archive.order 18 -> 22; state_parameter em18-family-archive.secrets 71 -> 74; mood 0.010000000000000009; knowledge “家里的纸箱里有一张没有寄出的明信片。”_

  _effects: state_parameter curse-bargain.doubt 25 -> 19; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Grete Kral对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.07999999999999996; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”_

- **1955.06** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把通知念给Grete Bauer听，Grete Bauer摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Klara去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1955.10** `fa17-studio.private-failure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/borrow-a-paragraph` tags: art, writing, failure, poverty, privacy, craft, plagiarism, vienna
  Klara从一九〇四年的画报上抄下一段描写雨夜的话，把地名和人名换掉，寄给一家征文比赛。抄的时候，Klara告诉自己只借这一小段；寄出去之后，却在邮局门口站了很久，反复回想那段话里有没有一个词是别人一眼就能认出来的。
  _effects: state_add fa17-plagiarist state:world-vienna-1912:1436; skill; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +borrowed_paragraph_

- **1956.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/back-to-work-too-soon` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，她把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Klara又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

- **1956.01** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Klara用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Rosa Huber指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1956.04** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Luise Bauer在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005945:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Anna Weiss把一张清单摊在钢琴上，说这间屋按“敌产”登记，家具要逐件编号。Klara说钢琴是母亲的，Anna Weiss说“那就写母亲的名字，东西还是不能动”。他在琴盖上贴了一张封条，封条压住了琴键。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; condition; flag +apartment_listed_as_enemy_property; knowledge “the piano is sealed under the enemy property inventory”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

### 285 subject events

- **1912.03** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-door` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  门外有人上楼，Mitzi忽然指着门说：“爸爸。”Rosa Lechner看了看空门口，没有纠正。那天晚上，孩子把家里每个人的名字都叫了一遍，叫错两个，却叫得很认真。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.02; flag +first_word_door_

- **1913.06** `vienna-infant-household.croup/doctor-call` tags: infancy, health, family, home
  Alois Lechner打发邻居去请街角的医生。医生提着皮包来，听了听胸口，留下半瓶药水，收走一克朗。Mitzi三天后不再咳得蜷成一团。
  _effects: cash -1 (医生出诊费和药水钱); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Alois Lechner说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Mitzi问是什么，Alois Lechner只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1915.06** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1915.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi从痢疾中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/stolen-marble` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Josef Lechner把Mitzi的玻璃弹珠藏进鞋里，被当场看见。Josef Lechner说只是“替你拿着”，Mitzi没有揭穿，也没有再拿出第二颗。两个人蹲在墙根，把一只蚂蚁看了很久，谁也没有先回家。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000098:child; skill; mood -0.01_

- **1916.04** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1916.04** `kernel-child.first-school-day::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-school-back-bench` tags: childhood, school, enrolment, teacher, fees, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Maria Moser让新生挨个伸手，看指甲，又让念一遍主祷文。Mitzi念到一半卡住，Maria Moser用尺子敲了敲桌沿，说回家再练。座位被排到最后一排，靠门，冬天风从缝里灌进来。Mitzi把石板立在膝上，尽量不让手抖。
  _effects: education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; health -0.010000000000000009 (cold draught from the door); condition; flag +back_bench; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1916.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`

- **1916.07** `p9-childhood-household.supper-bowl/caught-adding-ladle` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Mitzi的勺子刚碰到锅底，Alois Lechner就转过身来。两个人都没说话。Alois Lechner把自己的碗往桌子中间推了推，又拿起面包继续撕。Mitzi盯着碗里剩下的汤，一口一口喝完，没敢再抬头。
  _effects: mood -0.04; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1916.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1916.09** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Mitzi一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Alois Lechner听见哭声跑出来，把Mitzi抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Mitzi哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.04000000000000001 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1916.10** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Katharina Bauer家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Mitzi递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Katharina Bauer端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Mitzi把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1918.09** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  没有钱请医生，Alois Lechner把醋瓶拎到床边，拿两只碗轮换着兑水。Mitzi烧得嘴唇起皮，Alois Lechner用小勺喂了两口温水，又把冷布敷上去。鸡叫时热度退了一点，Mitzi睁开眼，看见Alois Lechner趴在床沿，手指还攥着湿布。
  _effects: health 0.03 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.02; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/recites` tags: childhood, school, hunger
  Grete Stein让Mitzi站到黑板前背乘法表。孩子背到七乘八时停了一下，还是背完了；放学时手指冻得发红，石板上的字被袖子擦掉一半，Grete Stein在名册上给了一个小小的勾，Mitzi把石板抱在胸前，一路没舍得放进书包。
  _effects: education primary_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-early-morning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  “老师，我能留下来吗？”Mitzi问完就盯着自己的鞋尖。Grete Stein想了一会儿，说早上来，人少。天没亮，Mitzi已经站在校门口，手指冻得伸不直。教室里只有炉火和粉笔灰的味道，Grete Stein把一道竖式拆成三行，让Mitzi跟着念一遍。
  _effects: skill; health -0.02 (天没亮出门，早春的冷); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

  _effects: condition; flag +school_closed_for_scarlet_fever; skill; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

  “就一口。”Mitzi把糖块掰开，换了一张火车头。Otto Moser在报亭窗口看着，等糖纸落地，才走出来。画片被扣在柜台上，扫帚递到Mitzi手里。Mitzi扫完半条后巷，Otto Moser用围裙擦了擦画片，还回来时角上多了一道折痕。
  _effects: mood -0.029999999999999995; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1926.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Mitzi就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Mitzi还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Mitzi在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1926.02** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/pass-it-on` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Stein把一本拉丁文课本推到Mitzi面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Mitzi翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Mitzi把纸抄了一遍，塞进Hedwig的历史作业本；那天晚上，Mitzi第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1926.04** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/kept-home-for-kin` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, university, culture, schooling, student
  Alois Lechner病倒在床，Mitzi留在家里看弟妹、煮稀粥、去井边打水。Grete Stein托人带来一包旧课本，Mitzi在灶火边翻了两页，弟弟哭了，只好合上。一个月后，学校把Mitzi的名字从名册上划掉，墨迹很淡。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +left_school_for_family; role_

- **1926.08** `p9-childhood-adolescence.courtyard-gang/gang-prater-locked-out` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.12** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1926.12** `yf18-firstlook.atlas-margin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/read-it-to-the-class` tags: first_love, school, atlas, note, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi以为那是谁开的玩笑，把地图册举起来念给全班听。Helene Bauer问是谁写的；教室最后一排有人把书立起来挡住脸。放学后，Rosa在楼梯口等Mitzi，只说了一句“你念错了一个字”，就从他身边走过去。

  Mitzi把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Mitzi在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Mitzi把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

- **1927.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`

- **1927.05** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  _effects: flag +found_dormitory_thief; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the watch was hidden in the neighboring bed”_

  Mitzi和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Mitzi在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。

- **1930.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/back-to-work-too-soon` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，她把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Mitzi又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1930.02** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Mitzi转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Josefine Hofer还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1930.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Gustav Kral 2排在前面，回头替Mitzi看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Mitzi用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1930.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Berta Lechner在门口替Mitzi捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1930.07** `r1-fam.marriage-contract::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::contract-reform/contract.new-law` tags: family, marriage, contract, law, money, reform, medical, schooling
  办事处的墙上贴着新婚姻法的告示，Berta Stein逐条念给两人听：婚后财产各半，离婚要双方到场。Alois Lechner在长椅上打瞌睡，Gustav Moser听到“各半”时看了Mitzi一眼。Mitzi说就按新的办；Berta Stein把印章盖得很正。
  _effects: cash -2 (婚约的第七条); flag +married_under_new_law; role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; knowledge “新婚姻法规定婚后财产各半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1931.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Grete Lechner抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Mitzi把床单对折，腾出一半，Grete Lechner用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005163_

- **1931.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/learned-to-read` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, university, culture, schooling, student
  Mitzi下班后不回家，直接去夜校。教室由一间酒馆后屋改成，桌上还留着酒渍。Grete Stein从字母表教起，Mitzi把每个字母念三遍，喉咙发干。三个月后，Mitzi第一次独立读完一张报纸上的短讯，把那张报纸叠好，放进工具箱最底层。学费一克朗，Mitzi用加班钱付的。
  _effects: cash -1 (夜校的第二排长凳); skill_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003695_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Mitzi最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Mitzi把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Mitzi说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 9 -> 15; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 52; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.01** `r13-civ.join-charity::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/accepted-as-visitor` tags: social, charity, membership, poverty, respectability, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Kral把登记簿摊在门房的小桌上，让Mitzi写下姓名和住址。Mitzi写完后，Luise Kral在名字旁边画了一个小十字，说下周去给孤老送煤。本子上前面几行，墨迹已经褪成了褐色。
  _effects: cash -1 (慈善会登记簿上的空行); reputation 0.04; flag +joined_charity_visiting_round; role; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1931.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Helene Kral在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Helene Kral敲开Mitzi的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1931.07** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/apply-for-a-municipal-flat` tags: housing, home, health, family
  Mitzi去市政办事处登记申请更大的市营住宅。办事员收下表格和两克朗手续费，说前面还有几百户，至少要等两年；Mitzi把回执折好，回家路上看见新盖的市营楼房，窗户亮着，门却还进不去。
  _effects: cash -2 (municipal housing application); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1932.01** `r14-edu.night-class::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/too-tired-to-stay` tags: education, adult_learning, literacy, night_school, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Mitzi在夜校撑了六个星期。白天的活儿从早上六点排到晚上七点，坐在长凳上就犯困，Helene Bauer叫名字时，Mitzi猛地站起来，把墨水瓶碰翻了。第二天，Mitzi没有再出现，课本留在桌肚里，夹着一张没写完的请假条。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +night_school_abandoned; skill_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004203_

- **1932.01** `r14-edu.self-taught-examination::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/restricted-book` tags: education, adult_learning, self_taught, library, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Mitzi在图书馆借了一本被列入禁书目录的小册子，登记时用了假名。第二天，Helene Huber把Mitzi叫到柜台后面，翻开借阅卡，指着那个名字。Mitzi说只是好奇，Helene Huber把书收回柜里，说下不为例，声音压得很低。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; flag +library_restricted_book; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004989_

  亲戚的婚礼上，Mitzi被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Mitzi低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 25; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi听说下奥地利的砖厂还招人，收拾了一只箱子，坐早班火车离开维也纳。厂里的工头在背后说，走了就别回来。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings)_

  Mitzi暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `r13-edu.accounting-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/bookkeeping-certificate` tags: education, accounting, bookkeeping, work, fraud, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi把一整年的进货、赊账和折旧做成三栏账，Helene Bauer看完只挑出一处小数点点错。结业考试那天，Mitzi用算盘打了七遍，余额终于对上。证书发下来，Mitzi把它夹在账本第一页，每次翻账都先看见自己的名字。纸角压得很平。
  _effects: flag +bookkeeping_certificate; skill_

- **1932.01** `ad20-public-mistake::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/everyone-saw` tags: public, mistake, shame, everyday, reputation, comic, reform, medical
  Mitzi在街口绊了一下，手里的纸包散开，里面的纽扣滚了一地。有人笑，有人弯腰帮忙，一个孩子把纽扣含进嘴里又吐出来。Mitzi蹲在地上捡，越捡越觉得所有人都在看自己的后脑勺。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:432; reputation -0.039999999999999994; flag +public_mistake; knowledge “纽扣滚了一地，整条街都看见了。”; flag +public_attention_active_

- **1932.10** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turned-back` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, reform
  Elisabeth Hofer 2把材料推回来，说出生纸缺了教区印章。Mitzi解释自己是从乡下赶来的，对方只指了指墙上的告示；排在后头的Josef Winter递来半块面包，两个人坐在候车室里等下一班回去的车，谁也没有先说话。
  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000214:child; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1932.10** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/burned-frame` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, reform
  胶片在片门里起火，火苗顺着观察窗舔进大厅。Mitzi没有先去拿灭火沙，而是把后排的孩子往侧门推；Luise Kral 2用湿毯子捂住片盒，手背烧掉一层皮。第二天，小巷电影院把烧黑的片盒留在柜台上，说“留着，提醒我们”。
  _effects: condition; reputation 0.05; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:431; cash 1 (emergency pay for the fire shift); flag +cinema_fire; knowledge “硝酸片起火时，观察窗像一只被点燃的眼睛。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1933.01** `r14-edu.self-taught-examination::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/library-assistant` tags: education, adult_learning, self_taught, library, university, culture, schooling, student
  Mitzi差四分，没有被录取。图书馆馆长看过Mitzi的笔记，问愿不愿意来做编目助手，工钱不多，但可以随便看书。Mitzi第二天就去上工，把书架从A排到Z，手指被纸边割了好几道口子。每月工钱三克朗。
  _effects: cash 3 (借书证背面的铅笔号); flag +library_post; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005466_

- **1933.01** `ld18-raid.private-club::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/names-in-a-notebook` tags: queer, raid, police, public, scandal, nightlife, secret_intimacy, reform
  警察在午夜后闯进地下舞厅，把灯全部打开。Helene Huber让所有人靠墙站好，逐个记下名字和住址；Mitzi看见Alois Lechner的鞋跟断了一只。第二天，名单上的一些名字出现在报纸的“风化事件”栏里。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:430; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 31 -> 51; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 49 -> 34; reputation -0.09999999999999999; cash -2 (a fine and a broken shoe); flag +name_in_police_notebook; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; flag +public_desire_active_

  办公室有人问Mitzi，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Mitzi说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Mitzi把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 51 -> 59; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 34 -> 30; reputation -0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006798_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/someone-asks-for-the-papers` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  房东要续租，必须出示结婚证或亲属关系证明。Mitzi说伴侣是表妹，房东却问为什么表妹的姓和籍贯都对不上。那天晚上，两个人把箱子重新打包，又把已经叠好的衣服一件件拿出来；谁也没有说下个月住哪里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 59 -> 71; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 30 -> 22; reputation -0.03_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  两年过去，Mitzi已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Mitzi也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 40 -> 50; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 20; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/warms` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  Mitzi在街角被人认出来，对方没有要求什么，只说“上次那件事做得不错”。这句客气话被旁边的人听见，下午便有两个陌生人朝Mitzi点头。关注没有减少，但空气松了一点。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 65 -> 61; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 30 -> 36; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 15 -> 19; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `kernel-culture.radio-broadcast::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/civic-radio-read` tags: culture, radio, broadcast, writing, reform, university, schooling, student
  播音室比壁橱大不了多少，Franz Moser让她离话筒一臂远。她读的是市集价格和一段天气预报，读到一半，耳机里传来自己的声音，比平时慢半拍；Otto Berg 2在玻璃后面竖起大拇指。播完后，Franz Moser让她把稿子留下，说明天还要重播。
  _effects: role; cash 3 (广播稿费); reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; knowledge “播音要离话筒一臂远，耳机里自己的声音会慢半拍”; flag +radio_broadcast; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1933.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Mitzi攥紧扶手。Otto Stein站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛)_

- **1933.05** `ad20-market-day::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/the-scale-is-light` tags: market, food, money, neighbor, everyday, credit, vienna, urban
  市场日的人挤到棚布底下。Maria Stein把Mitzi的篮子放上秤，秤砣往下一沉，又悄悄用拇指抵住秤杆。Mitzi看见了，没有说；Maria Stein多塞了两根葱，说“今天的葱不值钱”。Otto Kral从过道那头走来，两个人同时把篮子往身后挪了挪。
  _effects: cash 1 (a market stall gave short weight and then overpaid in change); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; flag +market_short_weight; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “市场那台秤的秤砣被摊主用拇指抵过。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

  Mitzi和Maria Berg在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Maria Berg把煤钱推过来，Mitzi接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_
