- **1934.01** `ld18-journalist.interview::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-story-is-refused` tags: press, interview, public_desire, scandal, money, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Mitzi拒绝了采访。一个星期后，报纸登了一篇没有名字的短文，写“某剧院两位女演员”的夜生活。Alois Berg在文末写“本报不评论私德”；Mitzi把报纸折好，垫在鸟笼底下。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 50 -> 70; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 0 -> 0; reputation -0.04999999999999999; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; flag +story_printed_without_consent; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  办公室有人问Mitzi，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Mitzi说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Mitzi把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 81 -> 89; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 22 -> 18; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

  房东要续租，必须出示结婚证或亲属关系证明。Mitzi说伴侣是表妹，房东却问为什么表妹的姓和籍贯都对不上。那天晚上，两个人把箱子重新打包，又把已经叠好的衣服一件件拿出来；谁也没有说下个月住哪里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 89 -> 100; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 18 -> 10; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Mitzi，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Mitzi没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 70 -> 78; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 40 -> 44; reputation -0.02999999999999997_

- **1934.01** `r13-edu.medical-study::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university::reform-social-medicine/tenement-fieldwork` tags: education, medicine, university, anatomy, health, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi在新设的市立医院实习，Helene Bauer让Mitzi跟着去工人住宅区出诊。Mitzi第一次看见一家五口挤在一间屋里，孩子的咳嗽和墙上的霉斑连在一起。回校后，Mitzi把见闻写成报告，附上了通风改造的草图。那张图后来被贴在医院的走廊里。
  _effects: flag +social_medicine_fieldwork; knowledge “the tenement damp is linked to the children coughs”; education higher_

- **1934.02** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/neighbor-help-night` tags: health, family, home

  _effects: health 0.08 (邻居帮助后烧退); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1934.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  Mitzi说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Ferdinand Moser 2没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Mitzi听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; flag +funeral_society_left; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001469:child_

- **1938.12** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Mitzi的裙角。Hedwig的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Mitzi咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `hs18-witness.reporter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/refuse-and-warn` tags: witness, reporter, memory, money, risk, history, vienna, urban
  Mitzi把钞票推回去，说那个人还住在这条街上。Anna Winter 4收起本子，问是不是有人威胁。Mitzi说没有，只是不想在下一个门洞里再认出谁。出门时，Mitzi绕到Helene Kral家，把记者来过的事说了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 35 -> 45; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.retellings 0 -> 1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; reputation -0.010000000000000009; flag +refused_witness_payment; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:known-affair/public-year/retold` tags: known-affair, love, scandal, secret, public, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Mitzi在市场上被人叫住，对方笑着说“就是那个人”。Mitzi也笑了一下，买完土豆就走；回到家才发现，手里的零钱少了一枚。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 15 -> 23; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Mitzi忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Mitzi听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 80 -> 72; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Mitzi又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Alois Lechner今年记得住Mitzi的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 38 -> 34; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 16 -> 14; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1939.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Mitzi替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Mitzi自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.040000000000000036; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1939.05** `df18-blessing.laundry-water::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-candle-stall` tags: blessing, omen, water, faith, fraud, consolation, religious, eerie
  Mitzi Winter在门口摆了一只木箱，卖蜡烛和印着水渍的小卡片，说摸一摸就能好。Mitzi看着邻居把零钱收进围裙，没有阻止。几天后，楼道里排起了队；Mitzi从后门进出，怕被人认成那个“显灵”的女人。
  _effects: cash 2 (邻居分给subject的蜡烛钱); reputation 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “邻居把我的水渍变成了卖蜡烛的摊子。”; flag +df18_water_stain_shrine; condition_

- **1939.11** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Franz Hofer在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1939.12** `ad20-rent-renewal::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/sign-the-side-letter` tags: rent, housing, renewal, everyday, obligation, money, vienna, urban
  Alois Lechner把正式租约放在桌上，又从口袋里摸出一张对折的纸，说这是“补充条款”。Mitzi看见上面写着若提前搬走，押金不退。Alois Lechner说大家都这么签；Mitzi看了看窗外的院子，还是把名字写在最后一行。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:718; cash -1 (the renewal fee); flag +side_letter_signed; knowledge “补充条款写着提前搬走不退押金。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1940.01** `hs18-occupation.lineup::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-someone-else` tags: occupation, lineup, witness, denunciation, police, history, vienna, urban
  Mitzi指向中间那个很少出门的会计，说他那晚从墙边走过。Elisabeth Berg没有核实，只让人把会计带走。Friedrich Winter低下头，肩膀松了一点；Mitzi回家后才发现，自己的手一直在抖。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 45 -> 70; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 72 -> 62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; reputation -0.030000000000000027; flag +falsely_identified_man; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:hs18-street-witness/interrogation-pressure` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  又有人来问那天的事。Mitzi把同一段话重说了一遍，对方却只记住其中两个名字；走的时候，门没有关严，楼道里的风把纸吹得直响。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 70 -> 80_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010956_

- **1940.01** `state_transition:hs18-street-witness/too-dangerous` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street

  _effects: state_transition hs18-street-witness -> political-suspect; flag +witness_became_political_suspect_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Alois Lechner今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Mitzi在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 34 -> 42; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 14 -> 20; cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  对方在一个不合时宜的时刻提起旧事，语气很客气，意思却不客气。Mitzi听见自己答应得太快，挂断以后才发现手心有一层薄汗。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 40 -> 48; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 50 -> 54; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 45 -> 41_

  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +wish:knowledge:pursuing_

- **1940.01** `r13-civ.public-health-campaign::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/well-found-contaminated` tags: health, public_health, campaign, poster, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi在告示背面记下几家井的位置，又去问了挑水的人。Gustav Berg 3看了记录，说东街那口井离粪坑太近，要马上封。市立太平间当天下午派人来撒石灰，Mitzi的名字被写进了报告末尾。
  _effects: reputation 0.09999999999999998; flag +traced_typhoid_to_well; knowledge “the east street well stands too close to a cesspit”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-109_

- **1940.02** `r2-uni.library-reserve::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/petitioned-for-copies` tags: education, library, scarcity, students, reading, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi写了一份请愿书，要求增购四本教科书，签名的有八十个人。Alois Hofer把请愿书送到校务会，回来说“预算里没有这一项”。Mitzi又写了一份，这次附上了每个人排队的小时数。第二学期，书架上多了两本。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +petitioned_for_second_copy; role; knowledge “the library bought two extra copies after a petition”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

  选票折成四折投进银盘，Mitzi以两票之差当上会堂管事。Maria Stein把钥匙交过来时，说账上只剩十一克朗，下个月的煤还欠着。Grete Lechner在门口与人握手，握得比平时久。
  _effects: cash -2 (长椅上的选票); flag +elected_synagogue_gabbai; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; organization_membership; knowledge “the synagogue treasury holds eleven kronen”_

  十二赫勒换来两杯淡啤酒，找零是四枚一赫勒的铜币。Mitzi把铜币收好，听师兄讲那年冬天的事，讲到一半师兄停了停，说“你别到处讲”。酒馆里有人拉手风琴，声音盖过了后半句。
  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; skill_

  _effects: state_parameter political-suspect.heat 0.4 -> 0.55; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.talk-tram` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Mitzi把报纸推到一边，和Otto Berg聊起电车票价要涨的传闻。两人拿过糖罐当算盘，在桌布上比划通勤开销，两杯咖啡四十赫勒，争论得忘了时间，直到窗外的煤气灯亮起来。Mitzi起身时发现大衣下摆沾了咖啡渍，晚饭的点早过了，却约好下回还坐这张桌子。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (两杯咖啡的价钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001469:child; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1941.07** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Franz Hofer从围裙里掏出几张揉皱的收据，Mitzi把它们按日期排开。屋外有人上楼，脚步在门口停了一下又走了。Mitzi把新表折成四折塞进衣袋，说先管这条街，别的明天再说。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Mitzi把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Mitzi走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1941.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Alois Berg把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Mitzi去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水)_

- **1948.07** `ad20-newspaper-interview::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/printed-as-a-person` tags: media, interview, newspaper, everyday, public, reputation, vienna, urban
  Alois Hofer 2把笔记本合上，问Mitzi能不能再讲一件小事，不要那些大词。Mitzi说去年冬天在车站帮一个陌生人看过箱子；Alois Hofer 2记下箱子的大小和颜色。第二天报纸出来，Mitzi的名字在第三版，旁边是一张没有拍好的侧脸。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:1090; reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; flag +interview_printed_kindly; mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “报纸第三版登了采访，旁边是一张没有拍好的侧脸。”; flag +public_attention_active_

- **1948.12** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/leave-the-table` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  Mitzi把牌一推，说今晚不借也不玩。Maria Huber 2耸耸肩，把桌上的钱收回口袋。Mitzi走到街上，口袋里的硬币只够买一杯咖啡；但经过当铺时，Mitzi没有停。第二天上工，Mitzi的手指还在发痒，像少做了一件本该做的事。
  _effects: cash -1 (一杯咖啡); flag +df18_refused_card_money; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “我拒绝了牌桌上的钱，但手指还记得那种感觉。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1949.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Alois Hofer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Mitzi把床单对折，腾出一半，Alois Hofer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016021_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/warms` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  Mitzi在街角被人认出来，对方没有要求什么，只说“上次那件事做得不错”。这句客气话被旁边的人听见，下午便有两个陌生人朝Mitzi点头。关注没有减少，但空气松了一点。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 60 -> 56; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 55 -> 61; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 40 -> 44; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi的「丧亲之痛」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1949.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `r13-edu.foreign-language::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/first-conversation` tags: education, language, foreign_language, study, culture, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi每天清晨跟着Helene Bauer念法语，把动词变位写在手背上。三个月后，Mitzi在咖啡馆替一位法国客人指路，对方说的“谢谢”Mitzi听懂了。回家路上，Mitzi把这句话重复了一路，连买菜时都在心里默念。第二天Mitzi提前半小时到了课堂。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +foreign_language_certificate; skill_

- **1949.08** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.first-come-order` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi把四只洗衣盆按到厨房的先后排成一列，用粉笔在门后写下各家的时辰：六点到七点半、七点半到九点。Berta Weiss起初嘟囔，看到自家名字排在第二格，也就把水壶提了回去。晾衣绳按同样次序分两段，谁家的床单先上谁先占。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss_

- **1949.09** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Ferdinand Huber用膝盖顶住，Mitzi把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销)_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/cools` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  Mitzi发现最近没有人再提那件事。报纸换了头版，邻居也换了话题；本该轻松，Mitzi却在买菜时反复整理衣领，好像还在等一个不会来的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 56 -> 48; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 61 -> 58; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 44 -> 42; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.01** `r13-fam.adopt-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::adoption-reform/old-forms-block` tags: task, family, adoption, child, law, household, vienna, urban
  科长说新法还要等实施细则，旧表格上没有养父母一栏，盖了章也无效。Mitzi把写满情况的纸折好，带回家里压在抽屉底下。Elisabeth Kral问是不是不能改姓了；Mitzi说再等等，心里却不知道要等多久。
  _effects: mood -0.12; condition; flag +adoption_delayed_by_forms; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:010333:child; knowledge “the reform law lacks implementing rules”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

- **1950.02** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Luise Hofer排在前面，回头替Mitzi看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Mitzi用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1950.04** `sm18-coincidence.same-name::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/gives-it-to-the-church` tags: coincidence, grief, name, letter, fate, everyday, vienna, urban
  Mitzi把信送到教堂，请神父替自己念一遍。神父念到一半停下，说这是银行催缴一笔旧债的通知，欠款人早已去世。Mitzi把通知折好，投进门口的捐款箱，听见纸落在木箱底的声音。
  _effects: cash -1 (把催债通知投进捐款箱时放进去的钱); knowledge “那封写着父亲名字的信是一张早已过期的催债通知”; flag +gave_the_coincidence_to_the_church_

- **1950.05** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/stay-and-tidy-chairs` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  店主来收煤气灯，Mitzi把铜灯擦净递过去，Helene Bauer在门外数着散场的人。有人回头问Mitzi下次站哪边，Mitzi说等椅子摆回原样再想。夜里风把纸板吹倒了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1951.01** `p21-family-secret::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-sibling-was-never-a-sibling` tags: family_secret, baptism, sibling, inheritance, secret, grief, legal, vienna
  Otto Kral把两份记录并排放在桌上，指出Luise Lechner的出生日期比父母的婚礼早了整整一年。Luise Lechner没有看Mitzi，只把伞收起来，说“原来我一直在替别人守孝”。Helene Berg在门口退了一步，Mitzi听见走廊里有人把箱子拖下楼。
  _effects: relation_end kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002333:child; state_add p21-fortune-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1208; cash -2 (a share of the inheritance was returned); reputation -0.020000000000000018; knowledge “手足的出生日期比父母的婚礼早了整整一年。”; mood -0.02000000000000013_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Mitzi在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。

  _effects: cash -5 (以自家名义赊购面粉); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Mitzi嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Mitzi用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005510:child; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  桌面响了三下，又响了三下。Gustav Stein说是死者在敲门，可问到最后，没有一个名字、日期或物件对得上。Gustav Stein擦着额头说，有时候灵只说一半；Mitzi看着窗帘缝里漏进来的街灯，忽然觉得那三下更像有人在楼下关车门。
  _effects: cash -2 (降神会的费用); state_add fm17-miracle-witness state:world-vienna-1912:1252; flag +fm17_seance_unexplained; flag +fm17_miracle_witnessed; knowledge “我看见的那件事，到今天我仍说不出它是什么。”_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/carried` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又过了一年。那个人的名字不再每天出现，但在某些气味和街道上仍会回来。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 1 -> 2; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `r13-civ.exhibit-painting::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/painting-ignored` tags: culture, painting, exhibition, art, critic, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi把画挂在画廊最里面的墙角，标签上只写了“无题”。三天里，来看的人从画前走过去，停在旁边的风景画前。闭展时，Mitzi把画取下来，画框背面落了一层灰。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +painting_ignored_at_exhibition_

- **1952.05** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.flea-market-cup/flea-market-cup.haggle-cheap` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, flea_market, object
  摊主是个戴眼镜的老头，开价时把杯子往摊布上一顿。Mitzi指出杯口有个磕痕，Gustav Stein又添了一句裂纹渗水。老头摘下眼镜擦了擦，说三十就三十，反正搁着也是搁着。她数出三枚十赫勒的硬币，心里算着比杂货铺的新杯子还是便宜。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (还价后买下旧杯子); skill_

- **1952.06** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  床架从楼梯上拖下来时油漆蹭掉了好几块，Mitzi站在旁边没作声。收破烂的走后，屋里空得回声都变大了，Mitzi抱着铁盒出门，只把门锁好，钥匙从看门人手里交出去。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1952.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.notary-inheritance/leave-unsigned` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Mitzi把文件推回Elisabeth Hofer 2面前，说不签，等Alois Lechner自己改主意。Alois Lechner的嘴角动了动，Elisabeth Lechner把三把钥匙一把把收进衣袋，金属碰着金属响了几声。Mitzi走下公证处的石阶，街对面面包房正在出炉，热气扑到脸上，口袋里一个赫勒也没多，房契的事仍旧悬着。
  _effects: relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001502:child; flag +inheritance_unsigned_

  洗衣房里蒸汽弥漫，熨斗压在衬衫领子上咝咝作响。老板娘翻了翻Mitzi熨好的第一件，说领口还差些火候，手上倒还稳。两打熨完，三个克朗放在围裙口袋里，铜板还带着体温。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill_

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/no-record` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Ferdinand Huber把三本名册都翻完，说没有这个人，也许登记时用了别的写法。Grete Stein不肯走，又把名字念了一遍，念到第三遍时声音低下去。Mitzi扶她到走廊的长椅上坐下，替她把围巾重新系好；窗口后面已经叫了下一个号。
  _effects: cash -1 (念错的名字); mood -0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +missing_relative_no_record; relation kin with person-grete-stein; knowledge “there is no record under the name the aunt remembers”_

- **1953.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Ferdinand Huber拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失)_

  第四把庄，Mitzi把家里的房租也押了上去。庄家翻出九点，闲家只有三点；Mitzi Moser没有哭，只把戒指从手上摘下来放在桌边。Mitzi在天亮前走出赌场，口袋里剩下一枚筹码和一张写着地址的借条；街上的扫雪人已经开始工作。
  _effects: cash -40 (rent and savings lost at the card table); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1349; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001810:child; condition; flag +lost_the_rent_at_cards; knowledge “纸牌没有记忆，三把庄之后闲仍然不是必然。”; flag +fortune_reversed_

  _effects: flag +betrayed_after_trusting; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003178:child; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:1348_

  半年里没有新的坏消息。Mitzi把零钱分成三份：房租、还账、留一点在抽屉里。旧债主没有上门，咖啡馆里的人也不再每见一次就提那件赔本的事；日子没有变好，但至少不再往下滑。

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm

  _effects: relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; skill; mood -0.01_

  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

  教堂音乐会的独唱轮到Josef，第一句拉丁文还稳，唱到“荣耀”那个高音时，嗓子忽然劈了一下。Josef看见Josef Bauer在第三排低下头，Grete Stein在风琴旁边打手势让继续；Josef用假声把剩下的两小节唱完，声音细得像一根线。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +yf18_finished_solo; mood 0.009999999999999995_

- **1925.07** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.1_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003223_

- **1925.10** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Josef在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Grete Stein跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Josef去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; education apprenticeship_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Josef在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Josef准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Josef才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.03_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/borrowed-specimen-book` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Anna Lechner说自己眼睛不行了，带不了徒弟，但从帆布袋里抽出一本旧字样本，让Josef拿回去临摹。书皮用麻线缝过三次，扉页上有一行褪色的签名。少年双手接过来，在咖啡馆门口鞠了个躬。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; flag +typesetting_specimen_book; cash -1 (咖啡馆里请老师傅喝一杯); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1926.02** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/buy-the-ticket-tonight` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef趁Josef Bauer去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Josef趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Josef说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:201; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.04; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.10** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Josef推刨子。Josef推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Josef，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josef Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1927.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/friend-cuts-in` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在第二支曲子前把Elisabeth Bauer请走，说Josef答应过要替她看管外套。Josef抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Josef把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 65 -> 70; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 13 -> 5; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 5; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.04_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/rehearsed-conversation` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Josef在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Elisabeth Bauer三步远的地方拐进面包店。Josef买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 70 -> 76; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 5 -> 8; mood 0.010000000000000002_

- **1927.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Josef的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:201; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.teacher-recommendation/night-school-first` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:education
  Grete Stein把练习本推回来，说排字排得再齐，簿记和德语不过关，印刷厂也不会收。Grete Stein在一张纸上写下夜校的街名，让Josef先去问学费。Josef当天傍晚就拐到六区，站在门口把课表抄了一遍，第二天把一克朗报名费交进窗口。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; cash -1 (夜校一学期报名费和课本钱); flag +enrolled_night_school; skill_

- **1927.03** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Hermann Bauer先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Josef每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:237; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.05** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuse-the-machine` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  教堂年历的订单催得紧，小巷电影院把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Josef看着那块铁板，说手会进去；小巷电影院把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Josef是不是不想出师了。Josef没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Grete Weiss接手。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 3; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.02_

- **1927.12** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/burn-it-in-the-courtyard` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef在学校的煤炉边把油印纸烧了，灰烬和煤渣混在一起。Helene Bauer第二天问第三十四页读得怎么样，Josef说译文看不出来；Helene Bauer点点头，把课本收回去，从此没有再借给Josef任何书。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; flag +yf18_burned_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1927.12** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.1_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004211_

- **1928.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Josef的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:163; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Josef又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 3 -> 23; skill; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/asked-to-come-back-later` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Elisabeth Berg看了看Josef抄的字母表，说大小不一，让他回去用直尺画格子重抄一遍，下个月再来。少年把纸折好，在咖啡馆门口站了一会儿，走回印刷厂时绕到排字房窗外看了很久。
  _effects: mood -0.03; skill; flag +mentor_pending_retry; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1928.05** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Josef把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Josef那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1928.09** `r3-teen.sexual-rumor::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-school-paint-order/denied` tags: rumor, shame, school, reputation, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  墙上出现了一行关于Josef的脏话，Grete Stein让全班提水刷墙，刷了三遍还能看见白印。Mitzi在背后学那句话的腔调，Josef回头时，他正低头抠桌角的漆。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; condition; flag +target_of_sexual_rumor; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Josef又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 23 -> 43; skill; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1929.01** `r13-work.part-time::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/late-for-class` tags: work, part_time, school, adolescence, cash, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef接了一单远活，回来时天已经黑透。第二天的算术课，Josef在课堂上睡着了，Grete Stein用粉笔头敲了敲课桌。放学后Grete Stein把Josef留下，说再缺两次课就要叫家长；Josef摸着口袋里那几枚铜板，没有说是去送货了。
  _effects: cash 1 (放学后的四个钟头); reputation -0.05; flag +work13_part_time_made_late_for_school; relation rival with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1929.04** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.two-slices-of-bread` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef说自家也快见底了，不能再借面粉，临关门前往Luise Kral手里塞了两块面包，说先给孩子垫一垫。Luise Kral攥着面包站了一会儿，说了声谢谢就走了，楼梯上传来脚步和纸袋的磨蹭声。
  _effects: food; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1929.05** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.opened-letter/opened-letter-church-steps` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Josef请同住的Helene Kral跑一趟，把Gustav Moser约到教堂前的台阶上。石台阶还带着晨露，两人各占一级，把原信从头念完；念到“工钱”那处，Gustav Moser说那是家里人的意思，不是自己的话。Josef把信纸折回原样，至少这一回没有隔着别人传话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1929.05** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Helene Winter敲了门。Josef掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Helene Winter什么也没说就走了。夜里Josef听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; mood -0.05_

- **1930.01** `r15-work.journeyman-test::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed` tags: work, craft, apprentice, exam, journeyman, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把最后一块接榫敲进去，严丝合缝。Ferdinand Winter没有夸，只把成品翻过来看了看底，然后在自己名字旁边写下Josef的名字。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000001; flag +passed_journeyman_test; state_transition apprenticeship-contract -> journeyman; role_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003228_

- **1930.01** `r13-civ.arrange-move::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/neighbors-carried` tags: social, housing, moving, landlord, cash, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef没有雇车，把家什分摊给楼里的几户人家，一趟一趟往新住处搬。Rosa用童车推着锅碗，孩子在后面扶着。天黑前搬完，大家在空屋里分了一壶茶。
  _effects: cash -1 (板车与押金收据（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +moved_with_neighbor_help; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1930.01** `r1-work.unemployment-office::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/benefit-granted` tags: work, unemployment, benefit, office, queuing, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Berg 2把Josef的失业证明翻过来，对着灯看水印，又在名册上找到前一份工作的注销章。Josef领到五克朗，钱是新印的，边角还粘在一起。Gustav Stein在门口等，问够不够交房租，Josef说够买面包，房租再想办法。
  _effects: cash 5 (救济窗口的第九十七号); flag +received_unemployment_benefit; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1930.04** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-and-mother-weak` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  孩子出生后取名Friedrich Bauer，Grete Winter 2一直出血。Josef跑去医院，医生来晚了，只留下药和十八克朗的账单；Grete Winter 2落下产后虚弱的毛病，Josef抱着孩子坐在床边，直到邻居把汤送来，才发现自己的鞋穿反了。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.2 (postpartum bleeding); cash -18 (doctor and medicine); condition; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:342_

- **1930.06** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Franz Kral在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood 0.04; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1930.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  印泥是赭红色的，像干了的面包瓤。Josef按完手印，工头把表举到窗边吹干，阳光里那张纸透出指印的轮廓。楼下第十区的街面传来运煤车的铁皮声，星期六下午突然显得很宽。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.05; flag +accepted_hours_reform; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1931.01** `r15-work.first-commission::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/paid-in-full` tags: work, craft, journeyman, commerce, reputation, reform, medical, schooling
  Helene Winter来取货，把工钱一张张数在桌上。Josef第一次没有经过师傅的手接过钱。晚上收工后，Josef把工具箱擦了一遍，又把订单簿翻到新的一页。
  _effects: cash 4 (第一笔自己的订单); mood 0.06; reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +first_independent_commission_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004987_

- **1931.01** `r13-civ.new-district-lodging::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turned-away-for-accent` tags: social, housing, lodging, landlady, district, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef刚开口问价，Berta Weiss就听出口音，说这栋楼只租给本区人。Josef说自己在附近上工，Berta Weiss把招租纸重新贴回门上，说下个月再来问。Josef沿着街口走了很久，才找到另一张招租纸。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +lodging_refused_for_accent; knowledge “some buildings rent only to families from the district”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1931.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/burn-the-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  等到第九天，Josef把没寄出的草稿和那封写了一半的信一起塞进炉子。纸卷起来，火苗发蓝，屋里一股焦味；Josef拿火钩把灰捅散，决定不再去问信的下落。炉门上的铁片烫手，那个“亲”字先没了。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +gave_up_on_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1931.07** `hs18-revolution.leaflet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hide-the-stack` tags: revolution, leaflet, cinema, neighbor, politics, history, reform, medical
  Josef在电影院后门捡到一叠传单，纸背还印着上周的片名。上面写着罢工和面包，字迹歪斜。Josef Winter说警察已经到街口，Josef把传单塞进煤箱，只留下一张垫桌脚。
  _effects: knowledge “传单是用电影票纸印的，印的人就在附近。”; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000214:child; flag +hid_revolution_leaflets; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05000000000000002 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005664_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/separate-under-one-roof` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation
  Josef和Grete Winter 2把床铺分开，米和煤也各算各的。两人仍住在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，因为谁都没有钱再租一间房；孩子在两床之间来回，三克朗买了隔开的锅碗和煤油，家里从此有两本账，夜里两盏灯先后熄灭。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood -0.07999999999999999; mood -0.08; cash -3 (separate household goods)_

- **1932.01** `oj18-radio.repair::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/cold-joint` tags: radio, repair, craft, customer, work, odd_job, suspense, reform
  Hermann Huber的收音机只发出炒豆子一样的杂音。Josef把后板拆开，发现一根接地线被老鼠咬断，焊点冷得像一颗坏牙。修好以后，Hermann Huber留Josef听完了整点新闻；出门时，Josef在巷口还听见那台机器在响。
  _effects: organization_found 屋顶无线电修理铺 (household_firm); position 无线电修理匠 @radio:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006207; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:384; cash 2 (radio repair fee); skill; reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; flag +radio_repairer; knowledge “收音机的接地线被老鼠咬断，焊点冷得像一颗坏牙。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Josef去了Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus，坐在后排听完了关于维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as participant; knowledge “我参加了维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济。。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.09** `bc18-inherit.parrot-condition::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/parrot-dies` tags: inheritance, family, condition, absurd, death, legal, quiet, reform
  鹦鹉死在第二个冬天。窗缝里灌进来的风把笼布吹开，它缩在食盆旁边，像一团被揉皱的灰纸。Berta Stein说条件没有完成，十二克朗归了姑妈的教子；Josef把鸟埋在院子里，留下那只笼子。春天，笼子里长出一丛野草，Josef没有拔。
  _effects: cash -2 (鹦鹉的药费和笼布); reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; condition; flag +parrot_died_before_the_will; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1932.09** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Josef听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 18; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 27; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

- **1932.12** `bc18-crime.inept-blackmail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pay-hush-money` tags: crime, blackmail, photograph, mistake, humiliation, absurd, reputation, reform
  Josef先给了两克朗，Alois Lechner又要四克朗；第三次来收钱时，Josef把他带到了警察局。警员在Alois Lechner的口袋里找到三张别人的照片，其中一张是市议员在酒馆后门。Josef拿回自己的照片，也花掉了一个下午，回家时晚饭已经凉了。
  _effects: cash -4 (付给勒索者的封口钱); reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person-alois-lechner; condition; flag +paid_then_reported_blackmail; mood -0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Helene Berg的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; flag +kept_first_masterwork_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。

  到了后半夜，Grete Bauer的呼吸越来越急，胸口凹下去一块。Josef把最后两克朗塞给邻居，让邻居去请医生；医生天亮才来，只说怎么不早点送医院。Josef抱着孩子坐在门槛上，等一辆借来的车。

- **1934.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Josef把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Josef读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

  Josef把Helene Berg的礼拜日外套叠好，放在门房。两人去市政办事处问过，办事员说没有哪张表格能写清这种事；神父也不肯在文书上签字。Helene Berg把铺盖搬到工友的合租房，Josef退掉Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang的大房间，在同一栋楼里租了一间后屋，把结婚照留在原来的墙上。
  _effects: household; relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; role; cash -10 (moving costs and the old room rent（其中10克朗记入债务）); mood -0.08000000000000002_

  图样在交货前被Grete Winter 2带去另一家作坊。Josef在市场上看见同样的书柜，铜边更薄，价格低三成；买主不知道差别，只说这家做得快。Josef把尾款收下，回到作坊把原图锁进柜子。

  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。

- **1935.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Josef在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Josef的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

  孩子出生后取名Anton Bauer，Rosa Kral一直出血。Josef跑去医院，医生来晚了，只留下药和十八克朗的账单；Rosa Kral落下产后虚弱的毛病，Josef抱着孩子坐在床边，直到邻居把汤送来，才发现自己的鞋穿反了。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.19999999999999996 (postpartum bleeding); cash -18 (doctor and medicine); condition; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:507_

  _effects: cash -2 (milk and oatmeal)_

- **1913.02** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dead-grandmothers-name` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Rosa扶着桌沿站起来，指着门口的空处，清清楚楚叫出一个名字。屋里没人教过这个词；Alois Lechner手里的铝勺停在半空。那是去年冬天埋掉的老人的名字，窗外正好有送煤的车经过，铁轮压过石缝。
  _effects: mood -0.03; knowledge “孩子说出的第一个词，是去年死去的祖母的名字。”; flag +first_word_dead_name_

- **1913.05** `vienna-life-toddler-accident/safe-in-the-doorway` tags: infancy, accident, family, danger
  Alois Lechner在最后一刻抓住了Rosa的后领。孩子悬在两级台阶之间，鞋底蹭着地，手里还攥着那只线轴。Alois Lechner把孩子放回地上，蹲下来看了很久，才说：“再跑，我就把门关上。”
  _effects: mood -0.02; skill_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Rosa把空碗推回Alois Lechner面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Alois Lechner愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03_

- **1915.06** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/stolen-marble` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Mitzi把Rosa的玻璃弹珠藏进鞋里，被当场看见。Mitzi说只是“替你拿着”，Rosa没有揭穿，也没有再拿出第二颗。两个人蹲在墙根，把一只蚂蚁看了很久，谁也没有先回家。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood -0.01_

- **1915.06** `kernel-child.household-rules::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-rules-hides-bread` tags: childhood, family, food, rules, everyday, rural, agriculture, small_town
  锅里只剩一层薄汤。Rosa趁Alois Lechner去院里提水，掰下半个面包塞进嘴里，又把剩下的藏进围裙口袋。晚饭时Alois Lechner数了面包，没有骂，只把Rosa的那份挪到自己盘边。夜里Rosa听见灶台那边有轻轻的哭声。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +hid_bread; mood -0.05_

- **1915.07** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/wooden-horse` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  木马从报纸里滚出来，落在Rosa的膝盖上。Alois Lechner说是旧货摊上挑的，漆掉了一块，Rosa用拇指摸了摸那块木头原色。那天Rosa没舍得骑，只把木马放在枕边，半夜醒来还伸手摸了一下。
  _effects: mood 0.08; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Alois Lechner在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Rosa问还回不回来，Alois Lechner把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1917.11** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  围巾从Rosa的头一直裹到脚，Alois Lechner抱着人下楼，在电车踏板上差点滑倒。急诊窗口先收押金，Alois Lechner把内袋里的票子一张张抹平，数出六克朗。Rosa躺在观察室的窄床上，头顶的灯管嗡嗡响，Alois Lechner的手一直按着被角。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Grete Weiss家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Rosa递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Grete Weiss端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Rosa把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-half-credit` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “月底一起结。”掌柜说完，把半筐煤推到柜台外。Rosa用胸口顶住筐沿往家挪，煤灰落进领口，手心被麻绳磨得发亮。Alois Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口等到人，先看了看筐底，再看了看Rosa的手，把漏掉的碎渣扫进簸箕。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Franz Kral从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Rosa。Rosa把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1920.04** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Rosa跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Otto Winter从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Rosa后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Rosa没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Rosa用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1921.02** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1921.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1921.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1921.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1921.08** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  土豆从围裙里滚出来，Rosa蹲下去追，手心一松，三枚十克鲁泽的铜板叮叮当当掉在石板路上。两枚捡回来了，最后一枚滚进排水格栅，在铁条下转了个圈。Rosa趴下去够，指甲刮在铁条上。回家Alois Lechner数完钱，脸色沉下来，Rosa站在桌边不敢动。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.05999999999999999_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-half-credit` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “月底一起结。”掌柜说完，把半筐煤推到柜台外。Rosa用胸口顶住筐沿往家挪，煤灰落进领口，手心被麻绳磨得发亮。Alois Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口等到人，先看了看筐底，再看了看Rosa的手，把漏掉的碎渣扫进簸箕。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1922.05** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳下挂着湿床单，孩子们绕着跑，鞋底把水甩到墙上。Rosa在第三圈超过Maria Kral，手先碰到墙根。两颗玻璃弹珠进了Rosa口袋，外套第二颗纽扣却崩进下水道。Maria Kral从二楼窗口骂了一句，扔下一根别针。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

- **1922.11** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，伤寒让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1922.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的伤寒没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Ferdinand Moser把Rosa拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Rosa把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Rosa蹲下把两包并排，按Ferdinand Moser说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1923.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的伤寒没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1923.05** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa熬过了这场伤寒，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1923.05** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/take-over-bed-and-ledger` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Franz Lechner走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Rosa把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Rosa坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-franz-lechner; skill; mood -0.05; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

- **1924.02** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Rosa把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Alois Lechner端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Rosa那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1924.04** `r3-teen.sexual-rumor::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-school-paint-order/confronted` tags: rumor, shame, school, reputation, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa在操场边堵住Maria Huber，问他从哪听来的。他先说是别人说的，后来承认是自己编的，因为Rosa在课堂上答对了他答错的问题。Anton Berg让他们各写一份说明，两份都锁进抽屉。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; flag +confronted_rumor_source; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; knowledge “the classmate invented the rumour out of envy”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1924.10** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Lechner看了看Rosa的手，说太小，拿不稳凿子。Rosa回家没有提这件事，第二天去码头找零工。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +apprenticeship_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/homesick-return` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa在寄宿学校住了不到一个月，夜里总听见火车声，想起家里的炉子和妹妹。Rosa给Alois Lechner写信，写了撕、撕了写，最后只寄出一张明信片。Alois Lechner来接Rosa那天，Rosa的箱子还没完全打开。回到家，Rosa把铁皮箱放在床底，再没打开，腌菜罐却一直留在桌上。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1925.01** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/finish-in-falsetto` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  教堂音乐会的独唱轮到Rosa，第一句拉丁文还稳，唱到“荣耀”那个高音时，嗓子忽然劈了一下。Rosa看见Alois Lechner在第三排低下头，Grete Stein在风琴旁边打手势让继续；Rosa用假声把剩下的两小节唱完，声音细得像一根线。
  _effects: reputation -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +yf18_finished_solo; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1925.01** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/buy-the-ticket-tonight` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa趁Alois Lechner去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Rosa趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Rosa说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:166; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1925.06** `yf18-firstlook.atlas-margin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/write-back-in-pencil` tags: first_love, school, atlas, note, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  地理课上，Rosa在地图册的空白处看见一行铅笔字：“多瑙河从维也纳流走，不从这儿。”Rosa认出是Hedwig的笔迹，趁Grete Stein转身，在下面添了一句：“那你往哪儿流？”下课铃响时，Rosa把地图册塞进Hedwig的课桌。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:167; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_atlas_note; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.08** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-parent-notice/fined` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Ferdinand Winter 2说按条例罚三克朗，交不出就通知学校。Alois Lechner从内衣口袋里摸出叠好的钞票，一张张数，数到第三张时手停了。回家后，那三克朗被写在厨房的账单上，旁边画了一道红线。
  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.07999999999999999; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1926.01** `state_expire:yf18-runaway-plan` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Rosa的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:166; flag -yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Rosa在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Hedwig。这一次对方先点了头，Rosa准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Rosa才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 60; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 30 -> 38; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

  Anna Kral种完痘把卡片交给老师，老师随手夹进点名册里。放学时点名册被带去办公室，Rosa空着手回家，Alois Lechner问有没有证明，她只能把袖子拉起来。
  _effects: health 0.04 (种痘室门口的袖子); mood -0.03; condition; flag +vaccination_card_misplaced; knowledge “the school keeps the vaccination card in the class register”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  Anton Berg 2的灯从摩天轮那边晃过来时，Josef已经跑没影了，Rosa的鞋带却挂在木槽边。Anton Berg 2把Rosa带到园门口，记下住址，说星期一让学校来领人；Rosa一路上只担心Josef会不会把这件事讲给全班。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +yf18_prater_caught; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; cash -2 (a fine paid at the Prater office); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1926.06** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/give-them-away` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把两张票送给门口卖花的小女孩，自己在普拉特大街上看橱窗。橱窗里摆着一双和Mitzi一样的白手套；Rosa站到商店关门，回家时说票弄丢了，谁也没有再问。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 68 -> 60; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_gave_tickets_away; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1926.08** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Mitzi Lechner说该自己走，Rosa说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Rosa摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 68; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 38 -> 48; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Rosa在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Hedwig。这一次对方先点了头，Rosa准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Rosa才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 48 -> 56; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1927.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Rosa就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Rosa还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Rosa在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1927.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Rosa把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Rosa用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 65 -> 62; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 2_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003953_

- **1927.01** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/burn-it-in-the-courtyard` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa在学校的煤炉边把油印纸烧了，灰烬和煤渣混在一起。Grete Stein第二天问第三十四页读得怎么样，Rosa说译文看不出来；Grete Stein点点头，把课本收回去，从此没有再借给Rosa任何书。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; flag +yf18_burned_pamphlet_

- **1927.06** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/kept-home-for-kin` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Alois Lechner病倒在床，Rosa留在家里看弟妹、煮稀粥、去井边打水。Helene Bauer托人带来一包旧课本，Rosa在灶火边翻了两页，弟弟哭了，只好合上。一个月后，学校把Rosa的名字从名册上划掉，墨迹很淡。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +left_school_for_family; role_

- **1927.06** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Grete Stein把成绩单按在讲台上，说Rosa还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Rosa签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; role; education apprenticeship_

- **1928.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Rosa的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:167; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

  Luise Winter看了看Rosa抄的字母表，说大小不一，让他回去用直尺画格子重抄一遍，下个月再来。少年把纸折好，在咖啡馆门口站了一会儿，走回印刷厂时绕到排字房窗外看了很久。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill; flag +mentor_pending_retry; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1928.04** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne捏了捏Rosa的虎口，又让Rosa张开手指，说骨节还没长开，推硬木会伤手。Alois Lechner想再问一句，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne已经转身去搬木料。Rosa退到门槛外，听见刨子重新响起来，木花从门缝里卷到脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1928.09** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/forge-the-permit` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa在市政厅的表格上替Alois Lechner签了名，又把日期往前挪了三天。办事员看了Rosa一眼，问父亲怎么没来；Rosa说他病在床上。印章落下去时，Rosa的手心全是汗，出门才发现自己把回执捏成了两半。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:271; cash -2 (stamps and a clerk's fee); flag +yf18_forged_permit; reputation -0.009999999999999995; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1928.09** `r3-teen.sexual-rumor::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-school-paint-order/denied` tags: rumor, shame, school, reputation, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  墙上出现了一行关于Rosa的脏话，Helene Bauer让全班提水刷墙，刷了三遍还能看见白印。Josef Winter在背后学那句话的腔调，Rosa回头时，他正低头抠桌角的漆。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; condition; flag +target_of_sexual_rumor; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000214:child_

- **1928.12** `r3-teen.first-rent::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-rent-receipt/pays` tags: housing, rent, arrears, eviction, independence, reform, medical, schooling
  Ferdinand Winter把收据铺在油布上，用蘸水笔写日期。Rosa把硬币一枚枚码好，推到桌子对面，听见最后一枚碰到桌沿。Ferdinand Winter点完，把收据撕下来，说下个月还是这一天。
  _effects: cash -6 (第一张房租收据); flag +paid_first_rent; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; knowledge “rent is due on the same day each month”_

  Rosa把去车站的路又走了一遍，记住了哪一段有煤气灯、哪一段住着巡夜的警察。回家后，Rosa在旧日历背面画了一条线，标出换乘的站名；那条线短得可笑，却比课本上的任何地图都清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 60 -> 72; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 20 -> 22; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Rosa把招工纸条带回家，父亲看了一眼就揉成团，说家里还没穷到要孩子去站柜台。母亲把纸条从炉子里抢出来，抹平了夹进课本。第二天放学，Rosa还是绕到铺子门口看了一眼，Elisabeth Berg正把一筐土豆搬进后屋。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +work13_parents_refused_part_time; knowledge “the grocer needs a boy after school”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1929.01** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Rosa把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Rosa没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 22 -> 30; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 65 -> 63_

  Ferdinand Moser来得比约定早，手里提着一小袋栗子。Rosa多摆了一副碗筷，又把汤里的肉往那只碗里拨了两块。饭吃到一半，Ferdinand Moser忽然说“你们家吃饭真安静”，Rosa才发现自己一直在听楼上的脚步。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; flag +family_dinner_guest_

- **1929.03** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Rosa对Grete Winter 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Grete Winter 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Rosa的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  Josef嫌Rosa的床单滴水滴到Josef家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Rosa下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Rosa把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Josef那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1929.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  下午的活是搬纱锭，Rosa蹲下去再站起来眼前发黑，扶墙站了一会儿。工友老约瑟夫把自己的水杯推过来，说去诊所开个假条吧，她摇头，说假条要扣钱。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

  Hedwig把粥熬得很稀，一勺一勺吹凉，托着Rosa的后颈喂下去。窗帘拉开半尺，太阳从床脚移到墙上，Rosa第一次觉得退烧后的骨头是轻的。Hedwig把药钱记在日历背面，说下个月少买两次肉。
  _effects: cash -2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); food; health 0.08 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_kin_care_

- **1930.01** `ad20-market-day::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-scale-is-light` tags: market, food, money, neighbor, everyday, credit, reform, medical
  市场日的人挤到棚布底下。Franz Hofer把Rosa的篮子放上秤，秤砣往下一沉，又悄悄用拇指抵住秤杆。Rosa看见了，没有说；Franz Hofer多塞了两根葱，说“今天的葱不值钱”。Grete Stein 2从过道那头走来，两个人同时把篮子往身后挪了挪。
  _effects: cash 1 (a market stall gave short weight and then overpaid in change); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; flag +market_short_weight; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “市场那台秤的秤砣被摊主用拇指抵过。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

- **1930.03** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Rosa把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Alois Berg递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1930.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Otto Stein在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Rosa把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1930.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Josef Bauer从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-josef-bauer_

  Rosa在车行谈好一辆板车，又去新住处交了押金。Helene Bauer把收据写得极简，只写“收到五克朗”，连地址都省了。Rosa把收据折好，和户口纸一起放进内袋。
  _effects: cash -5 (板车与押金收据); flag +move_arranged_with_cart; knowledge “the new landlord writes receipts without an address”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

  Rosa拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1931.04** `ad20-stranger-request::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/take-the-envelope` tags: stranger, envelope, request, everyday, obligation, risk, reform, medical
  Franz Hofer在巷口拦住Rosa，把一个没有字的信封塞过来，说“明天中午之前，别打开”。Rosa问里面是什么；Franz Hofer说“你不需要知道”。信封很薄，捏起来却像有一块硬东西。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:387; cash 2 (payment for carrying the envelope); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; flag +envelope_taken; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “信封很薄，里面像有一块硬东西。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1931.07** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.pay-later` tags: home, market, food, social
  Rosa捏着三个克朗硬币站在玛丽亚的菜摊前，土豆和洋葱已经装进布袋。周五结清时，玛丽亚用粉笔在小黑板上划掉名字，Otto Weiss点点头。
  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账（其中1克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1932.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Josef Bauer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Rosa把床单对折，腾出一半，Josef Bauer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  作坊里的学徒都在看。他把第三个榫头装上去，木槌敲了两下才到位。Elisabeth Berg说带一个来，明天。他把废料收进工具袋，出了门才发现袖子沾满木屑。

  Rosa傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Elisabeth Bauer在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Elisabeth Bauer敲开Rosa的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1932.03** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Rosa把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Josef Lechner放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Mitzi在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household_

- **1932.04** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.mediate-sibling-conflict::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/truce` tags: task, family, sibling, conflict, mediation, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi把Mitzi Lechner的帽子扔进院子，Mitzi Lechner又把晾衣杆撂倒。Rosa站在两人中间，先把帽子捡起来拍干净，再让两人各说一遍是谁先动的手。说到第三遍，两人都不那么肯定了；Rosa把帽子放在桌上，说谁再扔就赔谁的工钱。
  _effects: flag +mediated_sibling_fight; role; skill; relation kin with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1933.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Rosa手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1933.09** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/home-remedy` tags: health, family, home
  邻居过来看了一眼，教Rosa用湿布敷额。Katharina Lechner天亮时安静睡了，Rosa却耽误半日活计；一克朗五十赫勒的工钱没了，床边的醋味却留了一整天。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (缺工半日被扣工钱（其中1.5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1933.10** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把通知念给Grete Stein听，Grete Stein摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Rosa去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.flour-loan/flour-loan.full-bag` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Rosa把一整袋面粉倒进Elisabeth Bauer 2的空口袋，袋口用麻绳扎了两道，说等她男人发了薪再还。Elisabeth Bauer 2连声道谢，把写着借煤日期的小纸片折好放回围裙。柜子里只剩一袋面粉，Rosa当晚煮汤时多加了一勺水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Rosa把Elisabeth Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Elisabeth Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Rosa听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.01** `r13-work.inherit-business::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/debts-came-with-it` tags: commerce, inheritance, family_firm, debt, succession, vienna, urban, crisis
  钥匙交到手里第二天，供货商就拿着欠条上门。Alois Lechner说那些账是铺子的，不是她的；供货商说铺子现在归Rosa，账也归Rosa。Rosa把存货盘了一遍，能抵的抵，剩下十克朗写了还款计划；印章还在，但盖下去的第一份文件是欠条。
  _effects: cash -10 (父亲的账本和印章（其中10克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +work13_inherited_business_with_debts; role; employment Kaufmann @ taxi:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52-evt:world-vienna-1912:005114; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1934.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Mitzi Lechner拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1934.06** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-bed-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  看门人收下三克朗，把一把磨得发亮的钥匙交到Rosa手里。厨房角落的炉子上还坐着别人的锅，水汽混着霉味。Rosa把钥匙挂在脖子上，打算明日一早再回旧处取剩下的东西。
  _effects: cash -3 (先付一张床位的月租（其中3克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1934.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Rosa把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

- **1935.01** `vienna-life-depression/leaves-vienna` tags: adulthood, depression, work, migration, unemployment
  Rosa听说下奥地利的砖厂还招人，收拾了一只箱子，坐早班火车离开维也纳。厂里的工头在背后说，走了就别回来。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings（其中8克朗记入债务）)_

- **1935.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.borrow-handcart/handcart-and-ten` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  星期天搬家的前一晚，有人坐在印厂友人家的厨房里把事说全：车要借，十克朗也要借。对方合上账本，从抽屉里数出两张五克朗，说月底先还三克朗，剩下的按月摊。手推车的轮轴有点响，搬之前得先上油。
  _effects: cash 10 (友人借给搬家应急的十克朗); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1935.01** `p21-mistaken-identity::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-cell-for-someone-else` tags: mistaken_identity, police, identity, arrest, public, secret, absurd, vienna
  Grete Stein 2没有再看照片，只让Rosa把口袋里的东西全放在桌上。银怀表、一张电车票和半包烟；表盖打开时，里面那张小照片上的人不是Rosa。Franz Moser在门外想说什么，被另一个警察挡住了。拘留室的门关上时，Rosa才发现自己一直攥着那张电车票。
  _effects: state_add detained state:world-vienna-1912:549; state_add p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:550; reputation -0.03; flag +jailed_under_wrong_name; knowledge “表盖里的小照片上的人不是当事人，拘留室的门关上时，当事人还攥着电车票。”; mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +detained; flag +scandal_web_active_

- **1935.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Rosa靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1935.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  楼道里正晾着几家洗过的衣服，Rosa和Elisabeth Stein 2站在水滴下面说话，声音压得低。Elisabeth Stein 2说这栋楼里的住户不是头一回被这样要钱，先凑个口信比一个人硬顶强。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1936.02** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.pay-increase` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Rosa把纸条压在糖罐下面，算了半天：两克朗正好是四天的面包钱。月底还是把涨后的房租数出来交给看门人，铜币在桌上摞成两小堆。那一周的晚饭改成面包和稀汤，猪油省着抹。
  _effects: cash -2 (按涨租纸条多付的两克朗); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

  Rosa在办公室承认自己连火柴头用什么配都不知道。Otto Kral沉默了一会儿，忽然问Rosa愿不愿意每周三下午坐在展厅里，给参观者演示“发明家如何沉思”。酬劳是三克朗，外加一顶借来的礼帽。第一个周三，Rosa坐了四个小时，只被一个孩子问过“你会不会变魔术”。
  _effects: cash 3 (扮演活展品的酬劳); state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:593; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; flag +hired_as_living_exhibit; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -1 (付给乐队的小费与舞会入场费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +retroactive_sinecure; knowledge “临时调查员的岗位是上一任留下的，工作内容从未书面通知。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Rosa的名字从海报上撤下；Rosa在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Rosa是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 18 -> 10; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 10 -> 22; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 0; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Otto Stein半夜敲墙，说同屋的人烧得说胡话。Rosa隔着板壁问要不要去叫医生，Otto Stein说医生来了也只给退热粉，还要收出诊费。天亮时，楼梯间多了一股石灰水味，Maria Bauer 2把白线画到了隔壁门口。
  _effects: cash -2 (门框上的石灰线); flag +crisis_quarantine_house; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; knowledge “a second household on the stair was placed under quarantine”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000501:child; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +parent_hospitalized; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_
