  一笔该到的钱没有到。Rosa在账房里坐到天黑，听见隔壁把算盘珠子拨得很快；回家时，门口已经有人等着，手里拿着一份折过三次的账单。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.height 20 -> 12; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.fragility 80 -> 88; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.debt 60 -> 65; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +fortune_scaffold_shook_

- **1946.01** `kernel-family.housing-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/housing-relative.refused` tags: task, task:family, family, housing, relatives, godparent, guardian, household
  Rosa算了算家里的粮和煤，说实在住不下。教父Anna Kral在车站的长椅上睡了两夜，后来被巡夜的人带去收容所；Rosa听说后，托人送去一床旧毯子，没有留名字。收容所的回执上只写了姓名和日期。
  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; role; cash -1 (an old blanket sent to the shelter); flag +relative_turned_away; knowledge “亲戚在车站长椅上睡了两夜，后来被巡夜的人带走。”_

- **1946.02** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Hedwig把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Hedwig在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1946.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Otto Weiss在门口替Rosa捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中0.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙)_

- **1946.04** `oj18-taxi.night-fare::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/coop-keys` tags: taxi, night, driver, passenger, work, odd_job, everyday, vienna
  合作社的调度Franz Hofer把一枚铜牌和一把车钥匙推过柜台，说夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。Rosa把铜牌挂在后视镜上，第一晚就拉了七个客人，最后一个在车上睡着，打鼾像一台小马达。
  _effects: organization_found 第二区夜班出租合作社 (other); position 夜班司机 @taxi:person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:015398; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:1003; cash 2 (night taxi fares); flag +taxi_night_shift; knowledge “夜班只跑环城路，十一点以后不接去普拉特的车。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1947.01** `df18-dream.three-knocks::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/chalk-marks` tags: dream, omen, grief, neighbor, consolation, eerie, religious, vienna
  头七那夜，Rosa梦见有人敲了三下门。醒来时天还没亮，门板上真的多了三道粉笔印，像是小孩画的。Friedrich Lechner 2说可能是送煤的记号；Rosa没有擦，出门时绕开那三道印，晚上回来时它们还在。
  _effects: knowledge “我梦见三下敲门声，门板上真的出现了三道粉笔印。”; condition; flag +df18_dream_of_three_knocks; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/the-logbook-mislaid` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Rosa半个月的夜班钱；Rosa没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:p21-fortune-reversal/the-crowd-recounts/the-crowd-remembers` tags: p21-fortune-reversal, fortune_reversal, fortune, reversal, inheritance, ruin, public, witnesses
  一年过去，街上还有人记得Rosa那阵子的排场。债主没有立刻上门，亲戚也还愿意在星期天来吃饭；Rosa把新添的银器擦了一遍，发现其中一只底下刻着别人的名字。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.height 12 -> 16; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.witnesses 50 -> 56; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.fragility 88 -> 91; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Rosa在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; state_parameter grief.years 0 -> 1_

- **1947.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Rosa为圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsame出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3-evt:world-vienna-1912:006677-1936 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000013_

- **1947.05** `sm18-letter.misdelivered::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/opens-it-at-the-stove` tags: letter, mistake, grief, neighbor, secret, everyday, vienna, urban
  Rosa把信放在灶台边烘着，蜡封一软就揭开了。里面除了一张家常话，还夹着一张北站寄存处的牌子；收信人的名字不是Alois Berg家的姓。Rosa把牌子翻过来，背面用铅笔写着一个日期，正是Alois Berg的儿子阵亡后第三周。
  _effects: state_add sm18-town-secret state:world-vienna-1912:1048; knowledge “邻居阵亡儿子的信里夹着一张北站寄存牌，日期在阵亡后第三周”; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +opened_the_misdelivered_letter; flag +carries_a_neighborhood_secret_

- **1947.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.ask-master` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  柜台上摊着两张工资袋，一张油墨新，一张已经磨毛了边。Rosa把两袋并排放，掌柜伸出食指把旧的拨到一边，说“你管好自己的”。师兄在窗口看街对面修鞋摊，一直没回头。
  _effects: reputation -0.03999999999999998; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; skill_

- **1947.12** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Katharina Lechner 2在午休时把本子摊在Rosa的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Rosa从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Katharina Lechner 2当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108_

- **1948.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cover-up` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Rosa请Maria Bauer 2去咖啡馆，把一只没有标记的信封留在桌上。Maria Bauer 2没有碰信封，只把报告里“重复报销”改成“记账口径不一”；作为交换，Rosa签了Maria Bauer 2侄子的实习证明。两个人都没有提那只信封后来去了哪里。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; flag +bribed_auditor; knowledge “审计报告里的“重复报销”被改成了“记账口径不一”，实习证明由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:sm18-town-secret/the-secret-keeps/held-behind-the-teeth` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  半年里，Rosa在楼梯口三次遇见那件不该提的事，每次都把话咽回去。秘密没有变轻，只是被放到了一个更深的抽屉里；Rosa开始记得自己每一次沉默是在哪一层楼梯。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 25 -> 22; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.proof 60 -> 62_

  审计员在顾问的考勤卡上看见连续四十个“外勤”，而外勤地点写的是同一家咖啡馆。Rosa把考勤卡收进抽屉，补了一张“社区联络”的说明；Helene 2的工资没有停，但办公室门口多了一张审计通知。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:1091; flag +sinecure_audited; knowledge “考勤卡上的四十次外勤都指向同一家咖啡馆。”; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1948.05** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Anna Hofer用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Rosa才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; flag +parish_registered_

- **1949.01** `fm17-consolation.kitchen::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-empty-chair` tags: faith, consolation, grief, family, neighbor, care, quiet, vienna
  Rosa没有开门，只说今天不想见人。Maria Stein把汤留在门口，压了一张纸条：不用回话。天黑以后，Rosa把汤端进来，坐在那张空椅子对面吃完；碗底有一层薄薄的扁豆，她没有倒掉。
  _effects: food; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +fm17_consolation_at_the_door_

  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 45 -> 55; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 40 -> 34; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; reputation -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: flag +work13_reference_letter_refused; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

  Rosa把Luise Lechner的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Elisabeth Berg提醒说预算只够半个位置，Rosa还是把名字留下了。第二天，Luise Lechner提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @taxi:person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:015398; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002333:child; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +hired_protege_

  街口有人家出了丑事，围观的人转到这条巷子时，Rosa发现自己也站在门口。Rosa没有说任何名字，但回家以后，还是把窗帘拉得比平时早。

  Rosa在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Anna Hofer却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Rosa把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1950.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Rosa嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Rosa用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006147:child; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1951.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Rosa一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Franz Kral送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1951.01** `ad20-school-meeting::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/teacher-blames` tags: family, school, child, meeting, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Grete Stein说Emil Moser这学期退步，是因为家里晚上太吵。Rosa想说自己夜里在做计件活，话到嘴边又咽回去；Emil Moser站在走廊尽头，书包带子断了一边。回家的路上，Rosa把书包带打了个结，谁也没有提老师的话。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 67 -> 63; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:009673:child; cash -1 (extra exercise books after the meeting（其中1克朗记入债务）)_

  纪念仪式上，Franz Bauer问Rosa当年停战时在做什么。Rosa说在擦枪，擦到一半听见外面安静了，还以为是耳朵坏了。Franz Bauer笑，Rosa也笑，笑完把手里那支没点的烟放回口袋。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +told_armistice_story_to_neighbor; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

  巷口的争吵从一句脏话变成拳头。Rosa被推倒在排水沟边，Emil Hofer的鞋跟踩在肋侧；等巡警吹哨，打人的那伙人已经散了，只剩Rosa的一颗牙在雪里。他把那颗牙包在手帕里，第二天才想起去诊所。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (doctor, bandages and a tram home（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; knowledge “巷口打架时巡警要等哨声才来，打人的那伙人总先散，牙掉了只能自己捡。”_

  _effects: flag +has_work_lead; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147_

- **1952.01** `sm18-secret.market-rumor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-rumor-comes-back-wrong` tags: secret, rumor, market, neighbor, reputation, consequence, vienna, urban
  Rosa只在菜摊前说了半句“那家人地下室里”，第二天整条街都在传Elisabeth Winter 2杀了人。Grete Bauer一边称土豆一边问尸体埋在哪；Rosa说不是这么回事，Grete Bauer已经把这话当成了新的版本。

  Rosa在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Rosa去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为环城大道写字楼管理处的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。

  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Rosa让Anton Weiss冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Alois Hofer 2寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Rosa把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:1295; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003560:child; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110; flag +bureaucratic_ghost_

  Elisabeth Stein在门口堵了三天，第四天把Rosa的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Rosa把办公室的钥匙交给Alois Hofer 2，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 夜班司机 @taxi:person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:015398; reputation -0.08000000000000002; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Rosa学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Rosa的材料齐全。年底，Rosa从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 39; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 47; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1953.01** `kernel-crisis.task-rescue-neighbor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rescue-too-late` tags: task, rescue, fire, neighbor, betrayal, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa撞开阁楼门，烟先滚出来，Rosa Bauer倒在门后，手指还扣着门槛。Rosa和Maria Stein拽着Rosa Bauer的腋下往外拖，拖到楼梯口时人已经没气；梁木跟着塌下，只压住一只烧焦的箱子。消防队赶开围观的人，登记了Rosa的名字，Rosa Bauer的名字写在另一张纸上。Rosa的袖口烧穿，去诊所包扎花了三克朗，那只箱子留在院子里，谁也没再碰。
  _effects: death (smoke inhalation and burns in a building fire); condition; role; cash -3 (burnt clothes and a doctor visit)_

- **1953.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Anna Weiss在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Rosa把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水)_

  Rosa和门房为号码顺序争起来，被推到街边，帽子滚进水沟。傍晚回来时窗口已经关了，长椅上空空荡荡，只剩一张踩脏的挂号单；Rosa在湿冷街头耗了一天，咳嗽更深，诊所门口的人也都记住了这张吵红的脸。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

  一个税务员把Rosa的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Rosa收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Rosa先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 39 -> 46; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 59 -> 69; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead_

  Rosa把信写了三遍，最后只留下房租的数目和一句“开春还”。Elisabeth Lechner第三天就来了，从内袋摸出几张票子，没有数，只说别让嫂子知道。Rosa要写借条，Elisabeth Lechner把纸推开，说记得就行；两人在门口站了一会儿，谁也没有提小时候的事。
  _effects: cash 8 (信里只写了一个数目); flag +borrowed_from_sibling; role; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001502:child; knowledge “the sibling lent money without telling his wife”_

  Rosa对Alois Huber说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Alois Huber把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Rosa的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

  Gustav Lechner把住院单推回来，说押金不够，先去门外等。Rosa在台阶上坐到天黑，怀里揣着药和一张转诊单，最后跟着收尸车旁边的人流走回了家。第二天咳嗽更重，她把转诊单压在枕头下，没再去。

- **1955.04** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/queue-until-noon` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Rosa在诊所门口从五点半站到十一点，膝盖发僵，终于用最后四十赫勒付了挂号费。门房撕下转诊单，叫Rosa去综合医院排队，药费还得另算；队伍拐过街角时天还黑着，Rosa数着前面二十三个后脑勺，记住了综合医院药房下午才开窗口。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”_

- **1955.05** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Rosa点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Rosa Huber量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999994; flag +pasted_public_health_notices; role; skill; knowledge “the notice was answered with a handwritten instruction to bo”_

  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003178:child_

  Rosa把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Luise Lechner借给Rosa一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Rosa睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa看见Franz Hofer在楼梯口歇了三回，便把煤筐接过来，一筐一筐抬上三楼。最后一趟，Rosa的腰在门框上撞了一下，Franz Hofer从柜里摸出一小瓶李子酒，硬倒了一盅。
  _effects: food; flag +carried_neighbour_coal; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

  Josef Bauer把Emil抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josef Bauer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04; health 0.010000000000000009 (a good day)_

  Grete Stein的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Emil坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Grete Stein把桶一放，夹起Emil进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Josef Bauer赶来时，Emil正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。

  _effects: skill; flag +imaginary_friend_

- **1917.05** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Emil踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josef Bauer把Emil抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Emil举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.05_

- **1917.05** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home, injury
  Emil在洗衣房的长凳上睡着，醒来时喉咙像塞了棉花。Katharina Bauer把最后的干净布巾浸了凉水，敷在Emil额头上，整夜换了七八回。第四天Emil能坐起来喝粥，下嘴唇脱下一小片皮，Katharina Bauer用指尖替Emil拈掉。
  _effects: health -0.09999999999999998 (热病高烧); condition; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Emil的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Emil在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1918.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  Emil想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Otto Winter听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Emil试着站起来，被Otto Winter按回台阶上。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Emil空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Katharina Bauer来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Emil用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Emil把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Katharina Bauer。
  _effects: mood -0.04; food; skill_

- **1919.10** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，伤寒让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1919.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的伤寒没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1919.12** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  “三张换一张。”Josef把皇帝像在Emil眼前晃了晃。Emil数出三张攒了半年的烟画，一张张递过去。皇帝像到手还没捂热，Josef已经推开杂货铺的门，用同一张卡换了糖。Emil把卡举到光下看，觉得皇帝的脸有点歪。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Maria Kral把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Emil伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Maria Kral从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Emil手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Emil一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1920.03** `statistical_recovery`
  Emil从伤寒中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Emil空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Josef Bauer来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Emil用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Emil把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Josef Bauer。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; food; skill_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Emil站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Grete Lechner在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Emil掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Emil才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1922.03** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/passed-gift-to-younger` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Emil把木马推给弟妹，说“这个给你”，自己翻开那本旧图画书。纸页发黄，角上留着别人的铅笔字。Josef Bauer问真的不要木马吗，Emil点头，眼睛没离开书页。此后几天，Emil一有空就翻那本书，连书脊都摸得发亮。
  _effects: relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +likes_reading; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1922.06** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店的门铃响一次，Emil就往柜台边挪一步。Otto Bauer终于从柜台底下抽出一张风景卡，用手掌抹了抹，还是皱的。“拿去吧。”Otto Bauer说完就去招呼别的客人。Emil把卡举到鼻子前闻，一股烟草味，回家路上把多出来的一张卡分给Otto Bauer看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000167:child_

- **1922.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书页在桌肚里翻动时，Anton Berg已经站在旁边。Emil的手还按在算术本上，书被抽走，书脊在讲台边磕了一声。Anton Berg看了一眼折角，说“放学留下”，又拉开抽屉把书放进去。Emil盯着抽屉缝，整节课没听进去。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Grete Weiss把Emil拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Emil把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Emil蹲下把两包并排，按Grete Weiss说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1923.07** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Emil把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Emil那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1924.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/silent-follow` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Emil把要说的话在嘴里嚼了一路，到了门口却只跟着推车走。装订铺后院堆着纸毛，Helene Huber把一捆纸往Emil怀里一塞，Emil趔趄半步，还是抱进了门。天黑后铺子上了锁，Emil从后门退出来，手掌上全是纸毛，心里却记住了这条路。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1924.05** `r15-education.entry-offer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/conditional-support` tags: education, scholarship, opportunity, reform, medical, schooling, home, family
  Emil只拿到部分资助；剩下的学费要靠家里凑，或者靠夜校补。母亲把首饰盒翻到底，父亲说再等一年，学校却只给到下学期。
  _effects: state_add scholarship-holder state:world-vienna-1912:110; cash -15 (family pays remainder（其中14克朗记入债务）)_

- **1924.07** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.neither` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Berta Stein看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Josef Bauer求了半天，Berta Stein答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Josef干了三天就走了，Emil留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Berta Stein才让他碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1924.12** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/place-for-a-baptism` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, reform, medical, schooling
  Josefine Hofer说学额可以免，但Emil得先补洗礼，不然不算堂区孩子。Josef Bauer在圣器室门口站了很久，最后说洗就洗吧。Hermann Bauer在旁边把这名字写进名单，笔尖顿了顿，说下周一记得带铅笔。
  _effects: mood -0.039999999999999994; condition; flag +school_place_tied_to_baptism; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “a free parish school place requires baptism”; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

  亲戚把他带到工头面前，工头让他下星期来试半天，先看裁纸和搬版。母亲熨平的衬衫领子还是硬得硌脖子，他站在厂门口把姓名说了两遍。
  _effects: flag +half_day_trial_at_big_printshop; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

  Emil在高音上停住，嘴唇还张着，声音却没有出来。风琴继续往前走了两小节，Grete Stein开始让合唱队接；Emil从唱诗班的侧门跑下去，木楼梯在脚下响得像一串惊雷。散场时，Katharina Hofer 2在教堂门口等，Emil从后门走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; flag +yf18_fled_solo; mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

  Emil趁Josef Bauer去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Emil趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Emil说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:161; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.04; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  Grete Lechner把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Emil看期限、工钱和出师考试。Emil签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:162; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.11** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Emil把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Emil没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 70 -> 68_

  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance); role_

  Helene Bauer让全班按座位排队，Grete Lechner在讲台边摆开酒精瓶和针盒。轮到Emil时，Helene Bauer替他把袖子卷好，说别怕，去年他自己也种过。下课后，黑板上还留着一行没擦掉的日期。
  _effects: health 0.05 (种痘室门口的袖子); condition; flag +vaccinated_in_school_campaign; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  Emil蹲在面包房后门的台阶上，手指抠着筐沿。Maria Kral提着篮子过来，脚步很轻，掰下半个黑面包塞进Emil手里，说“别让里面的人看见”。Emil咬了两口，把剩下的包进纸里，回家放在桌上。

  面包房后门的筐里堆着隔夜面包。Emil拿起半个黑面包，刚要往怀里塞，Luise Moser从晾衣绳后面绕出来，手一伸，掌心朝上。Emil把面包放回去，Luise Moser用指节在Emil手背上敲了两下，声音不大，巷口的人都听见了。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; mood -0.10000000000000003_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.leave-bad-workshop/half-measures-stay` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Emil把窗户指给Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne看，说木板钉死了，味道散不出去。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让伙计拆掉一半，窗缝里透进风，油墨味淡了一点。清洗剂还是原来那桶，Emil每天下午太阳穴发紧，就用冷水搓手指。工位保住了，日薪照发，工钱袋和平时一样沉。

- **1929.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Emil在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中12克朗记入债务）); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1929.06** `p9-work-printshop.printed-secret/ask-for-name` tags: work, printshop, printing, compositor, craft, workshop, secret, handbill
  Emil把笔推过去，要来人在单子上写下姓名和地址。Luise Winter收起纸，把帽子压低，从后门走了，那晚机器一次也没开。第二天Mitzi Bauer问起，Emil只说人没来。

  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

  _effects: cash -2 (买磨刀石与补工具柄（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +apprenticeship_trial; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  印泥是赭红色的，像干了的面包瓤。Emil按完手印，工头把表举到窗边吹干，阳光里那张纸透出指印的轮廓。楼下第十区的街面传来运煤车的铁皮声，星期六下午突然显得很宽。

  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +birth_record_reconstructed; relation friend with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “a sworn statement can replace a damaged baptism entry”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  _effects: reputation -0.06; mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

  Emil在诊所门口从五点半站到十一点，膝盖发僵，终于用最后四十赫勒付了挂号费。门房撕下转诊单，叫Emil去综合医院排队，药费还得另算；队伍拐过街角时天还黑着，Emil数着前面二十三个后脑勺，记住了综合医院药房下午才开窗口。

  Emil在约瑟夫城租下一间带钢琴的会客室，每小时一克朗，女房东把钥匙拴在一截蓝线上。Josef Winter来时带着一束用报纸包好的紫罗兰，Grete Stein坐在窗边打毛线，偶尔抬头看一眼钟。第三个星期日，Grete Stein说自己要去楼下买咖啡，把门留了半掩。

- **1935.06** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/collect-the-benefit` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Emil让Grete Weiss冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Otto Kral寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Emil把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:546; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.06** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Emil刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Emil站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 52; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008474_

- **1935.11** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.fasching-dance/dance-first-polka` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  波尔卡重新响起时，Emil走到靠墙的长凳前向Luise Berg弯腰。姑母手里的扇子停了停，没说话。入场费和小费一共一克朗，Emil把找零塞回内袋；一支舞跳完，Luise Berg说了自己住在哪条街，Emil把名字和门牌在心里念了两遍。
  _effects: cash -1 (付给乐队的小费与舞会入场费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/still-hidden` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil把那段关系继续藏在日常的缝隙里。一次擦肩、一张没有署名的纸条、借来的半个钟头，都足够撑过这个月；回家前Emil在街角站了一会儿，把围巾重新系好。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 5 -> 0; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.reciprocity 50 -> 53; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Emil的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Emil收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Emil先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 42; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 62; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `r13-fam.apologize::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/forgiven` tags: task, social, apology, reputation, conflict, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil在院子的水龙头边等到Gustav Bauer，把那天酒桌上的话重复了一遍，没有找补。Gustav Bauer拧上水龙头，说那句话已经传到第三个院子了。Emil问要怎样才能收回，Gustav Bauer说收不回，但可以当着传话的人再说一遍是胡说的。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; flag +apology_accepted; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000524:child; knowledge “the insult has already reached three courtyards”_

- **1936.06** `hs18-paper.pulled-column::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/wrap-the-fish` tags: newspaper, censorship, reading, daily, rumor, history, vienna, urban
  Emil还是买下了报纸，回家用它包住一条鲱鱼。油墨沾在鱼皮上，连载的字迹倒着贴在盐粒里。晚饭时，隔壁的人问起今天报上写了什么，Emil说只记得价钱又涨了两格。
  _effects: food; cash -1 (bread and fish for dinner（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +wrapped_fish_in_newspaper_

- **1936.07** `r14-hist.arrest-door::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/taken-at-dawn` tags: political_arrest, repression, police, prison, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  五点刚过，Helene Huber带着两个人敲门，出示一张没有法官签名的搜查令。抽屉被倒扣在床上，书信按日期排好，装进一个印着鹰徽的纸袋。Elisabeth Berg披着外套站在楼梯口，Helene Huber让让开；Emil被允许穿上鞋，但没有系鞋带，走到楼下时袜子已经湿了。
  _effects: cash -3 (清晨五点的敲门声（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.08000000000000002; condition; flag +arrested_1934; role_

- **1936.12** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-borrow-from-neighbor` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  看门人正把垃圾桶往院角推，见你回来，眼神在你和门牌之间转了一圈。房东收下四克朗，在账本上划掉一行，没提换锁的事。你上楼时腿有点发软，楼梯扶手凉得扎手。
  _effects: cash 4 (向邻居借来补交欠租); relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1937.01** `bc18-ghost.double-tax::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/ghost-pays` tags: bureaucracy, tax, identity, paperwork, legal, absurd, money, vienna
  死人的档案里挂着一笔从未结清的税，Emil索性让那笔账继续欠着，自己用活人的名字去领煤票。两个窗口各自记账，互不打扰；Emil每月省下一克朗，却要绕两条街，避免同时经过两家税务所。
  _effects: cash -1 (绕路多花的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 42 -> 50; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 62 -> 74; flag +clean_living_tax_record; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  第二天，Grete Winter 2把外套送回来，说领口里缝着一张名字条，怕原主人家还要。Emil这才想起父亲总在衣服里侧写名字。Grete Winter 2把外套搭在椅背上，说改一改还能给孩子穿。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +donated_coat_returned; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1937.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Josef Winter 2攥着Emil的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/flees` tags: politics, persecution, 1938, migration
  Emil在夜里收拾了一只箱子，把证件缝进外套内衬，搭上往西的火车。Maria Bauer 2第二天来查人，只看到空床和没关严的窗。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging（其中20克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1938.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Emil和Otto Weiss在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Otto Weiss在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/seen-in-the-street` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil和那人在剧院后门分开时，被楼上的住户看见了。第二天，邻居的问话里多了一个不该出现的名字；Emil没有承认，只把外套挂到柜子最里面。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 0 -> 15; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 0 -> 5; reputation -0.03999999999999998_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:under-investigation/case-progress/evidence` tags: under-investigation, legal, police, investigation, risk, state, state_tick
  调查员又来找Emil谈了一次。问话比上次具体，记录也比上次长。

- **1938.01** `r14-soc.club-election::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/won-by-three-votes` tags: task, club_election, club, status, community, vienna, urban, crisis
  合唱团在酒馆后厅改选，票箱是一只装饼干的铁盒。唱票唱到第三遍，Emil比Elisabeth Hofer 2多三票。Elisabeth Hofer 2把账本推过来，说“你接”。散会后，两人在门口抽完同一支烟，谁也没有先提那三票。账本第一页记着上次义卖的收入，数字旁边有一道被擦过的铅笔痕。
  _effects: mood 0.12; reputation 0.08000000000000002; flag +won_club_election; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1938.01** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Hedwig问是谁动的。Hedwig说是Elisabeth Berg嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把通知念给Helene Berg听，Helene Berg摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Emil去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1938.11** `r1-cul.painting-commission::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/repainted-the-hand` tags: culture, painting, patronage, class, desire, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josefine Hofer看了三天，最后用指节敲了敲画布上那只手，说太像我的手，但不像我。Emil把右手重新上色，留了一道没干的铅白；Mitzi Moser在屏风后换衣服，衣架碰倒了一只瓷瓶。Josefine Hofer没有回头，只说下个月再来。
  _effects: cash 8 (肖像上的那只手); flag +commission_accepted_with_revisions; role; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the patron wants an idealized hand, not a likeness”_

- **1939.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sold-the-shop` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  房产经纪人只用了两天就找到买主，要把烟铺改成一家卖进口香皂的店面。Emil在契约上签了字，拿到十二克朗，比继承来的赊账多出不少。Berta Weiss和她的姐姐在月底搬去城郊的侄女家，临走前把猫留在空柜台下面；Emil没有回去看那只猫，只在账本最后一页写下“已结清”。
  _effects: cash 12 (卖掉烟铺的钱); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; reputation -0.04999999999999999; condition; flag +sold_the_tobacco_shop; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  在柜台前买了两只 Semmel，递过一只，自己咬另一只，旧账一个字没提。对方接过面包，两人并肩站在店门口，把那点热气吃完。钱的事还横在中间，但对方说星期六照旧来喝咖啡，别又躲着。
  _effects: cash -0.14 (两只 Semmel); food; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Friedrich Winter没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Emil听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); flag +funeral_society_left_

  Alois Lechner拿着联名信挨家敲门，说要让乡公所把草棚拆了。Emil在信上签了名，换来一句“早就该这样”。三天后，Elisabeth Hofer 2来收走了Emil Hofer的证件，说他们没有居留许可。Emil Hofer把工具一件件装上车，没有说话。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +signed_expulsion_petition; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation colleague with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the family’s residence papers were seized”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

  Grete Stein在门口拦住Emil，说这阵子别来了，名单上多一个名字就多一份麻烦。Emil把歌本还回去，走到街角又回头，看见Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的窗户用报纸糊了一半，里面还有人低声唱。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +kept_off_choir_roll_for_safety; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the choir is being watched”_

- **1940.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil没有立刻答话，先去敲Otto Bauer的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Otto Bauer提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Emil把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1940.09** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1940.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1940.12** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Emil把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Otto Weiss把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1940.12** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.12_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011584_

- **1940.12** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/hungry-winter` tags: war, homefront, shortage

  _effects: food; condition_

- **1941.01** `ld18-room.rented-hour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-key-is-refused` tags: love, room, rent, secret, queer, intimacy, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Mitzi Winter看见Josef Winter替Emil整理领口，忽然把钥匙收了回去，说这间房只租给正经夫妻。Emil站在门口，手里还提着刚买的煤；Josef Winter把围巾递给她，两个人沿着运河走了很久，谁也没有提下个星期。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 5 -> 15; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000214:child; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +room_key_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:forbidden-attachment/discretion-year/seen-in-the-street` tags: forbidden-attachment, love, intimacy, secret, courtship, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil和那人在剧院后门分开时，被楼上的住户看见了。第二天，邻居的问话里多了一个不该出现的名字；Emil没有承认，只把外套挂到柜子最里面。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 15 -> 30; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.jealousy 5 -> 10; reputation -0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: death (肺结核); flag -secret_attachment_active_

### 286 subject events

- **1912.09** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josefine Hofer把Lena抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josefine Hofer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04; health 0.010000000000000009 (a good day)_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-more` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Lena把空碗推回Josefine Hofer面前，清清楚楚地说了一个字：“还要。”Josefine Hofer愣住，随后把锅底最后一点糊刮进碗里。孩子学会了用一句话让世界再转一圈。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.03_

- **1914.04** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/happy-legend` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josefine Hofer讲起自己小时候在乡下追一只白鹅，追到河边，鹅没追到，倒捡回一篮鸭蛋。Lena笑得从凳子上滑下来，Josefine Hofer也跟着笑，说这个家不是只有欠账和葬礼。
  _effects: mood 0.04; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Hedwig在楼道里滚一只铁环，Lena站在门口看。Hedwig把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.049999999999999996_

- **1916.06** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/home-remedy` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Lena烧得翻来覆去，Josefine Hofer把醋倒进温水，用布蘸着擦手心、脚心，又去灶间煮接骨木汁。勺子送到嘴边，Lena偏头躲，Josefine Hofer就等一会儿再喂。鸡叫时炉火压小，床脚用旧大衣堵住。两天后热度退了，嘴唇起了一层干皮。
  _effects: health 0.030000000000000027 (土法退烧，热度渐退); mood -0.06_

- **1918.01** `vienna-life-school-enrolment/kept-home` tags: childhood, school, fees
  Josefine Hofer在报名处门口站了一会儿，最后把Lena带回家。家里需要有人看小妹妹，学校可以明年再去。Grete Stein在名册上把名字划掉了。
  _effects: education home; role_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-refused-nuggets` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  Lena空手站在窗口，听掌柜说“叫你Josefine Hofer来”，耳朵一直红到脖子。后墙的渣堆冻得硬，Lena用鞋尖刨开表层，把煤核一颗颗捡进围裙。回家路上碰到同巷的孩子，Lena把纸袋往身后藏，进门才交给Josefine Hofer。
  _effects: mood -0.04; food; skill_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-homemade-wood-ash` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  湿煤灰写出第一笔时，Lena还挺高兴，写到第三行，手一蹭就全花了。Josefine Hofer看见了，把自己的旧手帕撕下一角，蘸水给木板擦了底。第二天在Leopoldstädter Volksschule门口，同学指着那块发黑的木板问是什么，Lena说是石板，对方笑得弯下腰。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.04; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

  木窗“咔”地合上，Lena的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Lena在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

  Lena放学后替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的排字工送校样，穿过两条街，拿到一克朗。回家时天已经黑了，Josefine Hofer把饭留在锅里；Lena的鞋底磨薄，脚后跟起了泡，却把那一克朗捏得平平整整，放进Josefine Hofer的围裙口袋；这跑腿的差事从此固定下来，Lena也算家里一个能挣钱的孩子。
  _effects: cash 1 (running errands); role_

  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Rosa Moser；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Franz Moser把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:510_

- **1934.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/second-bridge-promise` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  在第二个桥洞下，Franz Moser先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Lena点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1934.05** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/keep-the-list` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Lena把第二区夜班出租合作社的名单藏进缝纫机抽屉的夹层。名单上有二十几个名字，其中两个已经搬走，一个被工厂开除。Lena没有把最后一行划掉。
  _effects: flag +movement_list_holder; reputation 0.03_

- **1934.11** `ad20-former-employer::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-door-is-open` tags: work, employer, return, everyday, stigma, public, vienna, urban
  圣马克斯殡葬合作社在门口认出Lena，先问家里怎么样，又问这些年在哪。Lena说换了几份工；圣马克斯殡葬合作社点点头，说“有空来坐”。两个人都知道这不是一句工作邀请，但谁也没有把话挑明。
  _effects: relation employer with person-berta-weiss; mood 0.02; flag +old_employer_cordial; cash 1 (a small gift from the old employer)_

- **1935.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-teaching` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Lena的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Lena被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1935.04** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-shared-crates` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  箱子棱角硌着肋骨的滋味到傍晚还没消。工头把工钱数给Lena时少了一半，Mitzi Winter凑过来把扁担一横，说名单上少写一个，钱可不能少算一个。
  _effects: cash 2 (争执后补到的搬运工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1935.06** `ad20-wedding::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-band-plays-twice` tags: wedding, family, celebration, everyday, public, gossip, vienna, urban
  乐队把同一支曲子奏了两遍，第二遍时Helene Berg家的老父亲已经睡着了。Lena被拉去跳舞，踩了舞伴两次脚；第三次时，两个人干脆不跳了，站在桌边看别人转圈。Lena笑得直不起腰，忘了自己本来不想来。
  _effects: mood 0.05; cash -1 (an extra coin for the band); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; flag +wedding_band_twice; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1935.12** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/queue-until-noon` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Lena在诊所门口从五点半站到十一点，膝盖发僵，终于用最后四十赫勒付了挂号费。门房撕下转诊单，叫Lena去综合医院排队，药费还得另算；队伍拐过街角时天还黑着，Lena数着前面二十三个后脑勺，记住了综合医院药房下午才开窗口。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1936.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Grete Moser。接生的人收六克朗，Franz Moser在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Lena起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:547_

- **1936.01** `r13-civ.arrange-move::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/trunk-withheld` tags: social, housing, moving, landlord, cash, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena把最后一箱书搬下楼时，Ferdinand Moser堵在门口，说墙皮被蹭掉一块，箱子先留下。Lena说要赶车，Ferdinand Moser说那就把修墙的钱付了。板车在巷口按铃，Lena只抱走了装证件的木盒。
  _effects: cash -2 (板车与押金收据（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +landlord_withheld_trunk; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; knowledge “the landlord keeps the trunk against a wall repair claim”_

- **1936.04** `ad20-stranger-request::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/take-the-envelope` tags: stranger, envelope, request, everyday, obligation, risk, vienna, urban
  Helene Stein在巷口拦住Lena，把一个没有字的信封塞过来，说“明天中午之前，别打开”。Lena问里面是什么；Helene Stein说“你不需要知道”。信封很薄，捏起来却像有一块硬东西。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:592; cash 2 (payment for carrying the envelope); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000711:child; flag +envelope_taken; mood -0.009999999999999998; knowledge “信封很薄，里面像有一块硬东西。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1936.06** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Helene Berg用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Lena才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; flag +parish_registered_

- **1936.12** `hs18-queue.figure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pretend-not-to-see` tags: historical_figure, queue, recognition, bread, witness, history, vienna, urban
  Lena把头转向橱窗，假装在看一双标价离谱的鞋。Maria Bauer 2从队伍旁走过，靴跟敲得很慢。Sigmund Freud往前挪了一步，帽檐仍压着；两人之间隔着三个人和一整条街的沉默。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:591; knowledge “保持沉默也需要记住每一个细节。”; flag +ignored_historical_figure_in_queue; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Luise Moser。接生的人收六克朗，Franz Moser在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Lena起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中3.6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:590_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena被叫到学校，老师说Grete Moser这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Lena没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.03_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Lena忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Lena听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 75 -> 67; mood -0.019999999999999997_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:ad20-private-bargain/the-favor-ages/called-in` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  对方在一个不合时宜的时刻提起旧事，语气很客气，意思却不客气。Lena听见自己答应得太快，挂断以后才发现手心有一层薄汗。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 55 -> 63; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 45 -> 49; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 35 -> 31; mood -0.03_

- **1937.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Lena替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Lena自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04000000000000001; mood -0.03_

- **1937.11** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.factory-gate/gate-queue-clash` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, factory, hiring
  招工纸上的工钱写得明白，一天三克朗八十赫勒。Lena怕再等下去名额没了，从人缝里挤到前面，后面的Grete Winter 2一把拽住袖子。门房抬头看了看乱糟糟的队伍，把玻璃窗拉上了。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.03_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Lena在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Elisabeth Hofer 2记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Lena带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1938.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Emil Moser。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Franz Moser把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Lena躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:635_

  Lena指向中间那个很少出门的会计，说他那晚从墙边走过。Maria Bauer 2没有核实，只让人把会计带走。Friedrich Winter低下头，肩膀松了一点；Lena回家后才发现，自己的手一直在抖。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 20 -> 45; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 67 -> 57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; reputation -0.03; flag +falsely_identified_man; mood -0.06_

  Lena被叫到学校，老师说Luise Moser这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Lena没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.03_

- **1938.01** `kernel-migrate.refugee-aid::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/civic-refugee-error` tags: migration, refugee, missing_relative, care, postwar, vienna, urban, crisis
  她在一张错误名单上找到熟悉的名字，跑去找Berta Stein确认。对方看了很久，说“不是他，是我哥哥”；她把纸折好，没有把那个名字划掉。她把那张纸折好，决定明天再去名单墙前问一次。
  _effects: flag +refugee_list_error; knowledge “名单上的名字可能属于同姓的另一个人，划掉前要当面确认”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; cash -1 (跑去确认的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.009999999999999995_

- **1938.04** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Emil Moser这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010426_

  Katharina Stein 2把汇款单退回来，说收款村的地址在新区划里改了名，钱只能退回。Lena又填了一张，把旧村名和新村名并排写上。Katharina Stein 2说这样不行，系统只认一个名字。汇款单在柜台上放了一下午，最后被盖上“无法投递”。
  _effects: cash -1 (汇款单背面的三行字（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +remittance_address_failed; knowledge “the post office accepts only one official village name”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

  停电后的第三个小时，Franz Bauer把收音机搬到楼道，电池快没电了。女播音员说“请市民保持镇静”，随后是一段军乐。Lena听见有人开始收拾行李；楼下的铁门被风撞了三次，像有人在敲。
  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:685; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +heard_radio_rumor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1939.05** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Lena去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为圣马克斯殡葬合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1939.09** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.bruised-apples` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  她蹲在菜市场湿漉漉的石板地上挑苹果，Klara从旁边经过，说这种碰伤的煮出来最甜。她回家把苹果切成块，锅里水刚开就下了糖精片，满屋子都是煮水果的酸香。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (买收摊前的碰伤苹果（其中0.1克朗记入债务）); food_

- **1940.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Anton Moser。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Franz Moser把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Lena躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中3.2克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:717_

- **1940.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/favor-refused` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Grete Lechner把一篮鸡蛋放在Lena家门口，说只要一个不用打卡的位子。Lena把鸡蛋拎回对方手里，说编制表不是自家的抽屉。两个人站在楼梯间，谁也没有再提这件事；第二天见面，还是点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; reputation 0.03; flag +refused_private_favor; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

  _effects: cash 1 (a day's mortuary pay); mood -0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; condition; flag +refused_mortuary; knowledge “那具孩子的棺材只比鞋盒大一点，{master}没有劝我留下。”_

  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +rival_audited; knowledge “委员会的差旅账目里有两次重复报销。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Lena把Grete Moser的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Grete Moser今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。

- **1941.05** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.notary-inheritance/leave-unsigned` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena把文件推回Helene Huber面前，说不签，等Josefine Hofer自己改主意。Josefine Hofer的嘴角动了动，Hedwig把三把钥匙一把把收进衣袋，金属碰着金属响了几声。Lena走下公证处的石阶，街对面面包房正在出炉，热气扑到脸上，口袋里一个赫勒也没多，房契的事仍旧悬着。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; flag +inheritance_unsigned_

- **1941.05** `r2-law.rent-contract::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signed-with-repair-clause` tags: law, rent, contract, housing, tenement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Gustav Stein 2把租约摊在桌上，第十三条写着“小修由房客自理”，旁边一栏是六克朗的钥匙押金。Lena问屋顶漏雨算不算小修，Gustav Stein 2说看漏多大。Helene Stein在旁边插嘴，说去年冬天水从三楼一直滴到院子里；Gustav Stein 2把笔递过来，说签了才给钥匙。Lena数出六克朗，在第十三条下面签了名。
  _effects: cash -6 (租约上的第十三条); flag +signed_lease_with_repair_clause; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; knowledge “clause thirteen makes the tenant pay for minor repairs”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1941.12** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home
  Lena托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Josefine Hofer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Lena晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Josefine Hofer的腿伤有人料理，Lena却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用); health 0.08 (看护妇料理后伤势好转)_

  Lena的读书会坚持了两个月，Anton Kral找到正式工作后再没来过。Lena一个人坐在阁楼里，把两个人的笔记对了一遍，发现自己漏掉的内容比记住的还多，就把Anton Kral那半本也抄了下来。抄完以后，Lena把两本笔记都锁进箱子。锁扣坏了。
  _effects: skill_

- **1942.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Lena攥紧扶手。Helene Berg站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛)_

- **1942.07** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/marked-deck` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  Lena赢了三克朗，起身时才发现牌背有指甲划出的记号。Josef Berg 2笑着把牌收进袖子里，说这叫“借来的手气”。Lena没有把钱退回去，只把那张有记号的牌偷偷带走。第二天，Lena在当铺门口看见Josef Berg 2正在和一个收旧货的人分钱。
  _effects: cash 3 (牌桌上赢来的三克朗); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:827; flag +df18_marked_deck; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

  Lena对Ferdinand Kral 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Ferdinand Kral 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Lena的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1943.01** `hs18-blackout.cellar::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/trade-for-shelter` tags: blackout, cellar, coal, neighbor, denunciation, history, vienna, urban
  Lena没有告发，也没有白帮忙。Helene Huber把两桶煤分出一桶，换Lena在搜查时把楼上的孩子领到后院。成交时两人都没有提“煤”字，只说“今晚借个火”。

  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 40; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 54; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; reputation -0.03_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:df18-borrowed-luck/luck-account/price-arrives` tags: df18-borrowed-luck, luck, debt, obligation, superstition, risk, bargain, transfer
  第六个月，债主在楼梯口拦住Lena，说利息不能总按原来的算法。Lena看着对方手里的账本，忽然发现那上面的墨迹比上次新，数字也比上次多。
  _effects: cash -2 (利息被重新计算（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 15 -> 21; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 15 -> 10_

- **1943.04** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Helene Kral合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1943.12** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home

- **1944.01** `state_reaction:hs18-street-witness/interrogation-pressure` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  又有人来问那天的事。Lena把同一段话重说了一遍，对方却只记住其中两个名字；走的时候，门没有关严，楼道里的风把纸吹得直响。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.danger 45 -> 55_

- **1944.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/balanced` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  Lena用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Maria Bauer 2发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.04; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +balanced_the_books; knowledge “停掉两份简报、合并零碎支出后，年底还剩一笔够换档案柜的钱。”_

  _effects: cash -1 (还掉一小笔（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 21 -> 13; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 10 -> 13; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.07** `r2-law.smuggling-cigarettes::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/caught-at-the-gate` tags: crime, smuggling, customs, risk, stations, vienna, urban, crisis
  Otto Kral没有捂鼻子，反而用撬棍敲了敲棺材底板。声音太实，他又敲了一下；Helene 2转身就跑，Lena被留在原地。棺材打开时，卷烟码得整整齐齐，白布下面连个人形都没有。Otto Kral把Lena的名字写在封条背面。
  _effects: cash -10 (棺材夹层里的卷烟); condition; flag +arrested_with_contraband_coffin; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002685:child; knowledge “the porter ran and left the subject at the checkpoint”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1944.11** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Katharina Hofer 2，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Katharina Hofer 2点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

  苏联士兵进城时，Lena躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Lena去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

  _effects: historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3-evt:world-vienna-1912:006677-1936 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000006_

- **1945.07** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/not-king-material` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, vienna, urban
  Katharina Lechner 2看了Lena的手，说这双手适合拿账本，不适合拿权杖。Lena回家路上买了一张最便宜的站票，在最后一排看完了整场戏；散场时，Lena记住的不是国王的台词，而是他坐下时椅子发出的声音。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.040000000000000036; cash -1 (买了一张站票); flag +cast_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108_

- **1945.08** `ad20-neighbor-dispute::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-line-moves` tags: neighbor, dispute, home, everyday, public, obligation, vienna, urban
  隔壁把晾衣绳往Lena这边挪了一尺，水滴正好落在窗台上。Lena拿粉笔在墙上画了一道线；Grete Weiss看见了，又把绳子挪回去半尺。两个人都觉得自己让了步，晚饭时却都把窗户关得很紧。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +laundry_line_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1946.02** `ml18-luck.strongbox-in-the-wall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-inflation-ate-them` tags: money, luck, housing, windfall, inflation, ownership, vienna, urban
  铁盒里的纸币已经作废，银扣也只值几个铜板。Alois Hofer 2把纸币一张张摊在窗台上，说战前的纸现在只能糊墙；Anna Winter 3笑得直不起腰，说这盒子在他家墙里藏了二十年，原来是一堆废纸。Lena把当票收好，去当铺换回一只银勺，勺柄上刻着外祖母的姓。
  _effects: cash 2 (the silver spoon redeemed with the old pawn ticket); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; flag +worthless_strongbox; knowledge “墙里的旧纸币已经作废，当票还能换回一只银勺。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.05** `r1-cul.tourism-trip::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/overheard-innkeeper` tags: culture, travel, leisure, companionship, weather, vienna, urban, postwar
  Lena半夜被隔壁的争吵吵醒，Elisabeth Berg 2也醒着。两人披着外套坐在楼梯口，听见旅馆老板在跟一个男人算账。Emil Weiss 2后来端来两杯热酒，什么也没解释；Elisabeth Berg 2说，这趟旅行至少带回一个故事。
  _effects: cash -2 (山间旅馆的钥匙); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +overheard_innkeeper_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; knowledge “the innkeeper owes money to a man from the valley”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130_

- **1946.12** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Franz Moser却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Lena把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; flag +still_looking_for_room_

  Lena摸了摸空口袋，说那不过是块旧铜，丢了就丢了。晚上，Berta Kral睡着以后，Lena又去电车站走了一趟，沿着白天的路线低头看每一块石板。

  第二区夜班出租合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。

- **1948.11** `ld18-performance.tango::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-impresario-offers-a-contract` tags: performance, desire, dance, public, queer, nightlife, public_desire, vienna
  Katharina Stein 3把合同推过来，说只要她们每星期跳三场，就付双倍的钱。Lena问要不要改舞步，Katharina Stein 3说不用，越像现在这样越好。Katharina Winter看着合同上的数字，先笑了，又有点想哭。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:1088; cash 2 (the first week of the scandal contract); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +scandal_contract; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1948.12** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-partner-bought-it-secretly` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  Franz Moser在开奖前偷偷去了烟草铺，用买菜剩下的钱买了那组号码，把票藏在糖罐后面。号码中了，Franz Moser把票放在餐桌上，说本来想等Lena生日再讲。Lena先是不信，随后笑得把椅子往后推了一截；两个人坐在厨房里数奖金，数到一半又停下来烧水。
  _effects: cash 70 (the lottery prize bought with the grocery money); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1089; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; reputation 0.029999999999999985; flag +partner_bought_the_ticket; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Lena的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Lena家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016684_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/a-receipt-in-the-lining` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  半年过去，Lena又翻了一遍那只装旧纽扣的铁盒，在盒盖夹层里摸到一小片发黄的收据。上面的日期和那件东西最后一次出现的那天对得上，但字迹已经淡得只剩半个印章。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 8 -> 14; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 8 -> 6; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Lena，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Lena没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 50 -> 58; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 10 -> 14; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Lena把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Lena来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena把Grete Moser的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Grete Moser今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 32 -> 40; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 38 -> 35; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena把Emil Moser的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Emil Moser今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 49 -> 57; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 61 -> 58; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Lena把Anton Moser的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Anton Moser今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 38 -> 46; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 64 -> 61; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-wedding-invitation` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  请柬上写着Lena的名字，旁边用铅笔注了一句“一个人来也可以”。婚礼上，新娘把手捧花直接递给她，笑着说“下一个该你了”；同桌的人都在等Lena怎么回答。她把花放在膝上，说这花很香。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 65 -> 73; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 14 -> 19; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017038_

- **1949.01** `r13-fam.write-missing-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/letter-sent` tags: task, family, letter, missing, war, literacy, vienna, urban
  Lena替Luise Bauer 2写信，把最后知道的部队番号、收容站和一处旧住址都写上去。Luise Bauer 2不识字，却要Lena把“家里都好”念三遍，怕写错。信投进邮筒时，她伸手在筒口摸了一下；Lena把回信地址又描粗了一笔。
  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); flag +wrote_to_missing_relative; skill; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138; knowledge “A letter with the last known unit and shelter addresses was ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

- **1949.05** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/leave-the-table` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  Lena把牌一推，说今晚不借也不玩。Anna Hofer耸耸肩，把桌上的钱收回口袋。Lena走到街上，口袋里的硬币只够买一杯咖啡；但经过当铺时，Lena没有停。第二天上工，Lena的手指还在发痒，像少做了一件本该做的事。
  _effects: cash -1 (一杯咖啡（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +df18_refused_card_money; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “我拒绝了牌桌上的钱，但手指还记得那种感觉。”_

- **1949.07** `r13-rel.introduction-friend::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/talked-until-the-stove-died` tags: relationship, introduction, friend, acquaintance, social, vienna, urban, postwar
  Anton Kral把Maria Huber领到桌边，说这是自己厂里最能熬夜的人。Lena给Maria Huber倒了半杯咖啡，Maria Huber先问Lena上哪个班。三人聊到炉子熄了，Anton Kral借口去买烟，把位置留给两个人。
  _effects: role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001294:child_

- **1949.08** `state_reaction:raising-child/child-death-in-the-household` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  孩子的名字从学校的名册上被划掉以后，家里的碗还多摆了一副。Lena没有把它收起来，直到有一天发现碗沿积了灰，才拿去洗。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 25; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 59 -> 74; mood -0.09999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017158_

- **1949.11** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Hermann Moser这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 35 -> 38; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017482_

- **1949.12** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Elisabeth Moser这周主动擦了黑板。Lena把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 33 -> 36; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Lena一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Elisabeth Berg 2送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-130_

- **1950.01** `ml18-charity.neighborhood-clinic::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-committee-buys-a-wagon` tags: charity, money, clinic, reputation, community, warm, vienna, urban
  捐款交出去三个月，诊所还没有开门，先来了一辆新的弹簧马车和一张桃花心木办公桌。Franz Moser把账本摊在桌上，说委员会认为“体面的门面”也是诊所的一部分。Maria Bauer在门口骂了一句，Lena把账本翻到最后一页，发现自己捐的钱只剩一半。
  _effects: cash -30 (the clinic endowment was spent on furniture and a wagon（其中30克朗记入债务）); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 60 -> 30; reputation -0.05; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; condition; flag +clinic_funds_misused; knowledge “诊所的捐款先买了一辆弹簧马车和一张桃花心木办公桌。”; mood -0.06000000000000005_

- **1950.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fever-breaks` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  Lena把湿布拧到不再滴水，敷在Hermann Moser的额头上。孩子烧得说胡话，Lena就一遍遍念窗外的街名，直到天快亮时，孩子的手心终于出了一层汗。Lena把被子掖好，自己靠在床沿睡了过去，醒来时锅里还剩半碗冷粥。
  _effects: cash -2 (第三遍换湿布); condition; flag +nursed_child_fever; health 0.05999999999999999 (the fever broke after a night of cold compresses)_

- **1950.08** `vienna-social-movement.aftermath/keep-the-list` tags: development, social_movement, organization, consequence
  Lena把圣马克斯殡葬合作社的名单藏进缝纫机抽屉的夹层。名单上有二十几个名字，其中两个已经搬走，一个被工厂开除。Lena没有把最后一行划掉。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

- **1951.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Lena和Rosa合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; household; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1951.01** `pc20-family.parent-mistake::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cover` tags: parent, child, mistake, second_chance, family, school, repetition, intervention
  Lena替Katharina Winter向Helene Bauer请了病假，又把那件可能惹麻烦的东西藏进自己的工具箱。Helene Bauer看了Lena一眼，说病假可以补，别的东西补不了；Katharina Winter在里屋听见，门没有关严。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:1207; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005542:child; flag +covered_child_mistake; knowledge “病假可以补，别的东西补不了。”; flag +second_chance_open_

- **1951.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/cupboard-again` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Lena刚提一句“那年分东西”，Hedwig就把勺子放下，说柜子是他先看中的，凭什么让。话越说越硬，汤还剩半碗，Hedwig起身拿了帽子，椅子在石地上刮出一声。Lena一个人坐到教堂的钟敲完，把两份饭钱都付了。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (付了两份饭钱); mood -0.06000000000000005_

- **1951.05** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Katharina Winter 2排在前面，回头替Lena看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Lena用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-151; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-151_

- **1951.07** `ad20-neighbor-dispute::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-line-moves` tags: neighbor, dispute, home, everyday, public, obligation, vienna, urban
  隔壁把晾衣绳往Lena这边挪了一尺，水滴正好落在窗台上。Lena拿粉笔在墙上画了一道线；Alois Huber看见了，又把绳子挪回去半尺。两个人都觉得自己让了步，晚饭时却都把窗户关得很紧。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

- **1951.10** `r13-rel.separation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/moved-to-shared-housing` tags: relationship, separation, household, conflict, marriage, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa把衣服收进箱子，锅碗分了一半。Lena没有拦，只把门后的雨伞递过去，说这几天有雨。Rosa走后，Lena把两个人的合照翻过来扣在柜子上，第二天照常去上工。
  _effects: relation partner with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; household_

- **1952.01** `ad20-child-teacher::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-child-says-it-first` tags: family, child, school, teacher, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Emil Moser先开口，说老师问家里是不是有客人。Lena问怎么答的；Emil Moser说“我说有，但客人睡在厨房”。Lena笑了一下，又觉得不该笑。那天晚上，Emil Moser把厨房的门帘换成了新的。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 32 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +child_spoke_to_teacher_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

  Lena算了算家里的粮和煤，说实在住不下。教父Elisabeth Kral在车站的长椅上睡了两夜，后来被巡夜的人带去收容所；Lena听说后，托人送去一床旧毯子，没有留名字。收容所的回执上只写了姓名和日期。

## Anton

Born 1912.01, died 1929.01 (age 17).
Education: apprenticeship 1; occupation: none; roles: infant, child, pupil, child_worker, caretaker, first_aid_certified.
