### 51 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/colic-night` tags: infancy, family, first, warm, injury
  夜里Anton哭了很久，Josefine Hofer抱着孩子在屋里来回走，数着地板缝。天快亮时孩子终于打了个嗝，安静下来；Josefine Hofer坐在床沿，发现自己还保持着走路的姿势。
  _effects: mood -0.03; health -0.010000000000000009 (lost sleep)_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-nonsense` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Anton说了一长串谁也听不懂的话，最后用力点头，像是在宣布一件已经决定的事。Josefine Hofer认真听完，也点了点头；孩子满意地爬走了，留下大人们互相看着，忍不住笑。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1913.11** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/a-word-for-bread` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Anton指着桌上的面包说出一个短词，声音轻得像在试音。Josefine Hofer把面包掰下一角，泡进热水里喂给孩子；Anton吃完又指了一次，这次说得更清楚。厨房里的人都笑了，连灶上的水汽也像在往上冒。
  _effects: food; mood 0.03; flag +first_word_bread_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josefine Hofer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Anton问还回不回来，Josefine Hofer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Hedwig在楼道里滚一只铁环，Anton站在门口看。Hedwig把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood 0.05_

- **1918.01** `vienna-life-school-enrolment/half-day-place` tags: childhood, school, fees
  学校只收半日班，Grete Stein说下午还有空位，但课本要家里自己买。Josefine Hofer先交了一克朗，把Anton的名字写在候补名单上。
  _effects: education primary; cash -1 (partial school fee（其中1克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Anton前面的大婶一直往后靠，Anton就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Anton的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill_

- **1918.11** `r1-child.bully-teacher-son::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/fought-in-the-snow` tags: bullying, school, teacher, class, fear, university, culture, schooling
  Anton在放学路上把教师儿子推倒在雪里，两个人滚成一团。Grete Stein赶来时，先扶起自己的儿子，再问Anton为什么打人。Anton说他先切我的橡皮。Grete Stein说一块橡皮也值得动手？那天Anton在走廊站到天黑。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; flag +fought_teachers_son; relation rival with person-grete-stein_

- **1918.12** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  土豆从围裙里滚出来，Anton蹲下去追，手心一松，三枚十克鲁泽的铜板叮叮当当掉在石板路上。两枚捡回来了，最后一枚滚进排水格栅，在铁条下转了个圈。Anton趴下去够，指甲刮在铁条上。回家Josefine Hofer数完钱，脸色沉下来，Anton站在桌边不敢动。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; mood -0.06_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/hungry-in-class` tags: childhood, school, hunger
  Anton早上只喝了一碗清汤，第二节课时眼前发黑。Otto Moser让孩子去走廊坐一会儿，给了半块面包；Anton把面包屑拢在手心吃完，胃里还是空的，但看Otto Moser的眼神软了些，回到座位时把腰挺直了一点。
  _effects: condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-early-morning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  Anton把错题纸举到讲台边，话说到一半就低下去了。Otto Moser放下红笔，说“明早提前来”。第二天Anton摸黑走到Leopoldstädter Volksschule，教室门已经开了，炉子冒着青烟。Otto Moser把竖式写在黑板上，先擦掉一半，让Anton补上，再一笔一笔讲为什么。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Anna Moser从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Anton。Anton把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill_

- **1920.02** `r14-edu.humiliation::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/mocked-for-patches` tags: education, school_humiliation, reputation, childhood, university, culture, schooling, school
  Helene Bauer让Anton站到黑板前，指着袖口的补丁说，这样的手也配拿钢笔。教室里有人笑，Maria Stein没有笑，低头翻书。Anton站到下课，回到座位时，发现课本被人用粉笔写了两个字。Anton用袖子擦，越擦越白。
  _effects: mood -0.18; reputation -0.12000000000000001; flag +humiliated_in_class; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/runs-errands` tags: childhood, labour, piecework
  Anton放学后替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的排字工送校样，穿过两条街，拿到一克朗。回家时天已经黑了，Josefine Hofer把饭留在锅里；Anton的鞋底磨薄，脚后跟起了泡，却把那一克朗捏得平平整整，放进Josefine Hofer的围裙口袋；这跑腿的差事从此固定下来，Anton也算家里一个能挣钱的孩子。
  _effects: cash 1 (running errands); role_

- **1921.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/attends-through-frost` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Anton踩着结冰的台阶进教室，第三个空位还空着。Helene Bauer让Anton把昨天的课文背完，又用戒尺敲了敲黑板，叫全班跟着念。放学时名字被写在红榜末尾，Anton把冻僵的手指揣进袖口，觉得明天还能来。放学路上，Anton把冻裂的手贴在热煤渣上。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +school_attendance_streak; skill_

- **1921.02** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Anton跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Anna Moser从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

- **1921.10** `r1-child.library-card::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school::reading-room-culture/confiscated-in-class` tags: library, reading, books, poverty, school, university, culture, schooling
  Anton把书藏在课桌里，被Helene Bauer没收。Helene Bauer翻了两页，发现是讲蒸汽机的，放学后把书还回来，说下次别在课上读。Anton问能不能借老师的书，Helene Bauer说先把这本读完。
  _effects: flag +teacher_lent_book; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; knowledge “the teacher keeps a shelf of engineering books”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1921.10** `vienna-school-childhood.fountain-pen/fountain-pen.broken` tags: school, social, childhood
  “钢笔要钱，钱在账单上。”Josefine Hofer说完把纸折起来。Anton的自制笔漏了一页墨，Helene Bauer用红笔写“重写”，本子边角被墨水泡皱。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; skill_

- **1921.11** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Anton在洗衣房的长凳上睡着，醒来时喉咙像塞了棉花。Josefine Hofer把最后的干净布巾浸了凉水，敷在Anton额头上，整夜换了七八回。第四天Anton能坐起来喝粥，下嘴唇脱下一小片皮，Josefine Hofer用指尖替Anton拈掉。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-list-instead` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  锁门的人在走廊里咳嗽了一声。Helene Bauer一边道歉一边收拾，最后从粉笔盒底下抽出一张旧练习纸，圈了四道题。Anton接过来，想问第二题怎么开头，Helene Bauer已经提着包下楼了。校门口还有太阳，Anton把纸摊在台阶上看了一会儿。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1922.04** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店门口的石阶上摆着一排烟画。Anton把三张皱巴巴的卡摊开，Hedwig挑了挑，从口袋里摸出一张皇帝像。交换完，Hedwig转身进了杂货铺，把皇帝像往柜台上一拍，换了一块糖。Anton站在门外，手里还留着卡片的油墨味。
  _effects: mood 0.03999999999999998; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912_

- **1922.06** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/took-punishment` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  皮带落在掌心时，Anton的手缩了一下，又伸回去。挨完几下，Anton去墙角拿扫帚，把碎碗片扫进簸箕，瓷片碰着铁皮响。Josefine Hofer把皮带挂回门后，坐在桌边点了半支烟，烟灰积了很长也没弹。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1922.10** `vienna-childhood-friends.secret-kiosk/traded-for-picture` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  “就一口。”Anton把糖块掰开，换了一张火车头。Josefine Hofer在报亭窗口看着，等糖纸落地，才走出来。画片被扣在柜台上，扫帚递到Anton手里。Anton扫完半条后巷，Josefine Hofer用围裙擦了擦画片，还回来时角上多了一道折痕。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Anna Moser从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Anton。Anton把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill_

- **1923.09** `vienna-youth-threshold.school-leaving/print-shop-trial` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Helene Bauer把离校证明折好塞进Anton手里，说西梅林区那家印刷厂正缺跑腿。Anton当天下午就沿墙根走到厂门口，油墨和湿纸的味道从门缝里钻出来。工头记下名字，让下周一再来试工，先不算正式雇用，也不给工钱。
  _effects: flag +seeking_work; skill; mood 0.02999999999999997_

- **1924.01** `kernel-edu.prepare-for-exam::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/edu-prepare-failed` tags: task, adolescence, school, exam, study, university, culture, schooling
  Anton把课本翻了两遍，却总在同一个公式上卡住。考试时，那道题占二十分，Anton空着交卷。Hermann Bauer发回卷子，用红笔在空白处画了一个圈，没有写评语。Anton把卷子折好，塞进课本最后一页。
  _effects: reputation -0.04000000000000001; mood -0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; flag +failed_exam; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1924.03** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne捏了捏Anton的虎口，又让Anton张开手指，说骨节还没长开，推硬木会伤手。Josefine Hofer想再问一句，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne已经转身去搬木料。Anton退到门槛外，听见刨子重新响起来，木花从门缝里卷到脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05000000000000002; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1924.05** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/kept-home-for-kin` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Josefine Hofer病倒在床，Anton留在家里看弟妹、煮稀粥、去井边打水。Grete Stein托人带来一包旧课本，Anton在灶火边翻了两页，弟弟哭了，只好合上。一个月后，学校把Anton的名字从名册上划掉，墨迹很淡。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +left_school_for_family; role_

- **1925.01** `kernel-edu.appeal-exam-result::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::edu-appeal-reform/edu-appeal-reform-transfer` tags: task, adolescence, school, appeal, official, reform, medical, schooling
  委员会维持原议，但建议Anton转到新办的市立中学，那里不看拉丁文。Ferdinand Winter给了一张转学单，Grete Stein在背面写了地址。Anton把单子折好，第二天就去新学校问了名额。新学校的教室比原来的亮，Anton却有点不习惯。
  _effects: education secondary; flag +transferred_to_municipal_school; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1925.03** `kernel-edu.friendship::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/edu-friend-forbidden` tags: adolescence, school, friendship, books, family, university, culture, schooling
  Josefine Hofer说Emil家里没有正经职业，不许再来往。Anton嘴上答应，放学还是绕路去Emil家。被发现后，Josefine Hofer把借书证撕了。Emil在门口等了两天，第三天没有再来。Anton把撕碎的借书证捡起来，夹进书里。
  _effects: relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; flag +forbidden_friendship; mood -0.06999999999999995_

- **1925.06** `r1-child.cuff-answers::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/formulas-in-rice-paste` tags: exam, cheating, school, shame, numeracy, university, culture, schooling
  考试前夜，Anton把公式抄在衬衫袖口内侧，用米汤固定。第二天出汗，字迹晕开，像一排灰色的蚂蚁。Grete Stein走过时停了一下，Anton把袖子往下拉，心跳得能听见。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +cheated_on_exam; skill_

- **1925.11** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stay-behind-the-curtain` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  Anton把浴巾裹到下巴，说自己怕水。Mitzi在门外笑，说去年还见Anton从木塔上跳下去；Anton没有解释，只隔着板缝看Rosa把水撩到肩上。回家时，Anton的泳衣还是干的，衣角被手攥得起了皱。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:160; flag +yf18_stayed_dry; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.reputation-repair::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/left-school-for-shop` tags: education, school_humiliation, reputation, repair, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Anton没有再回学校。Josefine Hofer托人在布店找了个位置，Anton每天量布、记尺码、给客人包纸。月底拿到工钱，Anton给自己买了一件没有补丁的衬衫，穿回家时，母亲看了很久，什么也没问。布店月钱两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (旧校服上的墨迹); mood -0.10000000000000003; flag +left_school_after_humiliation_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001593_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/factory-hand` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把Anton分到装订间，每天站十个小时，工钱按件算。第一周结束，Anton的指关节肿了，工头说习惯就好。
  _effects: employment Buchbinderhilfe @ org-winter-print; condition_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Rosa。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Grete Lechner把一条绷带绕在Anton的小臂上，教怎么打结才不会勒住血管。Anton在同桌的手臂上练了三遍，最后一遍得到了点头。课程结束时，Grete Lechner发了一张油印证书，上面盖着红十字的章。
  _effects: flag +earned_first_aid_certificate; role; skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

  Elisabeth Bauer 3把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Anton看期限、工钱和出师考试。Anton签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:199; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1926.09** `r3-crime.juvenile-court::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::court-without-bars/mother-fined` tags: crime, youth, court, family, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Berg 2判Josefine Hofer监管不力，罚了六克朗，从下个月的救济金里扣。Josefine Hofer在法庭上数了三遍口袋，最后把戒指摘下来放在桌上。Anton Berg 2看了一眼，说“这个不收”，让书记员记下分期付款。Anton一直低着头，看见Josefine Hofer的手在抖。
  _effects: cash -6 (长椅上晃着两条够不着地的腿); condition; flag +parent_fined_for_child; relation rival with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the fine will be deducted from the relief payment”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

  Rosa的父亲在车站找到活，全家要搬去另一区。临走前，Rosa把一本旧书塞给Anton，扉页上写着地址。Anton把书读完，按地址写了信，信被退回来，上面盖着“查无此人”。那本旧书Anton一直留在书架上。
  _effects: relation_end friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “朋友搬走前留下的地址已经找不到人了。”; skill; flag +friend_moved_away; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  舞卡上第一支曲子被Lena写走了。Anton在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Lena说没关系，把手放回Anton背后；第三支曲子响起时，Mitzi在门口冲Anton比了个手势，Anton假装没看见。

  Anton废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。

  Anton把借书证压在阅览室的台灯下，一页页抄书。Josef Bauer过来提醒闭馆，Anton求了十分钟，把最后一章抄完。回家路上，Anton把纸条贴在胸口，怕雪打湿，进门先烤干手才敢展开。第二天Anton把抄本交给老师，老师翻了两页，说字比课文好看。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +burned_the_schoolbook_letter_

  Anton趁Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne去喝咖啡，跑到街角的工厂检查所，把卸下来的护板指给Berta Stein看。Berta Stein第二天带着卷尺来了，在压印机旁边量了很久；Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne陪着笑，等门一关，就把Anton的工具从案子上扫进了木箱。
  _effects: relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; reputation 0.030000000000000027; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 13; flag +yf18_reported_press; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1927.08** `r14-edu.entrance-failure::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/passed-but-no-fee` tags: education, exam_failure, entrance_exam, shame, university, culture, schooling, student
  Anton过了录取线，学费却还差十二克朗。Josefine Hofer把存折翻到最后一面，又去问邻居借，回来时只说再等等。报到日过去三天，名额给了候补。Anton把录取通知压在玻璃板下，每天写功课前都看一眼那枚红章。报名费两克朗，是母亲从菜钱里扣的。
  _effects: cash -2 (放榜墙上的空白); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +admission_fee_shortfall_

  _effects: cash 2 (overtime pay); condition_

### 202 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josefine Hofer把Hedwig抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josefine Hofer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04; health 0.010000000000000009 (a good day)_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-nonsense` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Hedwig说了一长串谁也听不懂的话，最后用力点头，像是在宣布一件已经决定的事。Josefine Hofer认真听完，也点了点头；孩子满意地爬走了，留下大人们互相看着，忍不住笑。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josefine Hofer说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Hedwig问是什么，Josefine Hofer只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/stolen-marble` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Lena把Hedwig的玻璃弹珠藏进鞋里，被当场看见。Lena说只是“替你拿着”，Hedwig没有揭穿，也没有再拿出第二颗。两个人蹲在墙根，把一只蚂蚁看了很久，谁也没有先回家。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood -0.01_

- **1916.04** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  医院走廊里全是湿鞋印。Friedrich Hofer把Hedwig放在长椅上，自己去窗口交钱，六克朗换来一张薄薄的收据。护士把Hedwig领进观察室，白灯照得人睁不开眼。Hedwig抓着Friedrich Hofer的袖口不放，直到药水推进胳膊才松手。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票); health 0.07999999999999996 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04_

- **1917.07** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/home-remedy` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Hedwig烧得翻来覆去，Josefine Hofer把醋倒进温水，用布蘸着擦手心、脚心，又去灶间煮接骨木汁。勺子送到嘴边，Hedwig偏头躲，Josefine Hofer就等一会儿再喂。鸡叫时炉火压小，床脚用旧大衣堵住。两天后热度退了，嘴唇起了一层干皮。
  _effects: health 0.03 (土法退烧，热度渐退); mood -0.06_

  Hedwig的转学材料缺一张居住证明，Josef Bauer让Hedwig去区公所补。Hedwig跑了三趟，第三趟正赶上下雨，Josef Bauer看见Hedwig把湿透的纸一张张摊在柜台上，没再挑毛病，盖了章。Hedwig赶在开学前一天把名字写进新学校。那间教室在二楼，Hedwig第一天就找到了靠窗的座位，把旧课本摆好。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved_

- **1925.07** `r2-leisure.ferris-wheel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::ferris-wheel-reform/tested-the-new-brake` tags: leisure, fairground, ferris_wheel, fear, youth, reform, medical, schooling
  游乐场新装了紧急制动，铁牌上写着“由市议会验讫”。摩天轮升到一半，Grete Lechner拉了一下闸，座舱猛地停住，又慢慢往下走。Hedwig问这是不是坏了，Grete Lechner说这是新规矩，每趟都要试一次。Josef在座位上把栗子撒了一地。
  _effects: mood -0.03; condition; flag +tested_new_ferris_brake; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; knowledge “the municipal inspector requires a brake test on every ride”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1925.11** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Grete Stein在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Hedwig下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费); education secondary; skill_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.reputation-repair::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/passed-public-exam` tags: education, school_humiliation, reputation, repair, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Hedwig报名参加州里的中学毕业考试，考场设在原来的学校。Helene Bauer在监考名单上看见Hedwig的名字，什么也没说，只在发卷时多放了一支削好的铅笔。Hedwig考完最后一门，把铅笔留在桌上，走出校门时没有回头。
  _effects: reputation 0.1; flag +reputation_repaired; education secondary_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001596_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Hedwig下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance)_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1926.01** `r13-civ.vaccination::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.vaccination-reform/reform-class-vaccinated` tags: health, vaccination, school, parent, public_health, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Stein让全班按座位排队，Grete Lechner在讲台边摆开酒精瓶和针盒。轮到Hedwig时，Grete Stein替她把袖子卷好，说别怕，去年他自己也种过。下课后，黑板上还留着一行没擦掉的日期。
  _effects: health 0.05 (种痘室门口的袖子); condition; flag +vaccinated_in_school_campaign; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1926.03** `vienna-apprentice-printshop.lead-type-drop/lead-type-drop.scolded` tags: work, apprentice, factory, social
  Hedwig在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne碰翻一整盘8点铅字，字块滚了一地。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne敲着桌沿骂人，扣下两个克朗。Grete Lechner蹲在过道里帮忙捡字，只说了一句“下次用两只手托底”。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔偿散落铅字的工钱扣款); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

  Hedwig把信放在晚饭桌上。Josefine Hofer读完，问格拉茨远不远，又说家里的房租下个月要涨；那晚谁也没有说行或不行，信被压在糖罐下面，第二天边角沾了咖啡。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:200; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; flag +yf18_family_knows_letter; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Hedwig把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Hedwig没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 33 -> 43; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 20 -> 25; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 8 -> 13; flag +yf18_go_alone; mood -0.01999999999999999_

  Hedwig把袖口卷到肘上，用麻绳扎紧，替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一叠纸送进压印机。前两百张很顺，第二百零一张时，纸角卡住，Hedwig伸手去拨，机器没有停；等Grete Lechner拉开Hedwig，食指的指甲已经翻了起来。
  _effects: condition; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 30 -> 22; cash -2 (a doctor and a bandage); flag +yf18_press_injury; mood -0.05000000000000002_

  _effects: flag +left_domestic_service_for_factory; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; move place-wien-printshop-winter; employment Arbeiterin @ org-winter-print_

  早上醒来，Hedwig的手指还是麻的。Hedwig跟Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说想走，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把账本翻到最后一页，扣了午饭钱，把剩下的铜币推过来，少了半克朗。Hedwig抱着围裙过街，Luise Winter在门口说，另一家老板脾气急，但从不扣工钱，窗户也一直开着。
  _effects: unemployment (辞去通风不良的印刷作坊); cash -0.5 (被扣掉的午饭钱和少拿的工钱); health 0.03 (离开油墨味浓的作坊后头晕减轻); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1929.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/wage-question-silence` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  Josefine Hofer问起工钱和房租，Hedwig报了个数，又补了一句冬天活少。桌上一时只剩刀叉声。Rosa低着头把面包掰成小块，Josefine Hofer往汤里又加了一勺盐，谁都没再开口；这顿饭把两个人的将来问成了一个没答完的数目。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; relation partner with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1930.01** `task-vienna-writer-researcher.abandon-project/project-sold` tags: knowledge, culture, writing, editor, library, publication, task, task:knowledge
  房租催到门口，Hedwig把已写的章节卖给一家小报，换了八克朗，够付一个月房租和两顿热饭。长篇锁进抽屉，钥匙搁在盐罐后面，Grete Huber问起时他只说“先不提了”，第二天照常去上工。
  _effects: cash 8 (小报买下章节的稿酬); mood -0.06; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +novel_shelved; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1931.01** `p9-work-career.clerk-post/p9-work-career.clerk-post.archive` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, office, application
  Hedwig的信写得好，笔画干净，算盘也快，可速记一栏空了大半。Grete Stein 2把卷子批了“文书可用，速记欠熟”，人事科把他安排到档案室抄旧保单，日薪两克朗四十赫勒，比正式办事员少一截。桌旁没有窗，只有一盏悬在头顶的灯，他抄到下班时右肩发僵。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (档案室抄写日薪); skill; flag +awaiting_clerk_slot; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

- **1931.06** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home
  Hedwig托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Josefine Hofer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Hedwig晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Josefine Hofer的腿伤有人料理，Hedwig却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用); health 0.08 (看护妇料理后伤势好转)_

- **1931.07** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig把账本摊在灯下，一页一页念出日期和克朗数，念到买纸那笔时有人 From 门口咳嗽。Emil低头拨弄围巾穗子，没有替谁说话。桌子上的旧墨水瓶被碰倒，蓝墨慢慢洇进木纹。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1932.01** `major-task.scholarship-application/half-fee-place` tags: task, task:education, education, scholarship, school
  委员会只肯减一半学费。Helene Bauer替Hedwig争到六克朗书费，条件是走读，每天放学后替学校抄名册。名册要抄到天黑，手指常被蓝墨水染蓝。Hedwig算过车票和墨水钱，还是不够，只好在印刷厂接晚间折页活，把课本钱先挣出来。
  _effects: education higher; cash 6 (book allowance from the school fund); role; flag +works_evenings_for_school; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1932.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Alois Berg攥着Hedwig的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1932.07** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/held-the-room` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, reform
  首演夜，Hedwig在第二首歌的中间忘了两句词，干脆停下来听乐队多走了一个小节，再接着唱。台下有人以为这是新加的停顿，笑了一声，随后整间屋子都放松了。Ferdinand Moser在后台说，今晚之后，节目单上可以印Hedwig的名字。
  _effects: state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:427; cash 2 (首演酬劳与加演分成); role; reputation 0.060000000000000026; skill; flag +debut_success; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1932.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Gustav Stein借给Hedwig一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Hedwig睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Hedwig难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Elisabeth Bauer；Hedwig一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Emil把钟表送进当铺。Hedwig落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:426_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.volksbibliothek` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Hedwig听Grete Huber报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Hedwig穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1933.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  药房在街口，柜台玻璃下面压着价目纸。Hedwig把两克朗推过去，伙计找回来十二赫勒，药片用报纸包成一个小包。工友接过去时手还在抖，报纸边角很快被汗洇软。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1933.07** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Otto Stein却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Hedwig把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-divorce/separate-under-one-roof` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation
  Hedwig和Emil把床铺分开，米和煤也各算各的。两人仍住在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，因为谁都没有钱再租一间房；孩子在两床之间来回，三克朗买了隔开的锅碗和煤油，家里从此有两本账，夜里两盏灯先后熄灭。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.08000000000000002; mood -0.07999999999999996; cash -3 (separate household goods（其中3克朗记入债务）)_

- **1934.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sold-the-letter` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, vienna, urban
  Helene Berg把Hedwig托付保管的一封旧信卖给了收旧纸的人。信里提到几个已经不用的名字，买主又把信转给了另一个买主。Hedwig再见到那封信时，它被压在一块玻璃下面，标价是Hedwig半个月的工钱。
  _effects: cash -1 (买回自己的旧信（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.04999999999999999; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; knowledge “我写的一封旧信被人压在玻璃下面标价出售。”; flag +private_letter_sold; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1934.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Helene Berg病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 0 -> 1; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007300_

- **1934.01** `r13-civ.rehearse-play::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cast-as-understudy` tags: culture, theatre, rehearsal, play, ambition, vienna, urban, crisis
  Otto Stein让Hedwig站在舞台边上看完整场，说先背熟替补的台词。排练室的暖气坏了，演员们穿着大衣念对白。散场时，Otto Stein把一本卷了角的剧本塞给Hedwig，说明晚还来。
  _effects: flag +cast_as_understudy; role; skill; knowledge “the understudy must know the whole second act by Friday”_

- **1934.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Elisabeth Bauer 2借给Hedwig一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Hedwig睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1935.01** `r15-curse.aftermath::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/quiet-mercy` tags: curse, fear, supernatural, revenge, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Alois Berg病好以后来敲过门。两个人隔着门槛站了很久。Hedwig没有说原谅，只把一包药放在对方手里。Alois Berg走后，Hedwig把门关上，背靠着它坐了一会儿。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; flag +curse_mercy; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1935.01** `state_expire:fame` tags: fame, reputation, media, status, state, state_tick, state:fame
  Hedwig的「公众名声」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove fame state:world-vienna-1912:427_

- **1935.01** `r13-civ.collect-signatures::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/shopkeeper-reported` tags: politics, petition, signatures, institution, neighborhood, vienna, urban, crisis
  杂货店老板看见Hedwig在门口拦人，转身进了后屋。第二天，巡警在巷口等Hedwig，问签名纸是谁起的头。Hedwig说没有头，巡警把纸收走，说带回去看看。
  _effects: reputation -0.08000000000000002; flag +petition_reported_to_police; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; knowledge “the shopkeeper told the police who started the petition”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75_

- **1935.02** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Helene Stein从围裙里掏出几张揉皱的收据，Hedwig把它们按日期排开。屋外有人上楼，脚步在门口停了一下又走了。Hedwig把新表折成四折塞进衣袋，说先管这条街，别的明天再说。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000711:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.03** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home
  Hedwig托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Josefine Hofer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Hedwig晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Josefine Hofer的腿伤有人料理，Hedwig却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (看护妇料理后伤势好转)_

- **1935.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  印泥是赭红色的，像干了的面包瓤。Hedwig按完手印，工头把表举到窗边吹干，阳光里那张纸透出指印的轮廓。楼下第十区的街面传来运煤车的铁皮声，星期六下午突然显得很宽。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.04999999999999999; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

- **1936.01** `curse15-notice.the-second-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/calls-it-a-fever` tags: curse, notice, illness, fear, knowledge, supernatural, vienna, urban
  医生说只是换季的烧，Hedwig便也这么说。第二次退烧后，Hedwig把那张写满日期的纸烧了，灰倒进花盆。花盆里的植物活了下来，Hedwig因此更愿意相信巧合，也不再去数下一个十一天。
  _effects: flag +curse_dismissed_as_fever; condition; mood 0.02999999999999997_

- **1936.01** `r13-civ.ration-card::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/bought-black-market-card` tags: politics, rationing, food, war, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hedwig在巷口从一个戴鸭舌帽的人手里买了一张配给证，花了六克朗。证上的名字被刮过，纸面起毛。Helene Huber收证时多看了一眼，还是打了孔。回家路上，Hedwig把证藏在鞋垫下面。
  _effects: cash -6 (配给证上的号码（其中6克朗记入债务）); food; flag +bought_black_market_ration_card; knowledge “the clerk noticed the scraped name but stamped the card anyw”_

- **1936.03** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/burn-bird` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, vienna
  印刷机的滚筒咬住Hedwig的袖口时，谁都以为只擦破了一层皮。伤口结痂后，却留下一只张开翅膀的鸟形烫痕。Katharina Stein 2给了Hedwig两枚银币，说别去报工伤，厂里正查安全。Hedwig把钱收下，回到排字房，觉得那只鸟在袖子里发烫。
  _effects: cash 2 (工头给的封口费); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:588; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_injury; mood -0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1936.03** `state_reaction:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-public` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Hedwig在人群里挽了一下袖子，第二天就有人来问那块印子是不是“会动的”。Hedwig说不会，问的人却已经把自己的解释说完了。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 35 -> 43; mood -0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009062_

- **1936.05** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig和Klara一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1936.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig看Gustav Kral盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Gustav Kral愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Hedwig门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Hedwig打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: food_

- **1937.01** `df18-curse.name-in-the-door::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/carve-back` tags: curse, name, door, omen, neighbor, dark, supernatural, vienna
  Hedwig没有磨掉那个名字，而是在旁边刻上那个害过自己的人的名字。几天后，那个人在工厂的楼梯上摔断了腿。Anton Berg 2说是巧了，Hedwig没有说话；夜里，Hedwig用指尖摸过门框上的两道刻痕，觉得木头比人的皮肤还热。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; knowledge “我在门框上刻了仇人的名字，几天后他摔断了腿。”; flag +df18_carved_second_name; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Hedwig在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Hedwig低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 43 -> 53; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 20 -> 26; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed unknown -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.01** `kernel-social.lending::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::lending-crisis/lending.sewing-machine` tags: task, task:social, social, lending, debt, neighbor, money, everyday
  Hedwig的房租也到期了，只好把Helene Stein的缝纫机搬到自己屋里，说等还钱再还机器。Helene Stein来取针线盒时，看见缝纫机头上盖着布，站了一会儿就走了；第二天，整栋楼都知道这件事。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000711:child; role; reputation -0.039999999999999994; flag +seized_sewing_machine; knowledge “缝纫机被扣作抵押，针线盒还留在邻居手里。”_

- **1937.08** `statistical_infection`
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1937.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Hedwig的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1937.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Hedwig的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  又过了一年，Hedwig学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Hedwig的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 53 -> 47; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 26 -> 22; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Lena回信说自家也紧，孩子要交学费，实在腾不出。Hedwig把信折好，放进炉子，火苗舔到“见谅”两个字时，她才把信夹出来。第二天，Hedwig去当铺问那只旧表能当多少。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +relative_refused_loan; relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1938.02** `statistical_recovery`
  Hedwig熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1938.08** `ad20-police-stop::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/taken-in` tags: police, legal, street, everyday, obligation, risk, vienna, urban
  Helene Huber让Hedwig靠墙站好，把口袋里的东西一件件放在地上。钥匙、手帕、半块面包，还有一张写着地址的纸。Helene Huber把纸对着路灯看了很久，最后说“跟我们走一趟”。
  _effects: cash -2 (a fine after the police stop); reputation -0.03; flag +police_stop_record; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。

  _effects: health -0.05 (beaten in police custody); condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Elisabeth Kral。接生的人收六克朗，Anton Kral在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Hedwig起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。

  Katharina Stein 2在门房里听完，从抽屉里摸出一张印着厂名的信纸，写Hedwig“手脚干净，肯学机器”，又签了名。墨水没干，他用吸墨纸按了按，说别拿去给太大的厂，小厂才看这个。Hedwig把信折成四折，放进内袋最里面。
  _effects: cash 1 (旧工头的一封荐书); flag +work13_obtained_reference_letter; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; knowledge “the old foreman still has letterhead from the factory”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  Hedwig说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Luise Kral没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Hedwig听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。

- **1940.01** `df18-mark.reading-at-market::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/read-as-curse` tags: body, mark, omen, rumor, superstition, market, vienna, urban
  肉摊的Grete Weiss看见Hedwig挽袖子，刀停在半空，说这种印子像吊死的人留下的手印。旁边买菜的人安静了一瞬，又各自低头挑菜。Hedwig想笑，却发现自己的手在抖；那天回家，Hedwig绕开了平时走的巷子。

  入冬后，Hedwig在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Hedwig低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 80 -> 90; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 28 -> 34; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.039999999999999994 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000349:child_

  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Hedwig把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  _effects: cash -0.1 (买收摊前的碰伤苹果); food_

- **1941.01** `curse15-notice.borrowed-mourning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sells-the-mourning` tags: curse, money, fear, client, ritual, supernatural, work, vienna
  Emil Hofer请Hedwig去家里坐一夜，说只要Hedwig在灵堂待到天亮，那个欠钱的人就会自己上门。Hedwig收了钱，在灵堂坐到天亮，什么也没有发生。一个月后，那个人真的来了，送来一袋面粉；Emil Hofer把面粉分给Hedwig一半，没有解释。
  _effects: cash 3 (替人守灵的酬劳); reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; flag +sold_mourning_service; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

  开口时只提了手推车，钱的事咽回去。对方爽快地把车钥匙样的铁钩递过来，说用完了搁回院里就行。十克朗改去当铺想办法，一件旧外套和一只怀表抵了十二克朗，赎期三个月，票据折好收在证件夹里。
  _effects: cash 12 (当铺抵当旧外套和怀表换来的钱); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001758:child; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1941.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig没有立刻答话，先去敲Luise Hofer的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Luise Hofer提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Hedwig把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1941.04** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-chain-only` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

  _effects: knowledge “背叛与发烧之间隔着十一天。”; flag +curse_counted; skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -2 (板车与押金收据（其中1.8克朗记入债务）); flag +landlord_withheld_trunk; knowledge “the landlord keeps the trunk against a wall repair claim”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-110_

  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (看护妇料理后伤势好转); health -0.019999999999999997 (夜间加班的疲劳)_

  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Hedwig就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Elisabeth Berg摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1947.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014774_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Hedwig把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Hedwig这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 55 -> 61; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 25 -> 20; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 70 -> 74; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.03** `hs18-blackout.bakery::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/share-the-lamp` tags: blackout, queue, bread, rumor, neighbor, shortage, vienna, urban
  面包店里只剩一盏罩着蓝布的小灯。Elisabeth Stein把最后一筐面包放在柜台上，队伍却越挤越紧。Hedwig把带来的煤油灯举高，照见每张脸；有人开始按顺序报数，声音从队尾传过来，像一条看不见的绳。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.panic 74 -> 64; state_parameter hs18-blackout-rumor.credibility 80 -> 85; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; flag +shared_lamp_in_bread_queue; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig看Helene Berg盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Helene Berg愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Hedwig门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Hedwig打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; food_

- **1947.10** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Hedwig把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.01** `state_reaction:pc20-second-chance/public-doubt` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  旧事被人在街上重新提起，Hedwig的名字和另一件事绑在一起。有人替Hedwig辩解，也有人只笑了一声；Hedwig回家后把当天的收据一张张摊开，像在给自己准备证词。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 20 -> 28; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 61 -> 57; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015944_

  Hedwig把名单交给Ferdinand Huber，没有添一句解释。Franz Moser在走廊里拦住Hedwig，问是不是一定要这样；Hedwig说名单上不止自己一个名字，然后从他身边走过去，脚步比想象中稳。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 28 -> 36; reputation 0.03; flag +exposed_old_friend; knowledge “名单上不止自己一个名字，{subject}把它交给了{official}。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

- **1948.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  天色还灰着，Hedwig手里攥着几张折皱的证明。Katharina Stein 2让他念车皮上的号码，听见念对了，就朝装卸班一挥手，说先按日结钱，一天两克朗四十分。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ morgue:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83-evt:world-vienna-1912:008456; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1948.02** `af17-luck.wallet-in-the-snow::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/the-wallet-had-a-name` tags: luck, money, street, chance, irony, daily, vienna, urban
  Hedwig在雪地里捡到一只缝线开裂的钱包，里面有三克朗、一张彩票和一张写着地址的纸。Hedwig按地址送回去，开门的老太太说钱包不是她的，却把彩票塞给Hedwig，说“你拿着吧，我这辈子没中过”。
  _effects: state_add af17-lucky-idiot state:world-vienna-1912:1086; cash 3 (the wallet’s owner refused the money); flag +returned_the_snow_wallet; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +lucky_idiot; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.06** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +watch_pawned; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1948.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Hedwig缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1949.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Hedwig一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Otto Weiss送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/small-mercies` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  又一年过去，Hedwig在煤气灯下捡到一枚银币，又在当铺门口遇见一个认错人的老主顾。好事都不大，却一件接一件；街角咖啡馆里的人开始说，Hedwig一定知道什么别人不知道的门路。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 12 -> 12; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 5 -> 11; cash 1 (捡到的银币); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Hedwig晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Hedwig听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 63 -> 56; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 31 -> 39; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 25 -> 28; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `r13-edu.correspondence-course::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/certificate-by-post` tags: education, correspondence, adult_education, literacy, mail, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig每周收到一叠油印讲义，做完题再寄回去。Ferdinand Hofer在页边用红笔改错，有时附一句“第二步再想想”。十二个月后，Hedwig收到结业证书，纸很薄，但编号印得清楚。证书被Hedwig钉在床头，邮差再来时，问Hedwig要不要报下一门课。编号是手写的。
  _effects: flag +correspondence_certificate; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

- **1949.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  账本的纸边已经发毛，Hedwig翻到那一页时，手指沾上一层灰。有人说“三十四克朗够买两袋面粉”，没人接话。最后举手表决，赞成销账的手比反对的多两只，老成员把手放下去时，椅子又晃了一下。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000693:child; flag +union_debt_written_off_

- **1949.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Hedwig把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001758:child_

- **1949.11** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Hedwig走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000002 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000349:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.01** `bc18-luck.civic-mascot::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/luck-blamed-for-fire` tags: luck, charity, public, reputation, absurd, social, suspicion, vienna
  仓库失火那晚，委员会的活动棚因为风向改变而保住了。第二天，Ferdinand Kral却在门口拦住Hedwig，说有人看见Hedwig在火起前从仓库边走过；幸运不能证明清白，反而像一种从别人那里借来的东西。Hedwig把铜别针还给她，回家路上第一次觉得，好运也会留下脚印。
  _effects: cash -1 (被委员会辞退后的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.04000000000000001; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 11 -> 26; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; condition; flag +luck_blamed_for_fire; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1950.01** `r13-edu.practice-craft::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/mastered-dovetail-joint` tags: education, craft, apprenticeship, practice, skill, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig跟着Elisabeth Stein 2学做燕尾榫，第一块木料锯歪了，第二块凿穿了，第三块才严丝合缝。Elisabeth Stein 2把三块都留在工作台上，说以后Hedwig自己看。Hedwig把最好的那块收进工具箱，剩下的两块也没扔，用粉笔写上日期。那三块木头后来被师弟拿去学样。
  _effects: flag +mastered_dovetail_joint; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1950.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Rosa Huber点了牛奶咖啡，Hedwig要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.08** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Hedwig把照片铁盒交给Katharina Winter抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销（其中0.37克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005542:child_

- **1950.10** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Hedwig点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Elisabeth Stein 2量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Hedwig用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Elisabeth Stein指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003178:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `r13-work.report-defect::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/defect-fixed` tags: work, safety, bureaucracy, factory, whistleblowing, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hedwig把松动的螺栓画在纸上，标了机台号和班次，交给Maria Huber。Maria Huber起初把纸压在茶壶底下，Hedwig就站在窗口不走，直到他把维修班叫来。第二天螺栓换了新的，Luise Kral 2在早会上说这是“例行保养”，没提Hedwig的名字。
  _effects: cash 2 (报修单压在茶壶底下); reputation 0.05000000000000002; flag +work13_reported_and_fixed_defect; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1951.01** `state_reaction:af17-lucky-idiot/windfall-talk` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  咖啡馆里有人问Hedwig，是不是替哪个议员跑过腿，或者认识一个会看牌的女人。Hedwig说自己只是运气好；这句话说出口，连Hedwig自己都觉得像在遮掩。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 38 -> 50; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +luck_explained_as_a_deal; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Hedwig眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Anton Weiss隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food_

- **1951.12** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Hedwig把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Maria Huber放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Katharina Lechner在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001758:child_

- **1952.01** `af17-luck.quiet-windfall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/returned-at-the-box-office` tags: luck, windfall, daily, quiet, money, social, coincidence, vienna
  Hedwig在歌剧院门口的雪水里捡到一只鳄鱼皮钱包，里面有九克朗、一张当晚的站票和一张调琴师的名片。Hedwig把钱包交到售票处，Helene Bauer赶来时没有数钱，只问Hedwig会不会弹琴。三天后，Helene Bauer替Hedwig调好了一架旧钢琴，没收工钱，还写了一张去乐器行的介绍信。
  _effects: cash 2 (失主给的谢礼); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; reputation 0.03; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 50 -> 40; skill; flag +returned_a_lost_wallet; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/small-mercies` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  又一年过去，Hedwig在煤气灯下捡到一枚银币，又在当铺门口遇见一个认错人的老主顾。好事都不大，却一件接一件；街角咖啡馆里的人开始说，Hedwig一定知道什么别人不知道的门路。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 12 -> 12; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 40 -> 46; cash 1 (捡到的银币); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `r13-civ.rehearse-play::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rehearsal-broken-up` tags: culture, theatre, rehearsal, play, ambition, vienna, urban, postwar
  排练到第二幕，房东下来敲门，说楼下的住户嫌吵，再排就涨租。Maria Huber把布景折起来，演员们抱着道具站在楼梯口。Hedwig最后走，关灯时看见舞台地板上有一圈粉笔画的走位线。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000007; flag +rehearsal_broken_up_by_landlord_

- **1952.04** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-chain-only` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  链子从你指缝里滑下去，落在绒布上几乎没有声音。掌柜说两克朗四十赫勒，你点了点头。出门时你把表掏出来上了几圈弦，它还在走，只是再没法挂在马甲上了。
  _effects: cash 2.4 (典当银表链所得); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1952.05** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Ferdinand Kral追出来问怎么不领了，Hedwig说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Hedwig把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: food_

- **1953.01** `state_expire:af17-lucky-idiot` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  Hedwig的「走运的傻瓜」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove af17-lucky-idiot state:world-vienna-1912:1086; flag -lucky_idiot_

- **1953.01** `r13-civ.new-district-lodging::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/room-rented` tags: social, housing, lodging, landlady, district, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Huber把招租纸从门上揭下来，领Hedwig看了后间。屋里只有一张铁床和一只裂了口的洗手盆，窗户对着天井。Hedwig付了六克朗，Maria Huber把钥匙从围裙里解下来，说晚上十点关大门。
  _effects: cash -6 (新城区门牌下的招租纸); flag +lodging_secured_in_new_district; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; knowledge “the front door is locked at ten every night”_

- **1953.01** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-granted` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Hedwig在圣文生会门口登记，领到一张面包券和一小袋土豆。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.stitched` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Hedwig用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: skill_

- **1954.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/deposit-refused` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Lechner把住院单推回来，说押金不够，先去门外等。Hedwig在台阶上坐到天黑，怀里揣着药和一张转诊单，最后跟着收尸车旁边的人流走回了家。第二天咳嗽更重，她把转诊单压在枕头下，没再去。
  _effects: cash -1 (住院处窗口的押金); mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +hospital_admission_refused; knowledge “the hospital will not admit a patient without the full depos”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-168_

- **1954.09** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/sold-the-share` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Otto Berg 3把Hedwig寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Hedwig追到码头时，Otto Berg 3已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Hedwig在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-136; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-136_

- **1955.01** `kernel-work.task-wage-claim::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/work-wage-claim-partial` tags: task, work, wages, claim, deduction, vienna, urban, postwar
  Elisabeth Hofer 2承认有三克朗算错，却把另外三克朗推给“机器磨损”。Hedwig要求见Luise Kral 2，Luise Kral 2说厂里一直这么扣，别为这点钱闹。Hedwig收下补回的三克朗，把剩下的扣款抄进自己的小本子；出门时，Elisabeth Hofer 2把笔帽盖得很响。
  _effects: cash 3 (追回部分扣款); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; flag +partial_wage_settlement; knowledge “厂里把机器磨损也摊进工人工资袋，且没有明细。”; role_

- **1955.04** `statistical_infection`
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1955.04** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  第一趟过街口时车上的煤箱差点翻下来，Berta Kral 2用膝盖顶住，Hedwig把绳子又收了一道。第三趟回来，两人靠在门框上喘气，鞋底都是灰，省下的钱还揣在围裙里。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-174_

- **1955.05** `r2-law.building-permit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-printshop/permit-issued` tags: law, building_permit, construction, housing, inspection, vienna, urban, postwar
  Katharina Berg 2把图纸摊在窗口，用尺量了后院到邻居墙的距离，说退让不够，要改。Hedwig说改了就少一间房，Katharina Berg 2说少一间房总比拆了好。Maria Huber在旁边重新画了一条线，把厨房往后挪了半米；Katharina Berg 2收下修改图，盖了三个章，说脚手架搭起来之前还要来验一次。
  _effects: cash -18 (脚手架上的批文（其中18克朗记入债务）); flag +building_permit_granted_after_setback; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; knowledge “the kitchen must be moved half a meter from the boundary”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-174_

- **1955.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Hedwig的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1955.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Hedwig把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-174; skill_

- **1955.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Hedwig的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1955.09** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.share-spinach` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  两人同时伸手，又同时缩回去。Berta Weiss说自己本来想买回去做馅，Hedwig说那正好一人一半。摊主用草绳把菜捆好，嘴里嘟囔着收摊了收摊了，把剩下的零钱扒拉进木匣。
  _effects: cash -0.04 (分买最后一把菠菜（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss_

- **1955.10** `statistical_recovery`
  Hedwig熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1956.01** `r13-work.piece-rates::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/rate-raised` tags: work, wages, piecework, factory, negotiation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Elisabeth Stein 2把计件单钉在墙上，Hedwig看完没有签字，先问废品怎么算。Luise Kral 2在后面帮腔，说上个月三件废活全扣在工人头上。Elisabeth Stein 2把单价从八赫勒改到九赫勒，又在废品栏添了“材料由厂方负担”。
  _effects: cash 2 (计件单上的小数点); flag +work13_piece_rate_raised; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; employment Stückarbeiter @ org-winter-print_

- **1956.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig没有立刻答话，先去敲Luise Kral的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Luise Kral提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Hedwig把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1956.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Anna Weiss在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Hedwig把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1956.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.left-on-chair` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Hedwig只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +unfinished_coat_

- **1957.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/dismissed-after-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, unemployment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Hedwig不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Hedwig拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Hedwig的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); cash -6 (medical treatment（其中6克朗记入债务）)_

- **1957.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.relative-introduction/relative-introduction.warehouse-errand` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, relative, introduction
  “先替我看两个月仓库。”Luise Kral说得很平，好像这是最省事的安排。Hedwig跟着到仓库，麻袋咬破的地方漏出一路面粉，角落里那张床短得伸不直腿。
  _effects: relation kin with person-luise-kral; food; condition_

### 398 subject events

- **1912.04** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/collect-the-benefit` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Berta Weiss让Hermann Kral冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Friedrich Hofer寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Berta Weiss把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:1; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.01; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.01_

- **1912.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Berta Weiss傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Emil Weiss在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Emil Weiss敲开Berta Weiss的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.049999999999999996_

- **1912.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Berta Weiss把Hermann Kral和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Berta Weiss把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; mood 0.04; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1913.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Berta Weiss在Weiss-Wohnung, Alsergrund靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Karl Kraus的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1913.01** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Berta Weiss刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Berta Weiss站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 52; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Berta Weiss的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Berta Weiss收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Berta Weiss先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 42; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 62; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.03_

  她把Anna Moser带回自己家，在厨房支了一张行军床。房东第二天就来敲门，说多住一个人要加钱；她付了半个月的煤钱，把Anna Moser的行李藏进床底。房东来查煤表时，她把行军床折起来靠在墙边。
  _effects: move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; cash -4 (多住一人要补的煤钱和饭钱); condition; flag +sheltered_refugee; knowledge “房东按人头收煤钱，多住一个人要提前说”_

  Berta Weiss和Alois Lechner按原计划沿葡萄园间的土路往上走，鞋子沾满黄泥，到山顶时风把汗湿的衬衫吹得冰凉。下山在第一个酒棚坐下，两人合点了一壶新酒和一块面包，一共一克朗二十赫勒；Berta Weiss喝到第二杯时脸颊发烫，把剩下的半块面包包进手帕带回家，第二天腿酸，却睡得很沉。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (卡伦山酒棚的新酒和面包); food; health 0.020000000000000004 (星期天登山远足); relation friend with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.06_

- **1918.01** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Berta Weiss嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Berta Weiss用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1918.05** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.dispute-rate` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Helene Bauer用拇指甲刮着一件领子的边线，说这儿跳了针。Berta Weiss把煤油灯往前推了推，让他凑近看，说线是细了点，活没少做。Grete Stein倚在门框上没走，屋里一时只有尺子碰桌面的响。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1918.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Berta Weiss在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Katharina Bauer拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1918.11** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Josef Bauer凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Berta Weiss把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Berta Weiss没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000814_

- **1919.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Berta Weiss咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Berta Weiss手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Otto Moser等在门口，问值不值，Berta Weiss只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.08; reputation 0.020000000000000004; skill_

- **1919.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Otto Winter在门口替Berta Weiss捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1919.08** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/signed-for-a-favor` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, rural, agriculture
  Grete Stein在签字前把一张剧院包厢的票根留在桌上，说是“给孩子的”。Berta Weiss把票根推回去，却在许可证上签了名；排水图仍缺一页，但窗口当天就盖了章。扩建开工后，第一场雨把后院淹了半尺。
  _effects: organization_funds; relation friend with person-grete-stein; flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1919.09** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, injury
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Berta Weiss把照片铁盒交给Mitzi Winter抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); health -0.03 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1920.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Berta Weiss把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Katharina Bauer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Berta Weiss把床单对折，腾出一半，Katharina Bauer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; mood 0.020000000000000004_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001033_

- **1920.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-signature` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, rural, agriculture
  报纸放大的签名其实是Ferdinand Winter的：两个字母写得太像。更正启事登在第七版，只有三行；Berta Weiss的名字被拿掉了，Ferdinand Winter的名字留了下来。第二天，Ferdinand Winter没有来上班，桌上的茶杯还是满的。
  _effects: reputation 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; flag +scandal_misdirected; knowledge “被登出来的签名是书记员的，更正启事在第七版。”; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1920.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Berta Weiss想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.039999999999999994_

- **1920.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Anna Moser把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Berta Weiss去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-anna-moser_

- **1920.10** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Berta Weiss把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Katharina Bauer合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer_

  Grete Stein用Berta Weiss留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Berta Weiss发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Berta Weiss自己的名字。Grete Stein说只是替Berta Weiss省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱); reputation -0.1; relation rival with person-grete-stein; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand; mood -0.07_

  Berta Weiss想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。

- **1921.02** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Berta Weiss看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Ferdinand Moser追出来问怎么不领了，Berta Weiss说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Berta Weiss把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1921.03** `r3-crime.forensic-doctor::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-hospital::lectures-in-the-basement/humiliated-on-the-stand` tags: crime, medicine, evidence, court, bureaucracy, medical, university, knowledge
  Berta Weiss在法庭上展开解剖图，Elisabeth Berg打断说“别拿这些吓人”。Berta Weiss解释了三遍，Elisabeth Berg问“你是医生还是律师”。旁听席上有人笑，Otto Winter在最后一排低着头。Berta Weiss把图收起来，说“我的结论写在第几页”，声音很平。
  _effects: reputation -0.04999999999999999; condition; flag +testified_as_forensic_expert; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; knowledge “the judge refused to look at the autopsy drawing”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1921.09** `r1-cul.failed-replication::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-hospital/unnamed-technical-help` tags: science, replication, failure, rivalry, method, medical, university, knowledge
  Berta Weiss只是实验室的临时工，却看出恒温箱的门封条老化了。Grete Stein换上新的封条，复现结果立刻回来；论文致谢里没有Berta Weiss的名字，Luise Kral在脚注里加了一句：技术协助。Berta Weiss把那张脚注剪下来，贴在工具箱盖子里。
  _effects: mood 0.02999999999999997; flag +unnamed_technical_assistance; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the incubator door seal was the real variable”_

- **1922.01** `vienna-history-freud-letter/letter-answered` tags: history, psychoanalysis, freud, freud_contact, letter, medical_consultation
  Berta Weiss 把症状、睡眠和童年的一件小事写进四页信纸，寄到 Berggasse 19。十天后回信到了：Sigmund Freud 只写了两行，约Berta Weiss 在某个下午带信来，诊费在见面时再谈。Berta Weiss 把回信折好，发现信封背面有一道铅笔写的编号。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-sigmund-freud; knowledge “Freud 在 Berggasse 19 按固定诊时接待病人，先看信或听叙述，再按半小时收取诊费。”; cash -2 (postage and writing paper for the letter); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1922.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Berta Weiss为维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000013_

- **1922.02** `r13-turn.recovery::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-hospital/healed-cleanly` tags: health, recovery, medicine, body, hope, medical, university, knowledge
  Elisabeth Hofer把最后一层绷带剪开，伤口已经收成一条浅红色的线。Berta Weiss试着弯了弯手指，关节发出一点响声，但不疼了。Anna Bauer在门口说，能自己拿勺子吃饭就是好了。Berta Weiss当天下午走回院子，第一次没有扶墙。
  _effects: health 0.15 (拆掉绷带的那天); flag +recovered_from_wound; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation friend with person-anna-bauer_

- **1922.08** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼还滴着水，Berta Weiss在走廊里分成两份。一份拿进自家，一份搁在Otto Stein的门口。傍晚Otto Stein来还盘子，盘子里是一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1922.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Berta Weiss靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费)_

- **1923.01** `vienna-history-freud-consultation/continue-the-hours` tags: history, psychoanalysis, freud, freud_followup, medical_consultation
  Berta Weiss 连续几周在同一张椅子上坐着。Sigmund Freud 很少给建议，只让Berta Weiss 把每次醒来的时间、梦里的房间和当天花的钱记下来。有一次Berta Weiss 说到一半停住，Sigmund Freud 没有追问，只把怀表往前推了推；下一次会诊仍按同一时间开始。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-sigmund-freud; historical development:seed-vienna-psychoanalytic-circle-1912 as participant; knowledge “持续的会诊要求病人自己记录梦、时间和日常花费；解释不是一次给出的结论。”; cash -15 (a block of follow-up consultations); mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001731_

- **1923.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-hospital/neighbor-soup` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, medical, university, knowledge
  Josef Bauer端来一碗兑了水的牛奶，顺手把Berta Weiss家的炉子捅旺。Berta Weiss靠在床头，听Josef Bauer讲楼下谁也被传染了，谁家把病人送去了医院。人走后，屋里还留着一点煤烟和奶味。
  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health 0.04000000000000001 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-josef-bauer_

  Berta Weiss在掩体里教新兵怎么分辨炮声，讲到一半忽然忘了自己刚才说什么。新兵笑，Berta Weiss也跟着笑，笑完去壕外吐了。Ferdinand Winter 2后来在病历上写“疲劳”，没写“害怕”；Berta Weiss把那张纸折好，放在胸袋里。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; condition; flag +hid_shell_shock_symptoms; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1923.04** `r3-war.trench-foot::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-hospital::trench-foot-ward/saw-blackened-toes` tags: war, army, trench_foot, medicine, evacuation, medical, university, knowledge
  换药时Anna Winter 3从Berta Weiss脚底揭下一层死皮，Berta Weiss看见自己的脚趾甲也跟着掉了下来。Grete Stein把自己的镜子借给Berta Weiss，说“别看更好”；Berta Weiss还是看了，然后把镜子扣在床头，一整天没说话。
  _effects: mood -0.12; condition; flag +saw_own_blackened_toes; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  Berta Weiss点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Hermann Lechner量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

  两年过去，Berta Weiss已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Berta Weiss也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。

- **1931.01** `initiative:found_process:mortuary:person-berta-weiss-evt:world-vienna-1912:005481`
  圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 圣马克斯殡葬合作社的持续运动; historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-berta-weiss-evt:world-vienna-1912:005481-1931 as organizer; historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-berta-weiss-evt:world-vienna-1912:005481-1931 as organizer; knowledge “圣马克斯殡葬合作社的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.039999999999999994_

- **1931.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。

  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Berta Weiss的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Berta Weiss家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006117_

- **1931.03** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/letter-printed-as-praise` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, reform
  Elisabeth Huber写了一封告状信，列数Berta Weiss的懒惰、贪婪和“可疑的出身”，却把信封上的地址写成了报社。Otto Kral以为这是一篇自荐小品，删掉几处脏话登了出来；读者喜欢这种不留情面的自嘲，两个商行给Berta Weiss寄来聘书。Elisabeth Huber在咖啡馆看见报纸，差点把杯子捏碎。
  _effects: cash 4 (商行聘书的预付薪金); reputation 0.05; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:361; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; condition; flag +enemys_letter_became_an_advert; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.03** `state_reaction:public-scandal/gossip` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_reaction, enemy, mistake
  Berta Weiss走进咖啡馆时，邻桌的声音低了一拍。没有人当面说什么，但每个人都知道该说什么。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.03_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006027_

- **1931.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Berta Weiss把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person-helene-winter_

- **1931.07** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/turned-back` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, reform
  Grete Stein 2把材料推回来，说出生纸缺了教区印章。Berta Weiss解释自己是从乡下赶来的，对方只指了指墙上的告示；排在后头的Helene Winter递来半块面包，两个人坐在候车室里等下一班回去的车，谁也没有先说话。
  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day); mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”_

  又一年过去，Berta Weiss仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Berta Weiss学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 20 -> 27; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 2 -> 3; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.009999999999999995; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 40 -> 35; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Berta Weiss报名给巷子里送水，每天挑两桶，桶沿要用石炭酸擦过。第五天，防疫队抬走第三个人；Berta Weiss回家把手泡在盐水里，指甲缝还是黑的，Berta Stein在名单上添了她的名字。
  _effects: cash 4 (院子门口的石灰线); condition; flag +carried_water_inside_cholera_cordon; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; knowledge “the water barrels are disinfected at the rim, not at the tap”_

- **1932.02** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Berta Weiss说这个月先欠着，等发工钱补上。Alois Berg在名字旁画了个小圈，写明欠两周，叮嘱别拖过月底，否则要重新排进新会员的等待期。她走时把楼道的门带严，说冷风灌进来煤烧得快。Berta Weiss回头数了数抽屉里的零钱，还差三个赫勒。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Berta Weiss没有立刻答话，先去敲Franz Moser的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Franz Moser提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Berta Weiss把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

  Berta Weiss被叫到学校，老师说Gustav Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Berta Weiss没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 59 -> 54; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 68 -> 73; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  有人替Berta Weiss挡了一次采访，随后在走廊里提起自己儿子的学徒期满。Berta Weiss知道这不是闲聊；当天晚上，那张写着名字的纸条被压在糖罐下面，像一枚没有付清的硬币。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.leverage 43 -> 55; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 63 -> 59; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 50 -> 55; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  又过了一个冬天，Berta Weiss能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Berta Weiss画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。
