  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Maria Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Berta Weiss把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 51 -> 54; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/resigned` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Maria Stein在门口堵了三天，第四天把Berta Weiss的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Berta Weiss把办公室的钥匙交给Elisabeth Hofer 2，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 档案助理 @org-allgemeines-krankenhaus; reputation -0.08; mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +resigned_over_scandal; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1933.01** `state_reaction:sm18-missing-object/a-rumor-hardens` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Berta Weiss在院子里听见两个人在说“那家的东西”，话头在看见Berta Weiss时停住。晚上，Berta Weiss把首饰盒从五斗柜挪到床边，又觉得这样更像在防谁。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 5 -> 15; reputation -0.020000000000000004_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007133_

- **1933.05** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/favor-refused` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Helene Huber把一篮鸡蛋放在Berta Weiss家门口，说只要一个不用打卡的位子。Berta Weiss把鸡蛋拎回对方手里，说编制表不是自家的抽屉。两个人站在楼梯间，谁也没有再提这件事；第二天见面，还是点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; reputation 0.03_

- **1933.09** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Berta Weiss还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 0 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +mb17_border_slow_letters_

- **1933.11** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/fined` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Anton Berg 2在第三份合同上停住了：同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是Berta Weiss。罚款单当场开出，金额不算大，但足够让下一季度的办公用品全部改成最便宜的牌子。Berta Weiss没有争辩，只在收据背面写了日期。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.05; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:443; flag +fined_after_audit; knowledge “同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是我的。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1934.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/balanced` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  Berta Weiss用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Helene Huber发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +balanced_the_books; knowledge “停掉两份简报、合并零碎支出后，年底还剩一笔够换档案柜的钱。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.support 45 -> 50; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 40 -> 36; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Berta Weiss把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 3; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 55 -> 59; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.04** `r2-law.life-insurance::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pledged-policy` tags: finance, insurance, health, premium, family, vienna, urban, crisis
  Berta Weiss把保单借给Josefine Hofer做借款抵押，Alois Hofer在背书栏盖章时说，死了才赔的钱，活着不值几个。Josefine Hofer拿钱去盘了一间小铺，Berta Weiss每月多收一点租金。Otto Kral来复诊时，说这买卖比保险划算。
  _effects: cash 9 (每周二十赫勒的死亡); flag +pledged_life_policy; relation colleague with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the policy can be pledged for a loan at half its face value”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1934.05** `ml18-luck.wrong-horse::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/the-horse-is-disqualified` tags: gambling, luck, money, horse_race, absurd, windfall, vienna, urban
  马冲过终点，Berta Weiss的票已经举到半空；广播里却说骑师过磅轻了半公斤，成绩作废。Josef Bauer把票收回去，在背面盖了一个“无效”的章，又给Berta Weiss倒了一杯李子酒。Josefine Hofer说至少酒是免费的；Berta Weiss喝了一口，觉得比输钱还难受。
  _effects: cash -5 (the losing stake on a disqualified horse); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:484; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; flag +horse_disqualified; knowledge “那匹马第一个冲线，但骑师过磅轻了半公斤。”; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  名单上添的是Elisabeth Bauer，一个连公文格式都分不清的远房亲戚。Elisabeth Berg把考勤表放在桌上，没有问原因；Berta Weiss也没有解释，只在签字栏里写得比平时慢。第一个月，Elisabeth Bauer迟到了七次，工资照发。

  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Berta Weiss把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Berta Weiss来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 5 -> 6; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Berta Weiss想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。

- **1935.05** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/the-number-wins-without-them` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  Berta Weiss用了十几年同一组号码——父亲当年送奶路线的门牌号。这个月房租涨了，Berta Weiss少买了一张；开奖那天，那组号码中了头奖。Josefine Hofer把报纸摊在桌上，Berta Weiss看了很久，最后去厨房把水壶坐上，水开了才发现自己没有拿杯子。
  _effects: cash -2 (the drink and the extra trip to the newsagent); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:530; relation partner with person-josefine-hofer; flag +missed_the_numbers; knowledge “父亲送奶路线的门牌号中了头奖，而这一期我没有买。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.05** `state_reaction:ml18-reversal/a-second-chance-arrives` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  一封没有署名的信里夹着一张彩票，号码是Berta Weiss上个月随口说过的。信纸是廉价的学生用纸，字迹却写得很慢。Berta Weiss把彩票翻过来，背面只写着一句：这次别再押上房租。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -20 -> -10; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 30 -> 25; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +reversal_second_chance_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008299_

- **1935.06** `r3-war.refugee-train::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/blocked-car-door` tags: war, refugee, train, station, migration, vienna, urban, crisis
  Otto Kral说军人优先，把Gustav Stein的箱子踢到一旁。Berta Weiss把箱子重新拎起来，挡在车门前，说“她男人在前线”。Elisabeth Berg看了看Gustav Stein怀里的孩子，把哨子含在嘴里没吹；火车动了，Otto Kral在后面记下车厢号。
  _effects: flag +blocked_car_door_for_refugee_family; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-75; knowledge “the stationmaster recorded the carriage number”_

- **1935.08** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Berta Weiss和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Berta Weiss把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000002; mood -0.020000000000000018; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:531; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1935.08** `state_reaction:ml18-reversal/the-story-gets-told` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  咖啡馆里有人把Berta Weiss赔钱的事讲成了三段，最后一段里Berta Weiss押上了房子、妻子的首饰和一条不存在的船。Berta Weiss坐在角落听完，没有纠正；纠正只会让故事更长。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 25 -> 29; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +reversal_story_public_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008301_

- **1936.01** `sm18-object.tram-lost::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/advertises-in-the-paper` tags: object, loss, child, tram, family, everyday, vienna, urban
  Berta Weiss在《新维也纳日报》的失物栏里登了三行小字，花了四十个铜板。第二天，一个卖旧货的人说在电车终点站见过那样的铜牌。Katharina Lechner放学后跟着Berta Weiss去找，一路上把书包带绕在手指上，绕得发白。
  _effects: cash -1 (在报纸上登失物启事); state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:529; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001758:child; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag -something_is_missing; flag +something_is_missing_

- **1936.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Berta Weiss还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 6 -> 8; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008297_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Berta Weiss把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 8 -> 9; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 69 -> 73_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Berta Weiss把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Berta Weiss来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 8 -> 9; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:ml18-reversal/reversal-half-year/another-turn-of-the-screw` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  补洞的钱又从另一个洞漏出去：煤价涨了，房东换了锁，那个说好下月还钱的人搬走了。Berta Weiss在账本上把同一笔支出记了两次，第二次是因为不相信第一次。夜里听见楼梯响，就以为是债主。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -10 -> -18; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 29 -> 37; cash -2 (coal, a new lock and a missing debtor); mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:office-under-siege/pressure-year/held-on` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  质询会开了两个小时，Berta Weiss把每一笔签字都念了一遍。没有人被说服，但也没有人再追问；散会时，支持Berta Weiss的人比进门时多了两个。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 65; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 26; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Berta Weiss走到Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1936.02** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Otto Weiss排在前面，回头替Berta Weiss看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Berta Weiss用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.10** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.caretaker-notice` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Berta Weiss去找看门人，说院子里四面墙，声音绕不出去。第二天楼梯间的木板上贴了一张纸：晚上十点后请勿放留声机，鞋匠铺也请收锤。纸条没有落款，只有房东事务所的戳。Maria Bauer 2路过时看了很久，把留声机的盖子合上了。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Berta Weiss还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。

- **1937.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Berta Weiss把Gustav Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Gustav Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Berta Weiss听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 65 -> 73; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 70 -> 67; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `state_expire:ml18-sudden-fortune` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery

  _effects: state_remove ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:495_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Berta Weiss把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Berta Weiss忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 5 -> 13; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 25 -> 23; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: state_remove ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:530; flag -fortune_reversed_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:office-under-siege/pressure-year/new-allegation` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  第二天的报纸又登了一封匿名信，说Berta Weiss把一份合同给了亲戚。信里没有证据，但足够让两个原本答应到场的人临时出差。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 26 -> 36; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 65 -> 59; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 1 -> 2; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Berta Weiss在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Berta Weiss拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Helene Bauer找到Berta Weiss时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.05** `r2-law.loan-shark::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signed-weekly-interest` tags: finance, debt, usury, poverty, threat, vienna, urban, crisis
  Berta Weiss在Helene Berg的账本上按了手印，借十克朗，周息五分。Helene Berg把钱数在柜台上，又抽回一枚，说那是“跑腿费”。Franz Kral在门口看着，等Helene Berg进屋才低声说，这条街上没人还得清他的第二笔。
  _effects: cash 9 (五分利，按周算); condition; flag +took_shark_loan; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; knowledge “the lender charges five percent weekly plus a collection fee”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1937.11** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Berta Weiss把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Helene Bauer放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Josef Bauer在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Mitzi Moser 2在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Berta Weiss排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/motion-carries` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  投票结果是十九比十七。Berta Weiss把印章、钥匙和一本写满批注的账本放进纸箱，最后一圈走廊走得比平时慢。Maria Bauer 2在门口等着接手，手里已经拿着新的编制表；表上第一个被划掉的名字是Berta Weiss的秘书。
  _effects: position_end 顾问 @org-allgemeines-krankenhaus; reputation -0.05; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; flag +removed_by_vote; knowledge “新编制表第一个划掉的是我的秘书。”_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/letter-returned` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  汇款单被退了回来，理由是收件地址“查无此人”。Berta Weiss拿着那张盖了退件章的纸去问邮局，又去问同乡会；没有人知道那家人搬去了哪里，只有邮局墙上的钟还在走，秒针每一下都像在提醒钱没有到。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 73 -> 79; flag +mb17_remittance_letter_returned; knowledge “退件章意味着钱没有到，也意味着那个地址已经失效。”_

- **1938.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Berta Weiss为圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsame出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-berta-weiss-evt:world-vienna-1912:005481-1931 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015_

- **1938.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Berta Weiss在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Otto Bauer从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000167:child_

- **1938.10** `ad20-landlord-visit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/the-window-stays-broken` tags: housing, rent, landlord, everyday, repair, public, vienna, urban
  Alois Berg说修窗不在租约里，若嫌冷可以搬走。Berta Weiss问押金怎么算，Alois Berg把钥匙在手里转了一圈，说“到时候再说”。那天夜里风从缝里进来，Berta Weiss把椅子搬到炉边睡，第二天在门上贴了一张自己写的修窗单。
  _effects: cash -1 (coal for a room with a broken window); flag +eviction_threatened_

- **1938.12** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Berta Weiss发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Alois Hofer问是谁动的。Alois Hofer说是Otto Berg嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1939.01** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/sign-the-receipt` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  两名工人把Otto Berg家的钢琴抬上卡车，琴腿在门框上磕掉一块漆。Josef Winter 2把一张纸塞给Berta Weiss，说只要签“屋内无人认领”，就不必再搬第二趟。Berta Weiss签了名，看见Otto Berg站在对面窗帘后，手一直按在玻璃上。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:649; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001469:child; knowledge “签字比争辩更快，也比争辩更难忘。”; flag +signed_abandoned_piano_receipt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010164_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Berta Weiss把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Berta Weiss忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 21 -> 29; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 21 -> 19_

- **1939.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Berta Weiss去了Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg，坐在后排听完了关于普拉特动物园夜班队在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg经过数的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-zoo:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13-evt:world-vienna-1912:002065-1929 as participant; knowledge “我参加了普拉特动物园夜班队在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg经过数年的招募、。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.04** `p9-gap-elderhood-daily.church-fair/p9-gap-elderhood-daily.church-fair.leftover-clothes` tags: elderhood, daily, pension, housing, care, church, charity, social
  义卖散场时剩下一箱旧衣，Berta Weiss在箱里挑了两件能改的，一件灰呢外套、一条厚裙，叠好放进自己的布袋。剩下的她跟Helene Bauer一起抬去救济厨房，管事的说这些能拆了给小孩做夹衣；回来路上布袋压着肩，Berta Weiss在教堂台阶上歇了一脚。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1939.05** `vienna-elder-care.pharmacy-debt/ask-neighbor-for-loan` tags: elderhood, health, family, social
  Berta Weiss在走廊里拦住Mitzi Stein 2，说下周养老金到账就还。Mitzi Stein 2数出三枚克朗，说了句“别拖太久”。
  _effects: cash 3 (向邻居借的药费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; health 0.03 (及时取药)_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Berta Weiss把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 11; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 79 -> 76; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.01** `state_expire:sm18-missing-object` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Berta Weiss的「一件说不清下落的东西」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:529; flag -something_is_missing_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Berta Weiss又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 85 -> 87_

- **1940.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Berta Weiss想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Berta Weiss还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 11 -> 12_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011299_

- **1940.02** `kernel-health.chronic-pain::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pain-managed-at-home` tags: health, pain, elderhood, morphine, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  Berta Weiss把楼梯改成一步一步上，买菜只走平路，疼得厉害就坐下来揉。Helene Kral替Berta Weiss把煤筐拎上楼，换来一碗汤和一张旧报纸；两个人谁也没提医院。邻居把空筐靠在门边，顺手把楼梯上的冰敲掉。
  _effects: cash -4 (a cane, a hot-water bottle and tram fares to the market); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; knowledge “膝盖疼的时候走平路、分几步上楼、用热水袋，比去医院的排队更管用。”_

- **1940.03** `p21-elder-testimony::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-family-hears-a-different-death` tags: elderhood, testimony, death, family_secret, history, court, public, suspicious_death
  Mitzi Lechner在证词里读到另一段：卡尔·魏斯不是死于街头冲突，而是在一间公寓里被注射了镇静剂。Berta Weiss没有否认，只说“那时候说出来，会多死两个人”；Elisabeth Berg把钢笔放下，Berta Stein在门口说“你终于肯说了”。Mitzi Lechner把纸翻过去，背面还有一行被划掉的名字。
  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; state_add p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:740; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:741; flag +family_heard_other_death; knowledge “卡尔·魏斯不是死于街头冲突，而是在公寓里被注射了镇静剂。”; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +scandal_web_active_

- **1940.03** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Berta Weiss当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 76 -> 86; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011385_

- **1940.03** `state_reaction:hs18-street-witness/retold-in-public` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Berta Weiss在报上或会上听见自己的话被转述。句子大致没错，只是“我看见”被改成了“大家都看见”；回家的路上，有陌生人朝Berta Weiss点头。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.retellings 0 -> 2; state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 87 -> 89; reputation 0.020000000000000004_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011385_

  Berta Weiss在结冰的院子台阶上滑倒，篮子里的面包滚进雪里，右手腕肿了起来。
  _effects: cash -4 (请接骨师包扎); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; food_

- **1940.10** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Berta Weiss没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 6 -> 7; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 86 -> 84; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-berta-weiss-evt:world-vienna-1912:005481-1931 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015_

- **1941.04** `pc20-reversal.quiet-exoneration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/take-the-file` tags: elderhood, public_reversal, quiet_exoneration, reputation, family, archive, late_life, vienna
  Berta Weiss没有接受也没有拒绝，只把整份档案借出来，带回家和Josefine Hofer一起读。两个人在厨房桌上按年份排到半夜，最后把其中一页抄下来寄给了一个还在等消息的旧同事；更正有没有登报，反而成了次要的事。
  _effects: state_remove pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:779; state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:780; relation partner with person-josefine-hofer; cash -2 (copying and posting the file); flag +took_the_file_home; knowledge “档案按年份排到半夜，其中一页被抄下来寄给了旧同事。”; flag -public_reversal_active; flag +second_chance_open_

  Berta Weiss自己提着一小桶水擦碑，把字缝里的青苔抠掉，换上一支新蜡烛，又摆了一小把丁香。Helene Bauer在门口等着，说守墓人那边还留着空位。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (蜡烛和一小把丁香); mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  Berta Weiss没有改口，把上一次的说法一字一句重复完。Josef Winter 2问他是否确定，Berta Weiss说记得的不是纸上的日期，是门开后屋里那股煤气味；Berta Stein在旁听席上抬起头，第一次没有躲开他的目光。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:779; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 40 -> 50; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 49 -> 57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102; flag +confirmed_testimony; knowledge “记得的不是纸上的日期，是门开后屋里那股煤气味。”; flag +public_reversal_active_

  Berta Weiss没有改口，把上一次的说法一字一句重复完。Helene Huber问他是否确定，Berta Weiss说记得的不是纸上的日期，是门开后屋里那股煤气味；Helene Kral在旁听席上抬起头，第一次没有躲开他的目光。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:805; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 48 -> 58; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 59 -> 67; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; knowledge “记得的不是纸上的日期，是门开后屋里那股煤气味。”; flag +public_reversal_active_

- **1943.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Berta Weiss还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012657_

  一个不算亲近的人替Berta Weiss说了句话：不是保证Berta Weiss已经变好，只说那天Berta Weiss确实在场、确实没有躲。话传回来时，Berta Weiss正在补一只旧口袋，针脚第一次没有歪。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 46 -> 53; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 49 -> 54; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012657_

- **1943.01** `state_reaction:pc20-second-chance/public-doubt` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  旧事被人在街上重新提起，Berta Weiss的名字和另一件事绑在一起。有人替Berta Weiss辩解，也有人只笑了一声；Berta Weiss回家后把当天的收据一张张摊开，像在给自己准备证词。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 35 -> 43; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 53 -> 49_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Berta Weiss又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 83 -> 85_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/rehearsed` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  又过了半年，Berta Weiss把证词在心里走了一遍。顺序还在，但每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人补上的颜色；Berta Weiss把最重要的日期写在火柴盒背面，睡前又看了一次。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 45 -> 48; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 58 -> 56; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 67 -> 69; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:p21-scandal-web/the-web-tightens/held` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  又过了一年，那几张纸仍锁在Berta Weiss的抽屉里。街上的问话没有变多，也没有变少；Berta Weiss在咖啡馆里数了数还能信任的人，发现名单比去年短，但每一个名字都还站得住。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 54 -> 51; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.loyalty 43 -> 47; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 34 -> 31; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Berta Weiss把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Berta Weiss这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 49 -> 55; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 43 -> 38; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 54 -> 58; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Berta Weiss把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Berta Weiss来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1943.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1943.04** `vienna-elder-care.pharmacy-debt/ask-neighbor-for-loan` tags: elderhood, health, family, social
  Berta Weiss向Mitzi开口借三克朗垫付药费，邻居迟疑后从围裙口袋里摸出硬币。
  _effects: cash 3 (向邻居借的药费); health 0.03 (及时取药)_

  Katharina Lechner 2说版面已经排好，改一个字要挪三栏。Berta Weiss没有争，只把报纸带回家，用铅笔在照片下面画了一道横线；Mitzi问那是什么意思，Berta Weiss说意思是这张照片还没有说完。

  一个旧同事在公开场合替Berta Weiss说了话，没有把Berta Weiss说成无辜，只把当年那份名单的次序讲清楚。第二天，另一个人在面包店门口拦住Berta Weiss，说自己一直觉得哪里不对。

  Mitzi在证词里读到另一段：卡尔·魏斯不是死于街头冲突，而是在一间公寓里被注射了镇静剂。Berta Weiss没有否认，只说“那时候说出来，会多死两个人”；Josef Winter 2把钢笔放下，Luise Kral 3在门口说“你终于肯说了”。Mitzi把纸翻过去，背面还有一行被划掉的名字。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; state_add p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:837; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:838; knowledge “卡尔·魏斯不是死于街头冲突，而是在公寓里被注射了镇静剂。”; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126; flag -scandal_web_active; flag +scandal_web_active_

  法院的人又来了，这次带着上一回的笔录。Berta Weiss看见自己的句子被打印成另一种语气，旁边还留着书记员画的一个问号；对方说只改一处，改完就可以回家。

  _effects: cash -6 (丧葬会的铜板); reputation 0.1; flag +paid_for_neighbor_funeral_

- **1945.12** `p21-elder-testimony::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-name-is-restored` tags: elderhood, testimony, death, family_secret, history, court, public, suspicious_death
  Elisabeth Hofer 2把那份旧证词从档案袋里抽出来，放在窗边的桌上。Berta Weiss用蓝铅笔改了三处，把死者的名字从“无名”改回“卡尔·魏斯”；Mitzi在旁边把每一页按顺序排好，Berta Winter 2站在门口，手里的帽子一直没有戴上。签完字时，窗外正好有人敲响下午四点的钟。
  _effects: state_remove eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:885; state_add p21-fortune-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:934; relation kin with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.05; knowledge “蓝铅笔改了三处，死者的名字从“无名”改回“卡尔·魏斯”。”; cash 2 (the court refunded part of the costs); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125; flag -late_testimony_active; flag -fortune_reversal_active; flag +fortune_reversal_active_

- **1946.01** `pc20-testimony.new-caption::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/no-correction` tags: elderhood, testimony, newspaper, photograph, public_reversal, memory, family, vienna
  Anna Winter 4说版面已经排好，改一个字要挪三栏。Berta Weiss没有争，只把报纸带回家，用铅笔在照片下面画了一道横线；Mitzi问那是什么意思，Berta Weiss说意思是这张照片还没有说完。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:932; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; knowledge “照片下面被画了一道铅笔横线，意思是这张照片还没有说完。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; flag -public_reversal_active; flag +public_reversal_active_

  法院的人又来了，这次带着上一回的笔录。Berta Weiss看见自己的句子被打印成另一种语气，旁边还留着书记员画的一个问号；对方说只改一处，改完就可以回家。

  一个年轻人抓起铜烛台，把价签上的“一克朗五十”念成一克朗五赫勒，Berta Weiss隔着桌子纠正，两人先愣了愣，随后都笑出声。年轻人照价付了钱，说回去给母亲，Luise Kral在旁把零钱数了两遍才递过去。
  _effects: cash 0.5 (义卖上多收的零钱归入钱箱); mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1950.12** `p21-elder-testimony::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/the-family-hears-a-different-death` tags: elderhood, testimony, death, family_secret, history, court, public, suspicious_death
  Elisabeth Lechner在证词里读到另一段：卡尔·魏斯不是死于街头冲突，而是在一间公寓里被注射了镇静剂。Berta Weiss没有否认，只说“那时候说出来，会多死两个人”；Otto Kral把钢笔放下，Otto Lechner 2在门口说“你终于肯说了”。Elisabeth Lechner把纸翻过去，背面还有一行被划掉的名字。
  _effects: relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001502:child; state_add p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:1176; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:1177; knowledge “卡尔·魏斯不是死于街头冲突，而是在公寓里被注射了镇静剂。”; reputation -0.019999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133; flag +scandal_web_active_

  法院的人又来了，这次带着上一回的笔录。Berta Weiss看见自己的句子被打印成另一种语气，旁边还留着书记员画的一个问号；对方说只改一处，改完就可以回家。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 53 -> 61; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.danger 25 -> 30_

  又过了半年，Berta Weiss把证词在心里走了一遍。顺序还在，但每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人补上的颜色；Berta Weiss把最重要的日期写在火柴盒背面，睡前又看了一次。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 50 -> 53; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 40 -> 38; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 61 -> 63; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_parameter p21-scandal-web.exposure 60 -> 68; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.leverage 50 -> 44; state_parameter p21-scandal-web.danger 40 -> 45; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

  _effects: cash -6 (镜片上的第一道裂纹); condition; flag +bought_new_eyeglasses; skill; knowledge “the fourth lens sharpened the letters on the wall”_

- **1952.02** `eh19-bridge.question-arrives::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/fill-the-form` tags: elderhood, testimony, history, memory, bureaucracy, ordinary, vienna, urban
  区里寄来一张问话单，只有一页，问的是一九三四年住过这条街的人。Berta Weiss把老花镜从抽屉里找出来，先写自己的名字，再写“记得不清楚”；写到第三个问题，笔尖在纸上停住，窗外有人在收晾衣绳。
  _effects: state_add eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:1280; knowledge “区里在核对一九三四年住过这条街的人。”; flag +late_testimony_questionnaire; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +late_testimony_active_

  Berta Weiss把照片拿到照相馆，老师傅认出背面的编号，说底片还在，只是那一年拍合影的人家后来搬去了美国。Franz Hofer问要不要写信去问；Berta Weiss把照片放回相册，说先等等。
  _effects: knowledge “旧照片的底片还在照相馆，拍合影的人家后来搬去了美国”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; cash -1 (照相馆查底片的费用); mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +asked_the_photographer_about_the_double_

  Helene Huber把那份旧证词从档案袋里抽出来，放在窗边的桌上。Berta Weiss用蓝铅笔改了三处，把死者的名字从“无名”改回“卡尔·魏斯”；Otto Bauer在旁边把每一页按顺序排好，Alois Hofer 3站在门口，手里的帽子一直没有戴上。签完字时，窗外正好有人敲响下午四点的钟。

  Berta Weiss为圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsame出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。

- **1954.08** `em18-late-bloom.courting-family::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/move-in-anyway` tags: elderhood, late_life, love, family, inheritance, conflict, cohabitation, vienna
  Berta Weiss把朝北的房间腾出来，给Franz Berg放了一张床和一只旧五斗柜。Elisabeth Lechner知道后，把过年要送来的东西托邻居转交；Berta Weiss把东西收下，在厨房多摆了一副碗筷，谁也没有再提那副碗筷是给谁的。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 73 -> 63; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.publicness 30 -> 40; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 45 -> 53; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-169; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001502:child; cash -1 (a bed and a chest for the north room); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-169_

- **1954.10** `pc20-testimony.new-caption::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/no-correction` tags: elderhood, testimony, newspaper, photograph, public_reversal, memory, family, vienna
  Katharina Lechner 2说版面已经排好，改一个字要挪三栏。Berta Weiss没有争，只把报纸带回家，用铅笔在照片下面画了一道横线；Elisabeth Lechner问那是什么意思，Berta Weiss说意思是这张照片还没有说完。
  _effects: state_add pc20-public-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1380; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; knowledge “照片下面被画了一道铅笔横线，意思是这张照片还没有说完。”; flag -public_reversal_active; flag +public_reversal_active_

- **1954.10** `state_reaction:eh19-late-testimony/official-return` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  法院的人又来了，这次带着上一回的笔录。Berta Weiss看见自己的句子被打印成另一种语气，旁边还留着书记员画的一个问号；对方说只改一处，改完就可以回家。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 54 -> 62; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.danger 22 -> 27_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020762_

- **1954.12** `em18-late-bloom.legacy-walk::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-child-refuses` tags: elderhood, late_life, legacy, memory, family, child, neighborhood, vienna
  Otto Bauer说周末要加班，改天再去。Berta Weiss把准备好的路线图收回抽屉，图上用铅笔画了三个圈：旧居、作坊、学校；后来那张图被压在玻璃板下，边角慢慢卷起来。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 68 -> 73; knowledge “路线图上用铅笔画了旧居、作坊和学校三个圈。”_

- **1955.01** `em18-late-bloom.rent-increase::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/pays-the-increase` tags: elderhood, late_life, housing, rent, shop, money, risk, vienna
  房东把新租约摊在柜台上，说煤价涨了，房租每月多加一克朗。Berta Weiss把账本翻到最后一页，算了一遍修一把椅子的工钱，最后还是在租约上签了字；那天下午，Berta Weiss把修理价目也重新写了一张。
  _effects: cash -3 (the rent and the new price list); state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 60 -> 68; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 38 -> 35; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1955.01** `state_expire:p21-scandal-web` tags: p21-scandal-web, scandal_web, scandal, secret, blackmail, network, leverage, danger
  Berta Weiss的「丑闻蛛网」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove p21-scandal-web state:world-vienna-1912:1176; flag -scandal_web_active_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:p21-fortune-reversal/the-crowd-recounts/the-scaffold-shakes` tags: p21-fortune-reversal, fortune_reversal, fortune, reversal, inheritance, ruin, public, witnesses
  一笔该到的钱没有到。Berta Weiss在账房里坐到天黑，听见隔壁把算盘珠子拨得很快；回家时，门口已经有人等着，手里拿着一份折过三次的账单。
  _effects: state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.height 52 -> 44; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.fragility 58 -> 66; state_parameter p21-fortune-reversal.debt 15 -> 20; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:eh19-late-testimony/version-drift/rehearsed` tags: eh19-late-testimony, elderhood, testimony, memory, history, politics, legal, risk
  又过了半年，Berta Weiss把证词在心里走了一遍。顺序还在，但每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人补上的颜色；Berta Weiss把最重要的日期写在火柴盒背面，睡前又看了一次。
  _effects: state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.memory 50 -> 53; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.truth 28 -> 26; state_parameter eh19-late-testimony.pressure 62 -> 64; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:em18-late-bloom/late-life-year/takes-root` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  又一年过去，Berta Weiss把新生活的开销记在一本旧账簿背面：房租、药、车票，还有一件为公开场合买的衬衫。事情没有变轻，但Berta Weiss已经知道每周哪几天有力气，哪几天只适合坐在窗边。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 53 -> 57; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.stamina 35 -> 32; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.tether 63 -> 61; cash 1 (small receipts from the new work); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Berta Weiss把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Berta Weiss来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 7 -> 8; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:pc20-public-reversal/file-moves/the-file-disappears` tags: pc20-public-reversal, public_reversal, exoneration, accusation, testimony, proof, pressure, support
  Berta Weiss按约定时间去调卷，窗口的人说编号不对，又说管库的人病了。走廊尽头的门开着，里面只剩空架子和一层灰；Berta Weiss把手伸进去，摸到的是一截断掉的麻绳。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 40 -> 32; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.danger 45 -> 51; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.pressure 60 -> 65; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1955.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Berta Weiss想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1955.10** `vienna-elder-bereavement.empty-room/empty-room.lodger` tags: elderhood, bereavement, family, health, memory, housing
  泥瓦匠姓霍夫曼，靴子上有干了的灰浆。Berta Weiss把旧箱子挪到墙角，铺上一条干净床单，又把Katharina Lechner 2送来的那罐果酱摆在桌上，说早上有面包。
  _effects: cash 10 (空床租给年轻泥瓦匠的月钱); mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; flag +takes_lodger_

- **1955.11** `vienna-elder-care.winter-stairs/fall-on-stairs` tags: elderhood, health, family, social
  Anton Lechner听见闷响从窗户探头，看见Berta Weiss坐在冰上按着手腕。面包被踩进泥雪，Anton Lechner跑下来捡起篮子。
  _effects: cash -4 (请接骨师包扎); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-160; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-160_

- **1956.01** `p21-elder-testimony::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/the-name-is-restored` tags: elderhood, testimony, death, family_secret, history, court, public, suspicious_death
  Elisabeth Berg把那份旧证词从档案袋里抽出来，放在窗边的桌上。Berta Weiss用蓝铅笔改了三处，把死者的名字从“无名”改回“卡尔·魏斯”；Otto Bauer在旁边把每一页按顺序排好，Grete Stein站在门口，手里的帽子一直没有戴上。签完字时，窗外正好有人敲响下午四点的钟。
  _effects: state_remove eh19-late-testimony state:world-vienna-1912:1280; state_add p21-fortune-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:1414; reputation 0.05; knowledge “蓝铅笔改了三处，死者的名字从“无名”改回“卡尔·魏斯”。”; cash 2 (the court refunded part of the costs); mood 0.030000000000000027; flag -late_testimony_active_

- **1956.01** `state_reaction:em18-late-bloom/public-eyes` tags: em18-late-bloom, elderhood, late_life, reinvention, love, work, legacy, risk
  有人在报纸的社交栏里看见Berta Weiss的名字，连旧住址都写对了。第二天，杂货店老板多问了一句，Berta Weiss笑着把话岔开；回家后，Berta Weiss把窗帘拉上，又打开。
  _effects: state_parameter em18-late-bloom.publicness 40 -> 46; reputation 0.02; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021507_

- **1956.01** `state_reaction:pc20-public-reversal/someone-defends` tags: pc20-public-reversal, public_reversal, exoneration, accusation, testimony, proof, pressure, support
  一个旧同事在公开场合替Berta Weiss说了话，没有把Berta Weiss说成无辜，只把当年那份名单的次序讲清楚。第二天，另一个人在面包店门口拦住Berta Weiss，说自己一直觉得哪里不对。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.support 30 -> 37; state_parameter pc20-public-reversal.proof 32 -> 36; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  入冬后，Berta Weiss在楼梯上停了三次。医生把“减少劳累”写在纸条上，Berta Weiss把它折好，放进装车票的口袋；第二天仍去开门，只是把营业时间改短了一小时。

- **1956.02** `em18-late-bloom.radio-hour::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refuses-the-microphone` tags: elderhood, late_life, fame, radio, comedy, mistaken_identity, absurd, vienna
  Berta Weiss在录音间门口站了很久，最后说嗓子不行，转身下了楼。编导追出来，把录音费塞进Berta Weiss的口袋；当天晚上，电台还是播了那段走廊里的脚步声和一句“算了”。
  _effects: cash 1 (the broadcast fee was pressed into a pocket); reputation 0.01; knowledge “电台没有经过同意，就播出了走廊里的脚步声。”_

- **1956.05** `vienna-life-old-age/chronic-illness` tags: elderhood, pension, health
  Berta Weiss的腿在阴天肿起来，去药房买了药膏。医生说这是年轻时站得太久留下的毛病，除了休息没有别的办法。
  _effects: cash -5 (medicine); condition_

- **1956.10** `em18-late-bloom.rent-increase::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/moves-to-the-courtyard` tags: elderhood, late_life, housing, rent, shop, money, risk, vienna
  Berta Weiss把工具装进两只木箱，搬到后院一间更小的棚屋。新地方没有临街的窗，但房租少了一半；Grete Bauer来帮忙，说这里冬天会冷，Berta Weiss说先把招牌挂上，冷了再想办法。
  _effects: cash -2 (the move and the new sign); state_parameter em18-late-bloom.publicness 46 -> 36; state_parameter em18-late-bloom.nerve 57 -> 62; flag +moved_workshop; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

  她挑出那张厂门口的合影，用布擦了擦玻璃框的角，第二天上午走到楼下旧同事的门口。两人对着照片点了半天名字，谁站哪一排、谁后来去了哪儿，说到一半都笑了，回来时她在楼梯口多站了一会儿。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; reputation 0.02_
